Catalog number : M00340
Uniform title: durgārcanaṣṛti
Author : lakṣmīnārāyaṇa goswāmī
Description:
This is modern manual for the worship of the goddess Durgā. It was written by Lakṣmīnārāyaṇa Goswāmī a Bengali brahmin who was professsor of the Sanskrit College of the Vidyādharmavarddhinī Pāṭhaśālā in Agra where he taught Yajurveda. He was also an expert in ritual . The manual was published in 1952 with a commentary (bhāṣātikā) by his son Ghanaśyāma Gosvaāmī.
The rite is extensively described with all the details neccesary to do it competely just by following the directions given in the book. The full rite which is quite extensive is primarily performed during the period of Navaraatri in combination, as usual, with the recitation of the Durgāsāsaptaśati drawn from the Mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇa. The layout is very clear and systematic allowing anyone with a basic knowledge of ritual performance and Sanskrit to do the rites. It has therefore been a very popular work throughout North India.
Notes:
Data entered by the staff of Muktabodha under the direction of Mark S. G. Dyczkowski.

Revision 0: February 21, 2016
Publisher : Ghanaśyāma Gosvāmī
Publication year : 1952
Publication country : India

śrī gaṇeśāya namaḥ

atha durgā pūjane

kalaśa sthāpana vidhiḥ

jetuṃ yastripuraṃ hareṇa hariṇā vyājādvaliṃ vadhnatā |
sraṣṭuṃ vādi-bhavodbhavena bhuvanaṃ śeṣeṇa dhaturdharāma ||

pārvatyā mahiṣāsura pramathane siddhādhipaiḥ siddhaye |
dhyātaḥ pañcaśareṇa viśva jitaye pāyātsa nāgānanaḥ ||

tatra pratipadi pūrvāhne puṣpatailādinā kṛtamaṅgalasnānaḥ 
[rudrayāmale || snānaṃ māṃgalikaṃ kṛtvā tato devīṃ prapūjayet | 
śubhābhirmṛttikābhiśca pūrve kṛtvā tu vedikām || 1 ||] nityakriyāṃ 
kṛtvā navevāsasī paridhāya candanamṛgamadakuṁkumaiḥ 
sarvāṅgamanulipya tripuṇḍraṃ ūrdhvapuṇḍraṃ vā kṛtvā 
[devatāpūjane prācī madhye pūjaka pūjyayoḥ || 

āsana upaviśeddevi ! vaddhvā vīrāsanādikam | 
upaviśyatatomantrī dravyāṇisthāpayetpuraḥ || 

gandhapuṣpākṣatādīṃśca dakṣedīpāṃśca sarvataḥ |
naivedyaṃ dakṣiṇe vāme purato vā na pṛṣṭhataḥ ||

ghṛtaṃ dīpaṃ dakṣiṇe tu tailadīpantu vāmataḥ |
vāmatastu tathā dhūpamagrevā natu dakṣiṇe ||

nivedayetpurobhāge gaṃdha puṣpañca bhūṣaṇam |
sarvaṃ svadakṣiṇe sthāpyaṃ vāmecārghyaṃ niveśayet ||

sthāpeyccarvyacoṣyādinaivedyādīnisaṃnidha | 
karayoḥ kṣālanārthāya pṛṣṭhe pātraṃ vinirdiśet ||

svasya śaktyanurūep'n sarvaṃ saṃpādyayatnataḥ |
pūjādravyāṇi saṃprokṣya mūlamaṃtreṇa sādhakaḥ ||

darśayeddhenu mudrāṃ ca dravya śuddhiritīritā |
naivedyādikaṃ ca yattu puṣpagandhādikañca yat ||

sarvamācchāditaṃ kāryaṃ yāvadāvāhayetparām || 

(iṣṭadevatāṃ)

rākṣasāḥ prati gṛhṇantinirācchādanakaṃyataḥ ||






(1) pūjāgṛhasya īśānadiśi pūjāsthānaṃ kalpayitvā 
gomayopaliptāyāṃ dharāyāṃ vindu-trikoṇa-ṣaṭkoṇa-ṣṭadala##-
vṛttaṃ caturasraṃ likhet || tasyopari tīrthamṛttikayā śubhamṛttikābhirvedī 
racayitvā yavān godhūmānvā vāpayet || tatsamīpe kāṣṭhapīṭhopari śvetavastraṃ 
prasārya gaṇeśādīnsthāpayitvā pūjayet || paścāt kalaśaṃ saṃsthāpya durgāṃ 
pūjayitvā stuvīta || navamīdine sthāpitadevānāṃ uttara pūjanaṃ kṛtvā visarjayet ||

siddhāntaśekharokta yavāṃkura parīkṣā ||

yajamānābhivṛddhyarthaṃ maṃkurāṇiparīkṣayet |
samyagūrddhvaṃ prarūḍhāni komalāni sitāni ca ||

dhūmravarṇānyapūrvāṇi tathā tiryggatāni ca |
śyāmalāni ca kubjāni varjayedaśubhāni ca ||

yavāṃkura phalajñānaṃ

avṛṣṭikurute kṛṣṇaṃ dhūmrābhaṃ kalahaṃ tathā |
apūrṇaṃ jananāśaṃ ca durbhikṣaṃ śyāmalāṃkuraṃ ||

tiryaggatebhavedvyādhiḥ || kubje śatrubhayaṃ tathā ||

aśubhecāṃkure jāte śāntihomaṃ samācaret |
mūlamantreṇa juhuyādgurumūrti dhariaḥ saha ||

aghorāstreṇa cāstreṇa śataṃ vātha sahasrakam |

sarasvatepi

prarūḍhairaṃkuraiḥ karturnirdiśeccaśubhāśubhaṃ |
śyāmaiḥ kṛṣṇairaṃkurairarthahānistiryagrūḍhairvyādhirāṃdolitaistaiḥ || 
kubjairduḥkhaṃduḥṣprarūḍhairmṛtiṃ ca rogaṃbhugnaiḥ sthānadeśeṣṭa hāniḥ 
||

yavāṃkura ropaṇa niyamaḥ

dīkṣādivasātprāk saptabhirdinaiḥ | etena dīkṣādinamaṣṭagaṃ yathā bhavati tathā 
kartavyamityuktaṃ | vidhivadityanenanavabhiḥtribhiḥ sadyovettyuktaṃ || taduktaṃ 
siddhāntaśekhare || 

pratiṣṭhāyāṃ ca dīkṣāyāṃ sthāpanecotsave tathā | 
saṃprokṣaṇe ca śāntyarthaṃ vivāhe mauṃjibaṃdhane ||

sarvamaṃgalakāryeṣu kārayedaṃkurārpaṇam |
pratiṣṭhādivasātpūrvaṃ navamesaptame dine ||

paṃcame vā tṛtīye vāsadyocāṃkurārpaṇam |
puṇyāhaghoṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā brāhmaṇaiḥ sahadeśikaḥ ||

maṃgalāṃkurayajñe ca kuryāttatrivacā hani || saptamānnavamādvāpi prāgeva 
yajña karmaṇa iti ||

tantrāntarepi

utsaveṣu vividheṣvapi dīkṣāsthāpanādiṣu pavitravidhau ca | maṃgalāṃkura 
viśeṣaṇapūrvaṃ maṃgalaṃ bhavati karmakṛtaṃ tat || śastayogadivasāttu 
purastāt saptamehani śubhe navame vā || paṃcametha sudine sumuhūrte 
maṃgalāṃkura vidhi vidadhīteti || tatra pūrvedyu rupavāsaṃ kṛtvā 
svagṛhyoktavidhinā nāndīśrāddhaṃ kṛtvā aṃkurārpaṇamārabhet | taduktaṃ || 
gururviśuddhaḥ prāgeva śuddhāhāt prathamehani || saṃkalpyopoṣya 
kartavyamaṃkurāropaṇaṃśubham || kuryānnāṃdī mukhaṃ śrāddhaṃ pūrvedyuḥ 
svastivācanaṃ || svagṛhyoktaprakāreṇa tadetadvidadhītavai || iti || saṃhitāyāmapi || 
sarvatrābhyudayāśrāddhamaṃkurotpādanaṃ tathā || ādāveva 
karmaṇobhyudayātmanaḥ || iti 

havana krama sūtra ||

ādau gaṇeśvaraḥ pūjyaḥ oṃkāraṃ paṃcadhā tataḥ |
gaṇayāgo gaṇeśasya vāstu yogini pūjanam ||

mātaro mātṛpūjā ca vṛddhiḥ śrāddhamataḥ param |
ṛtvijāṃ varaṇaṃ rakṣāvidhivaccakuśaṃḍikā ||

utpattisthāpanaṃproktaṃkalaśastadanantaram |
pradhānāvāhaṃ pūjā grahādīnāṃ ca pūjanam ||

homaṃ ca balidānaṃ ca pūjāyāntadanantaram |
vedapāṭhograhāṃ stutvā dvijātīnāṃpratarpaṇam ||

pūrṇāhutyabhiṣekaṃ ca tatoyajñapradakṣiṇā |
visarjanaṃkramaḥśānterghanaśyāmenakīrtitā ||] pūrvābhimukho devīmukho 
vā samupaviśya sopagrahapāṇirācamya || oṃ mūlam bhūḥ ātmatattvāya 
namaḥ svāhā || 1 || oṃ mūlam bhuvaḥ vidyātattvāya namaḥ svāhā || 2 || oṃ 
mūlam svaḥ śivatattvāya namaḥ svāhā || 3 || (mūlam cātra durge durge 
rakṣiṇi svāheti)

p. 3)

oṃ apavitraḥ pavitro vā sarvāvasthāṃ gato'pi vā | 
yaḥ smaret puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ sa bāhyābhyantaraḥ śuciḥ || 
oṃ pṛthvīti mantrasya merupṛṣṭha ṛṣiḥ sutalaṃchandaḥ kūrmodevatā 
āsanopaveśane viniyogaḥ || oṃ pṛthvī tvayā dhṛtā lokā devi tvaṃ 
viṣṇunādhṛtā | tvaṃ ca dhāraya māndevi pavitraṃkurucāsanam ||

p. 4)

svastivācana

oṃ śrīmanmahāgaṇādhipataye namaḥ || hariḥ oṃ gaṇānāntvā gaṇapatiguṃ 
havāmahe priyāṇāntvā priyapatiguṃ havāmahe nidhīnāntvā nidhipatiguṃ 
havāmahe vasomama || āhamajānigarbhadhamātvamajāsi gabhadham || 1 ||

svastina'-indro vṛddhaśravāḥ svastinaḥ pūpāviśvavedāḥ || 
svastinastārkṣyo'-ariṣṭanemiḥsvastinobṛhaspatirdadhātuḥ || 2 ||

payaḥ pṛthivyāṃ paya'-oṣadhī pupayo divyantarikṣe payodhāḥ || 
payasvatīḥ pradiśaḥ santumahyam || 3 ||

p. 5)

viṣṇorarāṭamasiviṣṇoḥ śnaptrestho viṣṇoḥ syūrasi viṣṇordhruvosi || 
vaiṣṇavamasi viṣṇavetvā || 4 ||

agnirdevatā vātodevatā sūryodevatā candramādevatā vasavodevatā 
rudrādevatā dityādevatā marutodevatā viśvedevā devatā 
bṛhaspatirdevatendrodevatā varuṇodevatā || 5 ||

dyauḥ śāntirantarikṣaguṃ śāntiḥ pṛthivī śānti rāpaḥśānti roṣadhayaḥ 
śāntiḥ || vanaspatayaḥ śāntirviśvedevāḥ śāntirbrahmaśāntiḥ sarvaguṃ 
śāntiḥ śāntireva śāntiḥ sāmāśāntiredhi || 6 ||

p. 6)

viśvānidevasaviturduritāni parāsuva || yadbhadrantanna-āsuva || 7 ||

etante devasavitaryajñaṃ prāhurbṛhaspataye brahmaṇe || tena 
yajñamavatena yajñapatintena māmava || 8 ||

manojūtirjuṣatāmājyasya bṛhaspatiryajñamimantano tvariṣṭaṃ yajñaguṃ 
samimandadhātu || viśvedevāsa'-ihamādayantāmoṃ 3 pratiṣṭha || 9 ||

eṣavai pratiṣṭhānāma yajño yatraitena yajñena yajante 
sarvamevapratiṣṭhitambhavati || 10 ||

oṃ śāntiḥ suśāntiḥ sarvāriṣṭa śāntirbhavatu || 

oṃ sumukhaścaikadantaśca kapilogajakarṇakaḥ |
lambodaraśca vikaṭo vighnanāśo vināyakaḥ || 11 ||

dhūmraketugaṇādhyakṣo bhālacandro gajānanaḥ |
dvādaśaitāni nāmāni yaḥ paṭhecchṛṇuyādapi || 12 ||

vidyārambhe vivāhe ca praveśe nirgame tathā |
saṃgrāme saṃkaṭe caiva vighnastasya na jāyate || 13 ||

śuklāmbaradharandevaṃ śaśivarṇaṃ caturbhujam |
prasannavadanaṃdhyāyetsarvavighnapaśāntaye || 14 ||

abhīpsitārthasiddhyarthaṃ pūjito yaḥ surāsuraiḥ |
sarvavighnaharastasmai gaṇādhipataye namaḥ || 15 ||

sarvamaṃgalamaṃgalye śivesarvārthasādhike |
śaraṇye tryaṃbake gauri nārāyaṇi namostu te || 16 ||

sarvadā sarvakāryeṣu nāsti teṣāmamaṃgalam |
yeṣāṃ hṛdisthobhagavānmaṃgalāyatano hariḥ || 17 ||

lābhasteṣāṃ jayasteṣāṃ kutasteṣāṃ parājayaḥ |
yeṣāmindīvaraśyāmo hṛdayasthojanārdanaḥ || 18 ||

vināyakaṃ guruṃ bhānuṃ brahmāviṣṇumaheśvarān |
sarasvatīṃ praṇamyādau śāntikāryārthasiddhaye || 19 ||

sarveṣvāraṃbhakāryeṣu trayastribhuvaneśvarāḥ |
devādiśantu naḥ siddhiṃ brahmeśānajanārdanāḥ || 20 ||

vakratuṇḍa mahākāya sūryakoṭisamaprabha |
avighnaṃ kuru me deva sarvakāryeṣu sarvadā || 21 ||

oṃ siddhi buddhi sahitāya śrīmangaṇādhipataye namaḥ || oṃ 
vāṇīhiraṇyagarbhābhyāṃ namaḥ || oṃ lakṣmīnārāyaṇābhyānnamaḥ || 

p. 7)

oṃ umāmaheśvarābhyānnamaḥ || oṃ śacīpurandarābhyānnamaḥ || oṃ 
mātāpitṛcaraṇakamalebhyo namaḥ || oṃ kuladevatābhyo namaḥ | oṃ 
iṣṭadevatābhyo namaḥ || oṃ grāmadevatābhyo namaḥ || oṃ sthānadevatābhyo 
namaḥ || oṃ vāstudevatābhyo namaḥ || oṃ sarvebhyodevebhyo namaḥ || oṃ 
sarvebhyobrāhmaṇebhyo namaḥ || oṃ sarvebhyo tīrthebhyo namaḥ || oṃ 
etatkarmapradhāna śrīdurgādevyai namaḥ || oṃ puṇyaṃpuṇyāhaṃ 
dīrghamāyurastu || vāme guṁ gurubhyo namaḥ dakṣiṇe bhaṁ bhadrakālyai 
namaḥ || upari gaṁ gaṇapataye namaḥ || hṛdi duṁ durgāyai namaḥ || 
pṛṣṭhe oṃ kṣaṃ kṣetrapālāya namaḥ || oṃ tīkṣṇadaṃṣṭra mahākāya 
kalpānta dahanopama || bhairavāya namastubhyamanujñāṃdātumarhasi ||

oṃ svastiśrīman mukundasaccidānandasyājñayāpravarta mānasyādya 
brahmaṇo dvitīye praharārddhe ekapañcāśattamevarṣe prathamamāse 
prathamapakṣe prathama divase ahno dvitīyeyāme tṛtīyemuhūrte 
rathantarādi dvātriṃśatkalpānāṃmadhye aṣṭame śrīśvetavārāhakalpe 
svāyaṃbhuvādi manvantarāṇāṃ madhye saptame vaivasvatamanvantare 
kṛtatretādvāparakalisaṃjñānāṃcaturyugānāṃmadhye vartamāne 
aṣṭāviṃśatitame kaliyuge tatprathamacaraṇe tathā 
pañcāśatkoṭiyojanavistīrṇabhūmaṃḍalāntargatasaptadvīpamadhyavartini 
jambūdvīpe tatrāpi navakhaṃḍānāṃmadhye navasahasrayojanavistīrṇe 
bharatakhaṃḍe tatrāpi paramapavitre bhāratavarṣe āryāvartāntargata 
brahmāvartaikadeśe kumarikākṣetre mathurāmaṇḍale [samīpavartī 
maṇḍala] reṇukā samīpa kṣetre [samīpavartī kṣetre] śrī 
gaṃgāyamunayoḥ paścimetaṭe śrīnarmadāyā##-
vikramādityarājyātīta-amukasaṃkhyāparimite 

p. 8)

pravartamānasaṃvatsareprabhavādiṣaṣṭhisaṃvatsarāṇāṃmadhye 
amukanāmasaṃvatsare amukāyane amukagole amuka-ṛtau amukamāse 
amukapakṣe amukatithau amukavāsare amukayoge amukakaraṇe 
amukarāśisthe sūrye amukarāśisthe candre amukarāśisthe devagurau 
śeṣeṣu graheṣu yathā yathā rāśisthānasthiteṣu satsu 
evaṃgrahagaṇaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭāyāṃśubhapuṇyatithau amukagotra##-
dīrghāyurvipulaghana putrapautrādyanavacchinnasaṃtativṛddhi 
sthiralakṣmī kīrtilābha śavaparājaya sadabhīṣṭasiddhyarthaṃ 
yathāsampāditasāmagryā śārada(vāsantika) navarātrirpatipadivihita 
kalaśasthāpana durgāpūjā kumārīpūjādi kariṣye || tadaṃgatvena 
nirvighnatāparisamāpyarthaṃ gaṇapati paṃcokāra vāstu divyādi 64 yoginī 
ajarāḍhi 50 kṣetrapāla saptaciraṃjīva sapta-ṛṣi 
gauryādiṣoḍaśamātṛkā varuṇakalaśa sūryādi navagraha tadaṃgabhūta 
adhidevatā pratyadhidevatādi sthāpanapūjanānantara 
bhittaudurgāsthāpanāvāhanaṃ kalaśasthāpanaṃ tasyopari durgāpūjanaṃ vā 
pratipadārabhya navamīparya tathā ca trivaṭyupari-akhaṃḍadīpakaṃ 
tilatailapūritaṃ tūlikāvartiyutaṃ ca kariṣye vā brāhmaṇa dvārākārayiṣye ||

agnikoṇe gaṇeśa pūjana ||

oṃ gaṇānāṃtvāgaṇapatiṃhavāmahe kaviṃkavīnāmupaśravastamam || 
jyeṣṭharājaṃ brahmaṇāṃ brahmaṇaspata-ānaḥ śṛṇvannūtibhiḥ 
sīdasādanaṃ || oṃ he heraṃba ! tvamehyehyaṃvikātryambakātmaja || 
siddhibuddhipatetryakṣa koṭisūryasamaprabha || nāgāsya nāgahāra tvaṃ 
gaṇarājacaturbhuja || bhūpitaḥ svāyudhairdivyaiḥ 
pāśāṃkuśaparaśvadhaiḥ || āvāhayāmi pūjārthaṃ rakṣāsrthaṃ ca mama 
kratoḥ || 

p. 9)

ihāgatya gṛhāṇa tvaṃ pūjāṃ rakṣa ca me kratum || oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ 
gaṇeśa ihāgaccha ihatiṣṭha gaṇapataye namaḥ gaṇapatimāvāhayāmi 
sthāpayāmi namaḥ || pādayoḥ pādyaṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ || 
hastayorarghyaṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ || mukhe ācamanīyaṃ samarpayāmi 
namaḥ || sarvāṃge snānīyaṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ || vastropavastrārthe 
alaṃkārārthe kausumbasūtraṃ sākṣatañca samarpayāmi namaḥ || 
yajñopavītaṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ || gaṃdhaṃvilepayāmi namaḥ || 
akṣatānsamarpayāmi namaḥ || puṣpāṇi samarpayāmi namaḥ || durvāṃkurāṇi 
samarpayāmi namaḥ || dhūpamāghrāpayāminamaḥ || pratyakṣadīpaṃ 
[devatāyāḥ dhūpapātraṃ tailadīpaṃ vāme | ghṛtadīpaṃ sitavartiyutaṃ 
dakṣiṇe raktavartiyutaṃ ghṛtadīpamapi vāme | sitavartiyutaṃ taila 
dīpamapidakṣe ||] darśayāmi namaḥ || dhūpadīpapātrayorakṣatānnikṣipet 
|| hastauprakṣālya || naivedyaṃnivedayāmi namaḥ || jalenābhyukṣya || 
gandhapuṣpābhyāmācchādya || dhenumudrayā amṛtīkṛtya 
satyaṃtvartena paripiṃcāmi ( ṛtatvāsatyena paripiṃcāmi iti sāyaṃ) 
grāsamudrāṃ pradarśya oṃ prāṇāya svāhā - oṃ apānāya svāhā - 
oṃ udānāya svāhā oṃ vyānāya svāhā - oṃ samānāya svāhā - 
madhye 2 vācamanīyaṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ || uttarāpoṣaṇārthe 
kiṃcinnaivedyaṃ nivedayāmi namaḥ || punarācamanīyaṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ 
| karodvartanārthe gaṃdhaṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ || hastaprakṣālanārthe 
mukhaprakṣālanārthe jalaṃsamarpayāmi namaḥ || mukhaśuddhyarthe 
tāmbūlaṃ puṅgīphalaṃ elā lavaṃga karpūrayutaṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ || 
yathāśakti dakṣiṇādravyaṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ ||

(1) devatāyāḥ dhūpapātraṃ tailadīpaṃ vāme | ghṛtadīpaṃ 
sitavartiyutaṃ dakṣiṇe raktavartiyutaṃ ghṛtadīpamapi vāme | sitavartiyutaṃ taila 
dīpamapidakṣe ||

prārthanā ||

oṃ bhaktārtināśanaparāya gaṇeśvarāya sarveśvarāya śubhadāya 
sureśvarāya || vidyādharāya | vakaṭāya ca vāmanāya 
bhaktaprasannavaradāya namo namaste || 1 ||

p. 10)

vighneśvarāya varadāya surapriyāya lambodarāya sakalāya jagaddhitāya || 
nāgānanāya sitasarpavibhūṣitāya gaurīsutāya gaṇanātha ! namo namaste || 2 ||

anayā pūjayā siddhibuddhisahitamahāgaṇapatiḥ sāṃgaḥ saparivāraḥ 
prīyatām ||

oṃ ābrahmanbrāhmaṇo brahmavarcasī jāyatāmārāṣṭre rājanyaḥ śūra'##-
saptiḥ purandhiryoṣā jiṣṇuratheṣṭhāḥ sabheyoyuvāsya yajamānasya vīro 
jāyatānnikāme nikāme naḥ parjanyo varṣatu phalavatyona'-oṣadhayaḥ 
vacyantāṁyogakṣemo naḥ kalpatām || oṃbhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ pūrve brahman 
ihāgaccha ihatiṣṭha brahmaṇe namaḥ brahmāṇaṃ āvāhayāmi sthāpayāmi 
namaḥ || oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ dakṣiṇe gāyatri ihāgaccha ihatiṣṭha gāyatryai 
namaḥ || gāyatrīmāvāhayāmi sthāpayāmi namaḥ || oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ 
paścime govarddhana ihāgaccha ihatiṣṭha govarddhanāya namaḥ 
govarddhanamāvāhayāmi sthāpayāmi namaḥ || oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ uttare 
pṛthivi ihāgaccha ihatiṣṭha pṛthivyai namaḥ || pṛthivīmāvāhayāmi 
sthāpayāmi namaḥ || oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ madhye yajñapate ihāgaccha 
ihatiṣṭha yajapataye namaḥ yajñapatimāvāhayāmi sthāpayāmi namaḥ || iti 
pratiṣṭhāpya ||

pūrvavat pādyādi pūjana

oṃ brahmā devī ca gāyatrī tathā govarddhaneśvaraḥ | pṛthvī 
yajñapatiścaitān paṃcoṅkārānnamāmyaham || anayā pūjayā sāṃgāḥ 
saparivārāḥ brahmādipaṃcapraṇavāḥ prīṇantunamama || tatraiva gaṇeśa 
samīpe -

p. 11)

atha agnikoṇe vakrādidvādaśagaṇeśa pūjana

oṃ

vakrādi-dvādaśa-mūrti-gaṇapān āvāhayāmi sthāpayāmi 
namaḥ ||

pādyādi pūjana

oṃ namo devagaṇeśāya namaste vighnanāśana || namo mūṣakamārūḍha 
śubhakartre namonamaḥ || namaḥ kātyāyanīputra namaḥ paraśupāṇaye || 
raverudayaterūpaṃ vidyābuddhi vicakṣaṇa || dehi me rūpa saubhāgyaṃ dehi me 
putrasampadaḥ || icchāsiddhiprado deva yathoktabhava me sadā || anayā pūjayā 
sāṃgāḥ saparivārāḥ vakrādi 12 gaṇapāḥ prīṇantu namama ||

atha nair-ṛtyakoṇe vāstu pūjanam

p. 13)

vasoddhārāpū0 || oṃ vasoḥ pavitramasi śatadhāraṃ vasoḥ

vasoddhāre mahāmāye cāmuṇḍe muṇḍa mālini |
śīghraṃ mamavaraṃdehi parāṃgati namostute ||

sāṃkalpika nāndī śrāddhaḥ dakṣiṇottara krameaṇa ||

deśakālau saṃkīrtya adyadurgāhavanāṅgatvena sāṃkalpika vidhinā 
brāhmaṇayugma bhojana paryāptānnaniṣkriyī bhūta yathāśakti hiraṇyayena 
nāṃdī śrāddhaṃ kariṣye || oṃ satya vasu saṃjñakāḥ viśvedevāḥ 
nāndīmukhāḥ oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ idaṃ vaḥ pādyaṃpādāvanejanaṃ 
pādaprakṣālanaṃ vṛddhiḥ || tataḥ amuka gotrāḥ asmanmātṛ pitāmahī 
prapitāmahyaḥ nāṃdīmukhyaḥ oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ idaṃ vaḥ pādyaṃ 
pādāvanejanaṃ pādaprakṣālanaṃ vṛddhiḥ || amuka notrāsmatpitṛ 
pitāmaha prapitāmahāḥ nāndī mukhāḥ oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ idaṃ vaḥ 
pādyaṃ pādāvanejanaṃ pādaprakṣālanaṃ vṛddhiḥ || dvitīya gotrā 
asmanmātāmaha pramātāmaha vṛddha pramātāmahāḥ sapatnikāḥ 
nāṃdīmukhāḥ oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ idaṃ vaḥ pādyaṃpādāvanejanaṃ 
pādaprakṣālanaṃ vṛddhiḥ || atha āsanadānam || oṃ satyavasu 
saṃjñakānāṃ viśveṣāṃ devānāṃ nāndīmukhānāṃ oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ 
idamāsanaṃ sukhāsanaṃ svāhā namaḥ || iti kuśatrayaṃ sarvato dakṣiṇa 
gataṃ pūrvāgramutsṛjet evaṃ sarvatra || oṃ nāṃdī śrāddhe kṣaṇau 
kriyetām || oṃ tathāprāpnutāṃ bhavantau prāpnuvāva || oṃ amuka gotrāṇāṃ 
asmanmātṛpitāmahī prapitā mahīnāṃ nāndīmukhināṃ oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ 
svaḥ idamāsanaṃ sukhāsanaṃsvāhānamaḥ ||

p. 14)

saṃpadyatām vṛddhiḥ || amuka gotrābhyo'smanmātṛ pitāmahī

gotrebhyo'smatpitṛ pitāmaha prapitāmahebhyonāṃdīmukhebhyo oṃ

svaḥ idaṃ gaṃdhādyarcanaṃ svāhā saṃpadyatāmvṛddhiḥ || tato bhojana 
niṣkraya dravya dānama || oṃ adya tatsanmātrāditrayapitrāditraya 
mātāmahāditraya nāṃdīśrāddha saṃbaṃdhinaḥ satyavasusaṃjñakāḥ 
viśvedevāḥ nāṃdī mukhāḥ oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ idaṃ vaḥ yugmabrāhmaṇa 
bhojana prayāptaṃdāsyamānamannaṃ vā tanniṣkrayī bhūtaṃ 
kiṃciddhiraṇyaṃ amṛtarūpeṇa dattaṃ svāhā saṃpadyatāṃ vṛddhiḥ || 
oṃ adyatatsadamuka gotrāḥ mātṛ pitāmahī prapitamahyo nāṃdī mukhyaḥ 
oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ idaṃ vo yugma brāhmaṇa bhojana paryāptaṃ 
dāsyamānamannaṃ vā tanniṣkrayī bhūtaṃ kiciddhiraṇyaṃ amṛta rūpeṇa 
svāhā saṃpadyatāṃ vṛddhiḥ | amuka gotrāḥ pitṛ pitāmaha 
prapitāmahāḥ nāṃdī mukhāḥ

oṃ gaṇādhipaṃ namaskṛtya namaskṛtyapitāmahaṃ |

rakṣo haṇaṃ valagahanaṃ vaiṣṇavīmida mahantaṃvalagamutkirāmi

kalaśe gaṃdhaṃ prakṣepaḥ

oṃ gaṃdhadvārāṃdurādharṣāṃ nitya - puṣṭāṃkarīṣiṇīṃ || 
īśvarīṃsarvabhūtānāṃtāmihopahvayeśriyam || iti kalaśe gaṃdhaṃ 
prakṣepaḥ ||

atha sarvoṣadhī prakṣepaḥ [kuṣṭaṃ māṃsī haridre dve murā 
śaileyacandanam || vacā caṃpakamuste ca sarvoṣadhyo daśasmṛtāḥ ||]

oṃ yā'-oṣadhīḥ pūrvāyātādevebhyastriyugaṃpurā || 
manainuvabhrūṇāmaha guṃ śataṃdhāmānisaptaca || iti sarvoṣadhī 
prakṣepaḥ ||

atha dūrvāprakṣepaḥ 

oṃ kāṇḍātkāṇḍātprarohantīparuṣaḥparuṣaspari || evāno 
dīrvepratanusahasreṇaśateca || iti dūrvāprakṣepaḥ ||

atha kuśaprakṣepaḥ

oṃ pavitresthovaiṣṇavyausaviturvaḥprasava'-upunāmyacchidreṇa 
pavitreṇa sūryasya raśmibhiḥ || tasyatepavitrapatepavitrapūtasyayatkāmaḥ 
punetacchakeyam || iti kuprakṣepaḥ ||

p. 27)

atha saptamṛttīprakṣepaḥ [gajāśvaratha valmīka saṅgamāddhrada 
gokulāt || mṛdamānīya kumbheṣu prakṣipeccatvarāttathā || gokulāvadhi sapta 
catvareṇasahāṣṭhaubhaveyuḥ ||]

oṃ syonāpṛthivīnobhavānnṛkṣarāniveśanī || yacchānaḥ 
śarmasaprathāḥ || iti saptamṛdaprakṣepaḥ ||

atha puṅgīphalaprakṣepaḥ 

oṃ yāḥ phalinīryā'-aphalā'-apuṣpāyāścapuṣpiṇīḥ || 
bṛhaspatiprasūtāstānomuñcantva guṃ hasaḥ || iti puṅgīphalaprakṣepaḥ ||

atha pañcaratnāniprakṣepaḥ [kanakaṃ kuliśaṃ nīlaṃ padmarāgaṃ ca 
mauktikam || etāni pañcaratnāni ratnaśāstra vido diduḥ |]

oṃ parivājapatiḥ kaviragnirhavyānyakramīt || dadhadratnānidāśupe || vā || 
sahiratnānidāśupesuvātisavitābhagaḥtaṃbhāgaṃcitramīmahe || iti 
pañcaratnāniprakṣepaḥ ||

dakṣiṇāprakṣepaḥ

oṃ hiraṇyagarbhaḥ samavartatāgre bhūtasya jātaḥ patireka'-āsīt || 
sadādhāra pṛthivīndyā mutemāṃ kasmaidevāya haviṣā vvidhema || iti 
dakṣiṇā prakṣepaḥ ||

atha pañcapallavāni prakṣepaḥ [brāhme | aśvatthodubara plakṣa cūta 
nyagrodha pallavāḥ | pañca bhaṅgā iti khyātā sarva karmasu śobhanāḥ || 1 ||
]

oṃ aśvatthevo niṣadanaṃ parṇevovasatiṣkṛtā || gobhāja'-itkilā 
sathayatsanavatha pūruṣam || iti pañca pallavāni prakṣepaḥ ||

atha kausumbasutra baṃdhanam

oṃ yuvā suvāsāḥ parivīta'-āgātsa'-uśreyān bhavati jāyamānaḥ || 
tandhīrāsaḥ kavaya'-unnayanti sādhyo manasā devayantaḥ || 
itikausumbasūtra baṃdhanam ||

p. 28)

atha kalaśopari tandula pūrṇapātra nidhānam

oṃ pūrṇādarviparāpata supūrṇā punarāpata || vasne vavikrīṇā vahā'-iṣa 
mūrjaguṃ śatakrato || iti kalaśopari tandula pūrṇapātra nidhānam ||

atha śrīpahalanidhānam

oṃ śrīścate lakṣmīśca patnyā vaho rātre pārśve nakṣatrāṇi 
rūpamaśvinau vyāttam || iṣṇannṣāṇa mumma '-iṣāṇa sarvalokamma'##-

atha varuṇaṃ saṃpūjya

oṃ tatvāyāmi brahmaṇā vandamānastadā śāstre yajamāno havirbhiḥ || 
aheḍamāno varuṇe havoḍhyuruśaguṃ samāna'-āyuḥ pramoṣīḥ || 
ityanena pādyādibhiḥ varuṇaṃ saṃpūjya |

prārthanā

oṃ kalaśasya mukhe viṣṇuḥ kaṃṭhe rudraḥ samāśritaḥ | 
mūle tatrasthito brahmā madhye mātṛgaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ || 1 ||

kukṣau tu sāgarāssapta saptadvīpā vasundharā |
ṛgvedotha yajurvedo sāmavedohyatharvaṇaḥ || 2 ||

aṃgaiścasahitāḥ sarve kalaśantu samāśritāḥ |
atra gāyatrī sāvitrī śāntiḥ puṣṭi karī sadā || 3 ||

āyāntu yajamānasya (mama gṛhe ca) durita kṣayakārakāḥ |
sarve samudrāḥ saritastīrthāni jaladā nadāḥ || 4 ||

āyāntu yajamānasya [devapūrārthaṃ ityapipāṭhaḥ] (mama gṛhe 
ca) durita kṣayakārakāḥ || deva dānava saṃvāde mathyamāne 
mahodadhau || 5 ||

utpannosi tadā kuṃbhaḥ vidhṛto viṣṇunā svayam |
tvattoye sarva tīrthāni devāḥ sarve tvayisthitāḥ || 6 ||

tvayitiṣṭhanitibhūtānitvayiprāṇāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ |
śivaḥsvayaṃtvamevāsi viṣṇustvaṃca prajñāpatiḥ || 7 ||

p. 29)

ādityāvasavorudrāviśvedevāḥ sa paitṛkāḥ |
tvayi tiṣṭhanti sarve'pi yataḥ kāma phala pradāḥ || 8 ||

tvatprasādādimaṃ yajñaṃ kartumīhe jalodbhavaḥ |
sānnidhyaṃ kurume deva ! prasannobhava sarvadā || 9 ||

oṃ pāśapāṇe namastubhyaṃ padminī jīvanāyaka ! |
pradhāna pūjanaṃ yāvattavattvaṃ sannidhau bhava || iti kalaśapūjanam ||

atha tāntrika rakṣā 

oṃ sūryaḥ somoyamaḥ kāla sandhye bhūtānyahakṣapāḥ || pavano 
digpatirbhūmirākāśaṃkhacarāmarā || brāhmyaṃ śāsana māsthāya 
kalpadhvamiha saṃnidhim || ityanena bhūmiṃ spṛṣṭvā digbaṃdhanaṃ 
kuryāt ||

sāmānyārgha kalaśa sthāpanam

[paṃcagavya pramāṇa - vasiṣṭha saṃhitāyām || gomutraṃ goyayaṃ 
kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpi kuśodakaṃ || paṃcagavyamidaṃ proktammahāpātaka nāśanam | 
1 || gośakṛddviguṇaṃ mūtraṃ dugdhaṃ dadyāccaturguṇam || ghṛtaṃ 
cāṣṭaguṇaṃ caiva paṃcagavyetathādadhi ||

paṃcagavya saṃmelana prakāraḥ

oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ tatsaviturvareṇyaṃ bhargodevasya dhīmahi dhiyo yonaḥ 
pracodayāt || gomūtraṃ || oṃ gandhadvārāṃ durādharṣāṃ nityapuṣṭāṃ karīṣiṇīm 
| īśvarīṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ tāmihopahvayeśriyam || govara || oṃ 
āpyāyasvasametutevviśvataḥ somavṛṣṇyam || bhavāvvājasya saṃgathe || dudha || 
oṃ dadhikrāvṇo'-akāriṣañjiṣṇo raśvaraya vvājinaḥ || 
surabhinomukhākaratpraṇa'-āyūṣitāriṣat || dahī || oṃ 
tejosiśukramasyamṛtamasidhāmanāmāsi || priyaṃ devānāmanā 
dhṛṣṭaṃdevayajanamasi || ghī || 

oṃ devasya tvā savituḥ prasaveśvinorbāhubhyāmpūṣṇo hastābhyām ||]

oṃ raṁ iti jaladhārayā agni prākāraṃ vicintya pūjā mārabhet || tatra 
sādhāraṇa kalaśaṃ saṃsthāpya varuṇaṃ sampūjya pīṭha pūjāṃ kuryāt ||

p. 30)



svavāmabhāge bindu trikoṇavṛttaṃ caturasramaṇḍalaṃ nirmāya || oṃ hrīṃ

ityaṃkuśamudrayā [aiṃ hrīṃ klīṃ cāmuṇḍāyai vicce vā durge durge

saṃyojyākuṃcayetkiṃcinmudraiṣāṃkuśa saṃjñitā ||] tīrthānyā bāhya ||

p. 31)

oṃ hiraṇya varṇāṃ hariṇīṃ suvarṇaṃ rajatasrajām |
candrāṃ hiraṇmayīṃ lakṣmīṃ jātavedo ma āvaha || 1 || śirasi ||

oṃ tāmma āvaha jātavedo lakṣmī manapa gāmanīm |
yasyāṃ hiraṇyaṃ vindeyaṃ gāmaśvaṃ puruṣānaham || 2 || netrayoḥ ||

oṃ aśvapūrṇāṃ (rvāṃ) rathamadhyāṃ hastināda prabodhinīm |
śriyaṃdevī mupahvaye śrīrmādevījuṣatām || 3 || karṇayoḥ ||

oṃ kāṃsosmitāṃ hiraṇyaprākārāmārdrāṃ jvalantīṃ tṛptāṃtarpayantīm 
|
padme sthitāṃ padma arṇāṃ tāmiho pahvaye śriyam || 4 || ghrāṇayoḥ ||

oṃ candrāṃ prabhāsāṃ yaśasā jvalantīṃ śriyaṃ loke deva juṣṭāmudārāṃ 
|
tāṃ padmanī (ne) mīṃ śaraṇamahaṃ prapadye 
a'lakṣmīrmenaśyatāṃ tvāṃ vṛṇe || 5 || mukhe ||

oṃ ādityavarṇe tapaso'dhi jāto vanaspatistavavṛkṣo thabilvaḥ |
tasyaphalāni tapasānudantu māyāntarāyāścavāhyā alakṣmīḥ || 6 || grīvāyāṃ 
||

oṃ upaitu māṃ deva sakhaḥ kīrtiścamaṇinā saha |
prādurbhūto smi rāṣṭresmin kīrtimṛddhaṃ dadātu me || 7 || karayoḥ ||

oṃ kṣutpipāsāmalāṃjyeṣṭhāmalakṣmīṃ nāśayāmyaham |
abhūtimasamṛddhiṃ ca sarvāṃ nirṇudame gṛhāt || 8 || hṛdi ||

oṃ gandhadvārāṃdurādharṣāṃ nityapuṣṭāṃ karīṣiṇīm |
īśvarīṃsarvabhūtānāṃ tāmihopahvayeśriyam || 9 || nābhau ||

oṃ manasaḥ kāmamākūtiṃvācaḥsatyamaśīmahi |
paśūnāṃ rūpamannasyamayi śrīḥ śrayatāṃyaśaḥ || 10 || liṅge ||

oṃ kardamenaprajābhūtā mayi saṃbhava kardama |
śriyaṃvāsaya me kulemātaraṃ padmamālinīm || 11 || gude ||

oṃ āpaḥ srajantusnigdhāni ciklīta vasa me gṛhe |
nicadevīṃ mātaraṃ śriyaṃvāsaya me kule || 12 || ūrvoḥ ||

oṃ ārdrāṃ puṣkariṇī puṣṭiṃ piṃgalāṃpadmamālinām |
candrāṃ hiraṇmayīṃ lakṣmīṃ jātavedo ma āvaha || 13 || jānunoḥ ||

oṃ ardrāyaḥ kariṇīṃ yaṣṭiṃ suvarṇāṃ hemamālinīm |
sūryāṃhiraṇmayī lakṣmīṃ jātavedo ma āvaha || 14 || jaṃghayoḥ ||

p. 32)

oṃ tāmma āvaha jātavedo lakṣmīmanapagāminīm |
yasyāṃ hiraṇyaṃ prabhūtiṃ gāvodāsyośvānviṃdeyaṃ puruṣānaham || 15 || 
caraṇayoḥ ||

oṃ yaḥ śuciḥprayatobhūtvājuhuyādājyamanvaham |
sūktaṃ paṃcadaśarcaṃ ca śrīkāmaḥ satataṃjapet || 16 || sarvāṅge ||

tataḥ kalaśopari svarṇamayīṃ śrī durgā pratimāṃ agnyuttāraṇapūrvakaṃ 
saṃnidhāya paṭṭavastrairācchādya puruṣa sūktena śrīsūktenapurāṇokta 
mārgeṇa vā ṣoḍaśopacāraiḥ yathopacārairvā saṃpūjayet || tadyathā ||

athāgnyuttāraṇa vidhiḥ ||

tatra tāvatsācāryo yajamānaḥ || deśakālausaṃkīrtya || asyāḥ svarṇamayī śrī 
durgā pratimāyāḥ ghaṭanādi doṣaparihārārthaṃ agnyuttāraṇa pūrvakaṃ 
prāṇapratiṣṭhāṃ kariṣye || mūrtiṃ ghṛtenābhyajya || tadupari dugdha 
miśrita jaladhārāṃ kuryāt pātayedvā ||

agnyuttāraṇa mantrāḥ ||

oṃ samudrasyatvā vvakayāgne parivyayāmasi |
pāvako'-asmabhyaguṃ śivobhava || 1 ||

oṃ himasya tvā jarāyuṇāgne parivyayāmasi |
pāvako'-asmabhyaguṃ śivobhava || 2 ||

oṃ upajmannupavetase vataranadīṣvā |
agne pittamaṣāmasi maṇḍukitābhirāgahi semannoyajñaṃ pāvakavarṇa guṃ 
śivaṃkṛdhi || 3 ||

oṃ apāmidaṃ nyayanaguṃ samudrasya niveśanam |
anyāṁste'-asmattapantu hetayaḥ pāvako asmabhyaguṃ śivobhava || 4 ||

oṃ agne pāvaka rociṣāmandrayādevajihvayā || ādevānvakṣiyakṣi ca || 5 ||

oṃ sanaḥ pāvakadī divogne devāṁ 2 '-ihāvaha || upayajña guṃ haviśca 
naḥ || 6 ||

oṃ pāvaka yā yaścitayantyā kṛpākṣāmanruruca ' upasonu bhānunā |
tūrvannayā mannetaśasya nūraṇa'-āyoghṛṇenatatṛṣāṇo' ajaraḥ || 7 ||

p. 33)

oṃ namaste haraseśociṣe namaste'-astvarciṣe |
anyāṃste asmattapantu hetayaḥ pāvako asmabhyaguṃ śivobhava || 8 ||

oṃ nṛṣadevveḍapsuṣadevveḍvarhipadevveḍvanasadevveḍ svarvideveṭ || 9 ||

|

oṃ yedevā deveṣvadhi devatvamā yanye brahmaṇaḥ pura'-etāro.āsya |
yebhyona'-ṛte pavatedhāma kiñcananatedivona pṛthivyā'##-

oṃ prāṇadā'-apānadā vyānadā vvarcodā varivodāḥ |
anyāṃste'-asmattapantuhetayaḥ pāvako.āsmabhyaguṃ śivobhava || 12 ||

tataḥ prāṇapratiṣṭhāṃ kuryāt ||

oṃ āṃ hrīṃ kroṃ yaṃ raṃ laṃ vaṃ śaṃ ṣaṃ saṃ haṃ laṃ kṣaṃ haṃ saḥ 
soham asyāḥ śrīdurgā pratimāyāḥ prāṇā iha prāṇāḥ || oṃ āṃ hrīṃ kroṃ 
yaṃ raṃ laṃ vaṃ śaṃ ṣaṃ saṃ haṃ laṃ kṣaṃ haṃ saḥ sohaṃ asyāḥ śrī 
durgā rpatimāyāḥ jīva ihasthitaḥ || oṃ āṃ hrīṃ kroṃ yaṃ raṃ laṃ vaṃ 
śaṃ ṣaṃ saṃ haṃ laṃ kṣaṃ haṃ saḥ sohaṃ asyāḥ śrī durgā pratimāyāḥ 
sarvendriyāṇi vāṅ manastvakcakṣuḥ śrotra jihvāghrāṇapāṇi pāda 
pāyūpasthāni ihaivāgatya sukhaṃciraṃtiṣṭhantusvāhā || oṃ 
manojūtirjuṣatāmājyasya bṛhaspatiryajñaṃmimantanotvariṣṭaṃ 
yajñaguṃ samimandadhātu || viśve devāsa'-ihamādayantāmoṃ 3 
pratiṣṭha || oṃ eṣavai pratiṣṭhā nāmayajño yatraitena yajñe na yajante 
sarvameva pratiṣṭhitambhavati || iti pratiṣṭhāya || atha netronmīlanam || oṃ 
vṛtrasyāsi kanīna kaścakṣurdā'-asicakṣurmedehi || gaṃdhādi 
paṃcopacārāndatvā saṃskārasiddhaye ṣoḍaṣapraṇavāvṛttiṃ kuryāt || 
anena asyāḥ śrī durgā pratimāyāḥ garbhādhānādi ṣoḍaśa 
saṃskārānsaṃpādayāmi || iti vedet || tataḥ śrī durgā pratimāṃ pradhāna 
kalaśoparidhṛtvā ṣoḍaśopacāraiḥ pañcopacārairvā pūjayet ||

p. 34)

oṃ jaṭājūṭa samāyuktāmarddhendu kṛtalakṣaṇām |
locanatrayasaṃyuktāmpadmendu sadṛśānanām || 1 ||

atasīpuṣpa varṇābhāṃ supratiṣṭhāṃ sulocanām |
navayauvana saṃpannāṃ sarvābharaṇa bhūṣitām || 2 ||

sucāruvadanāṃ tadvatpīnonnatapayodharām |
tribhaṃgasthānasaṃsthāna mahiṣāsuramardinīm || 3 ||

triśūlaṃ dakṣiṇedadyātkhaḍgaṃ cakraṃ kramādadhaḥ |
tīkṣṇavāṇaṃ tathāśaktiṃ vāmatopi nibodhata || 4 ||

kheṭakaṃ pūrṇacāpaṃ ca pāśamaṃkuśamūrddhvajam |
ghaṃṭāṃ vā paraśūṃ vāpi vāmataḥ sannivedayet || 5 ||

adhastānmahiṣaṃ tadvaddviśiraskaṃ pradarśayet |
śiraśchedodbhavaṃtadvaddānavaṃ khaḍgapāṇinam || 6 ||

hṛdi śūlenanirbhinnaṃ nirdayantra vibhūṣitam |
raktaraktīkṛtāṅgañca raktavisphārite kṣaṇam || 7 ||

veṣṭitaṃ nāgapāśena bhrukuṭībhīṣaṇānanam |
sapāśa vāmahastena dhṛtakeśaṃ ca durgayā || 8 ||

vamadrudhiravaktrañca devyāḥ siṃhaṃ pradarśayet |
devyāstu dakṣiṇaṃ pādaṃsamaṃsiṃhopari sthitam || 9 ||

kiṃcidūrdhvaṃ tathā vāmamaṃguṣṭho mahiṣopari |
stūyamānañca tadrūpamamaraiḥ sanniveśayet || 10 ||

ugracaṇḍāpracaṇḍā ca caṇḍogrā caṇḍa nāyikā |
caṇḍā caṇḍavatī caiva caṃḍarūpāticaṇḍikā || 11 ||

ābhiḥ śaktibhiraṣṭābhiḥ satataṃpariveṣṭitam |
caintayetsatataṃ devīṃ dharma karmārtha mokṣadām || 12 ||

p. 36)

iti devīṃ dhyātvā mūlādhāratkuṇḍalinīmutthāpya || tayāsaha śivena

ehi durge! mahābhe! rakṣārthaṃ mama sarvadā |
āvāhayāmyahaṃ devi ! sarva kāmārtha - siddhaye ||

vibhāvya pūjayet || tatramaṃtrāḥ ||

athā āvāhanam [ṭi0 tatraivavācaspatau || 
kuryādāvāhanaṃmūrtaumṛṇmayyāṃ sarvadaivahi || pratimāyāṃ jale vahnau 
nāvāhanavisarjanam ||]

oṃ hiraṇyavarṇā hariṇīṃ suvarṇarajatasrajāṃ |
candrāṃ hiraṇmayīṃ lakṣmīṃ jātavedo mamāvaha ||

oṃ sahasraśīrṣā puruṣaḥ sahasrākṣaḥ sahasrapāt |
sabhūmiguṃ sarvataspṛtvātyatiṣṭhaddaśāṃgulam || 1 ||

oṃ āgacchehamahādevi ! || sarvasampatpradāyinī ! || yāvadvrataṃ samāpyeta 
tāvattvaṃ sannidho bhava || ityāvāhanam ||

oṃ tāṃ ma āvaha jātavedo lakṣmīmanapagāminīm |
yasyāṃ hiraṇyaṃ vindeyaṃ gāmaśvaṃ puruṣānaham || 1 ||

oṃ puruṣa evedaguṃ sarvaṃ yadbhūtaṃ yacca bhāvyam |
utāmṛtatvasyeśāno yadannenātirohati || 2 ||

anekaratnasaṃyuktaṃ nānāmaṇigaṇānvitam |
kārtasvaramayaṃ divyamāsanaṃ pratigṛhyatām || ityāsanam ||

pādyam ||

oṃ aśvapūrṇāṃ (rvāṃ) rathamadhyāṃ hastinādapramodinīm |

p. 37)

śriyaṃdevīmupahvaye śrīrmādevījuṣatām ||

oṃ etāvānasya mahimātojyāyāṃścapūruṣaḥ |
pādosyavviśvā bhūtāni tripādasyāmṛtandivi || 3 ||

gaṅgādi sarva tīrthebhyo mayā prārthanayāhṛtam |
toyametatsukhasparśaṃ pādyārthaṃ pratigṛhyatām || itipādyam ||

arghyam

oṃ kāṃsosmitāṃ hiraṇyaprākārāmādrāṃ jvalantīṃ tṛptāṃ tarpayantīm 
||
padmesthitāṃ padmavarṇāṃ tāmihopahvaye śriyam ||

oṃ tripādūrdhva udaitpuruṣaḥ pādosyehābhavatpunaḥ |
tato viṣvaṃvyakrāmatsāśanānaśane abhi ||

nidhīnāṃ sarva ratnānāṃ tvamanarghyaguṇāhyasi |
siṃhoparisthite devi ! gṛhāṇārghyanamostute || 4 || ityarghyam ||

atha madhuparkam [pārāśaraḥ | sarpireka guṇaṃ proktaṃ śodhitaṃ 
dviguṇaṃ madhu | madhuparkaṃ vidhau proktaṃ sarpiṣā ca samaṃ dadhi ||]

oṃ madhuvvātā ṛtāyate madhukṣarantisindhavaḥ |
mādhvīrnaḥ santvoṣadhīḥ || 

dadhimadhughṛtasamāyuktaṃ pātrayugmasamanvitam |
madhuparkaṃ gṛhāṇa tvaṃ śubhadā bhava śobhane || 5 ||

athācamanam 

oṃ candrāṃ prabhāsāṃ yaśasā jvalantīṃ śriyaṃlokedeva juṣṭāmudārāṃ 
||
tāṃ padmanīmiṃśaraṇamahaṃprapadye alakṣmīrmenaśyatāṃ tvāṃ 
vṛṇe ||

oṃ tato virāḍajāyatavvirājo'-adhipūruṣaḥ |
sajāto'-atyaricyatapaścādbhūmimathopuraḥ ||

karpūreṇasugaṃdhena surabhisvāduśītalam |
toyamācamanīyārthaṃ devi ! tvaṃ pratigṛhyatām || 6 || ityācamanam ||

p. 38)

atha snānam

oṃ ādityavarṇe tapasodhijāto vanaspatistava vṛkṣo'thabilvaḥ |
tasyaphalānitapasānudantu māyāntarāyāśca bāhyā alakṣmīḥ ||

oṃ tasmādyajñātsarvahutaḥ | saṃbhṛtaṃpṛṣadājyam || 
paśūṁ stāṁścakrevāyavyā nāraṇyāgrāmyāśraye || 
mandākinyāḥ samānītairhemāṃbhoruhavāsitaḥ ||
snānaṃ kuruṣvadeveśi ! salilaiśca sugandhibhiḥ || itisnānam || 7 ||

punarācamanīyam

ucchiṣṭoṣyaśucirvāpi yasyasmaraṇamātrataḥ |
śuddhimāpnotitasmai te punarācamanīyakam ||
snānavastropavītānte'pitatsmṛtam || 8 ||

sugandhita taila va itra snānam

oṃ aguṃ śunāte '-aguṃśuḥ pṛcyatāṃparupāparuḥ | 
gandhastesomamavatu madāyaraso'-acyutaḥ || oṃ snehaṃgṛhāṇa snehena 
lokeśvari ! mahāaghe ! || sarvalokeṣu śuddhātmana ! dadāmi snehamuttamam || 
9 ||

pañcāmṛta snānam

oṃ payaḥ pṛthivyāṃpaya'-oṣadhīṣu payodivyantarikṣepayodhaḥ |
payasvatīḥ pradiśaḥ santumahyam || 
kāmadhenusamudbhūtaṃ sarveṣāṃjīvanaṃ param ||
pāvanaṃ yajñahetuśca payaḥ snānārthamarpitam || 1 ||

dadhisnānam

oṃ dadhikrāvṇo-akāriṣaṃ jiṣṇoraśvasyavvājinaḥ || surabhino 
mukhākaratpraṇa āyū guṃ pitāripat || payasastu samudbhūtaṃ madhurāmlaṃ 
śaśiprabham || dadhyānītaṃ mayā devi ! snānārthaṃ pratigṛhyatām || 11 || 

p. 39)

ghṛtasnānam

oṃ ghṛtaṃ ghṛta pāvānaḥ pivatavvasāṃ vasāpāvānaḥ pivatāntarikṣasya 
havirasi svāhā || diśaḥ pradiśa' ādiśovvidiśa uddiśodigbhyaḥ svāhā | 
navanītasamutpannaṃ sarvasaṃtoṣakārakam || ghṛtaṃ gubhyaṃ pradāsyāmi 
snānārthaṃ rpatigṛhyatām || 12 ||

śahadasnānam

oṃ madhunaktamutoṣaso madhumatpārthiva guṃ rajaḥ || madhu dyaurastunaḥ 
pitā || tarupuṣpasamudbhūtaṃ susvādu madhuraṃ madhu || tejaḥ puṣṭikaraṃ 
divyaṃ snānārthaṃ pratigṛhyatām || 13 ||

śarkarā snānam

oṃ apā guṃ rasamudvayasa guṃ sūrye santaguṃ samāhitam | apā guṃ rasasya 
yorasastaṃ vo gṛhṇāmyuttamamupayāmagṛhītosīndrāyatvā juṣṭaṃ 
gṛhvāmyepate yonirindrāyitvā juṣṭatamam || ikṣusāra samudbhūtā 
śarkarā puṣṭikārikā || malāpahārikā divyā snānārthaṃ pratigṛhyatām || 
14 ||

paṃcāmṛta snānam [skāṃde - kṣīddaśaguṇaṃ dadhnā 
ghṛtenaivadaśottaram | madhunātaddaśaguṇaṃsitayātutatodhikam ||]

oṃ paṃcanadyaḥ sarasvatīmapiyanti sasrotasaḥ || sarasvatī tu paṃcadhā so 
deśe bhavatsarit || payodadhi ghṛtaṃ caiva madhu ca śarkarānvitam || 
paṃcāmṛtaṃ mayānītaṃ snānārthaṃ pratigṛhyatām || 15 ||

candanasnānam

oṃ gandha dvārāṃ durādharṣāṃ nityapuṣṭāṃ karīṣiṇīm |
īśvarīṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ tāmihopahva yeśriyam ||
malayācalaṃsaṃbhūtaṃ candanāgaru saṃbhavam ||
candanaṃ devi ! deveśi ! snānārthaṃ pratigṛhyatām || 16 ||

p. 40)

sugandhita ubaṭanasnānam

oṃ aguṃ śunāte'-aguṃ śuḥ pṛcyatāmparuṣāparuḥ || gandhaste soma 
mavatu madāya raso'-acyutaḥ ||

nānāsugandhi dravyaṃ ca candanaṃ rajanīyutam |
udvartanaṃ mayā dattaṃ snānārthaṃ pratigṛhyatām || 17 ||

atha śuddhajalasnānam

oṃ śuddhavālaḥ sarva śuddha vālo maṇivālasta' āśvināḥ śyetaḥ 
śyetākṣoruṇaste rudrāya paśupataye karṇāyāmā'-avaliptā raudrā 
nabhorūpāḥ pārjanyāḥ ||

punarācamanīyaṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ ||

dvau vastram

oṃ upaitumāṃ devasakhaḥ kīrtiśca maṇinā saha |
prādurbhūto surāṣṭresmin kīrtiṃ vṛddhiṃ dadātu me ||

tasmādyajñātsarvahuta'-ṛcaḥ sāmāni jajñire |
chandāṃsi jajñire tasmādyajustasmādajāyata ||

paṭṭakūlayugaṃ deve ! kaṃcukena samanvitam |
paridhehi kṛpāṃ kṛtvā durge ! durgatināśini ! ||

iti yugmavastram || punarācamanīyam ||

athopavītam ||

oṃ kṣutpipāsāmalāṃ jyeṣṭāmalakṣmīṃ nāśayāmyahaṃ | 
abhūtimasamṛddhiṃ ca sarvānnirṇuda me gṛhāt ||
oṃ tasmādaśvā'-ajāyanta yekeco bhayādataḥ ||
gāvohajajñire tasmāttasmājjātā ajāvayaḥ ||

p. 41)

svarṇasūtramayaṃ divyaṃ brahmaṇā nirmitaṃ purā |
upavītaṃ mayā dattaṃ gṛhāṇa parameśvari ||

atha candana snānam

oṃ gandha dvārāṃ durādharṣāṃnityapuṣṭāṃ karīṣiṇīm |
īśvarīṃ sarva bhūtānāṃ tāmihopahvaye śriyam ||

oṃ taṃ yajñaṃ barhiṣi praukṣanpuruṣañjātamagrataḥ |
tena devā ayajanta sādhvā'-ṛṣayaśca ye ||

śrīkhaṇḍacandanaṃdivyaṃ gandhāḍhyaṃ sumanoharam |
vilepanaṃ ca deveśi ! candanaṃ pratigṛhyatām ||

iti candanam || saubhāgya sūtra dānam ||

oṃ saubhāgyasūtraṃ varade ! suvarṇa-maṇi-saṃyutam |
kaṃṭhe vadhnāmi deveśi ! saubhāgyaṃ dehi se sadā ||

kaṃṭha sūtraṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ ||

akṣatam

oṃ akṣannamīmadantahyavapriyā'-adhūpata | astopatasvabhānavo 
vaprānaviṣṭhayāmatīyojānvindrate harī ||
akṣatānnirmalān śuddhān muktāmaṇisamanvitān || gṛhāṇemānmahādevi ! 
dehi me nirmalāṃ dhiyam || ityakṣatān ||

haridrācūrṇam

haridrārañjite devi ! sukhasaubhāgyadāyinī |
tasmāttvāṃpūjayāmyatra sukhaṃ-śāntiṃ prayaccha me ||

haridrācūrṇaṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ ||

gulālam

kuṅkumaṃ kāntidaṃ divyaṃ kāminīkāmasaṃbhavam |
kuṅkumenārcite devi ! prasīda parameśvari ||

p. 42)

iti kuṅkumaṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ ||

sinduram

sindūramaruṇābhāsaṃ japākusumasannibham || 
arpitaṃ te mayā devi ! prasīda parameśvari ! ||

sindūraṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ ||

kajjalam

cakṣurbhyāṃ kajjalaṃramyaṃ subhage ! śāntikārakam |
karupūrajyotirutpannaṃ gṛhāṇa parameśvari ! ||

iti netre kajjalaṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ ||

dūrvāṅkuram

oṃ dārdrāṃ puṣkariṇīṃ puṣṭiṃ piṃgalāṃpadmamālinīm |
candrāṃhiraṇmayīṃ lakṣmīṃ jātavedoma-āvaha ||

oṃ kāṇḍātkāṇḍatprarohantīparuṣaḥ paruṣaspari |
evānodūrve pratanu sahasreṇa śatena ca ||

dūrvādaleśyāmaletvaṃ mahīrūpeharipriye ! |
atodūrvābhirbhavatīṃ pūjayāmi sadā śive ! ||

iti dūrvāṅkurān samarpayāmi namaḥ ||

bilvapatra arpaṇam 

oṃ dārdrāṃ yaḥ kariṇīyaṣṭiṃ suvarṇāṃ hemamālinīm |
sūryāṃ hiraṇmayīṃ lakṣmīṃ jātavedoma āvaha ||

oṃ namo bilmane ca kavacine ca namo varmiṇeca varūthineca namaḥ |
śrutāyaca śrutasenāya ca namo dundubhyāya cāhananyāya ca namaḥ ||
amṛtodbhavaḥ śrī vṛkṣomahādevapriyaḥ sadā ||
bilvapatraṃ prayacchāmi pavitraṃ te sureśvari ! ||

iti bilvapatrāṇi samarpayāmi namaḥ ||

palla arpaṇam

oṃ tāṃma āvaha jātavedo lakṣmīmanatagāminīm |
yasyāṃ hiraṇyaṃ prabhūtiṃ gāvodāsyośvān vindeyaṃ puruṣānaham ||
oṃ aśvatthevo niṣadanaṃ parṇevovvasatiṣkṛtā ||

p. 43)

gobhāja-itkilāsathayatsanavatha pūruṣam ||
gṛha dvāre cogramapiduṣṭāsura nivarhiṇi || 
pūjāṃ karomi cārvaṃgi ! pallavairnaṃdanodbhavaiḥ ||

iti pallavānsamarpayāmi namaḥ ||

phalamālā arpaṇam

oṃ mahādevī ca vidmahe viṣṇu patnī ca dhīmahi || tanno devīḥ pracodayāt || oṃ 
yāḥ phalanīryā' -aphalā' apuṣpāyāścapuṣpiṇīḥ || bṛhaspati 
prasūtāstānomuñcantvaguṃ hasaḥ || 

śaratkāle samudbhūtāṃ niśumbhāsura mardinī ! |
phalamālāṃ varāṃ devi ! gṛhāṇa surapūjite ! ||

iti phalamālāṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ ||

ratnamālā dhāraṇam [muktā mānikya vaiḍūrya gomedānvajravidrumau | 
puṣparāgaṃ marakataṃ garuḍodgāra (nīlam) mevaca || ebhistugrathitā 
svarṇe ratnamāleti kathyate || -śāradāyāṃ ||]

oṃ parivājapatiḥ kaviragnirhavyānyakramīt || dadhadratnānidāśupe || oṃ 
kardamenaprajābhūtā mayi sambhrama kardama || śriyaṃ vāsaya me gṛhe 
(kule) mātaraṃ padma mālinīm || muktāphalayutāṃ mālāṃ 
ratnavaiḍūrya suprabhām || māṇikyasvarṇa grathitāṃ gṛhyatāṃ varade ! 
namaḥ ||

iti ratnamālāṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ ||

puṣpamālāṃ

oṃ āpaḥsrajantusnigdhāniciklīta vasamegṛhe | 
(nī)nicadevīṃ mātaraṃ śriyaṃ vāsaya me kule ||

oṃ śrīścatelakṣmīśca patnyāvahorātre 
pārśvenakṣatrāṇirūpamaśvinauvyāttam |
iṣṇanniṣāṇāmumma-iṣāṇa sarvalokamma'ṣāṇa |
padma śaṃkhaja puṣpādi śatapatrairvicitratām ||
puṣpamālāṃ prayacchāmi gṛhāṇa tvaṃ sureśvari ! ||

p. 44)

iti puṣpamālāṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ ||

puṣpam

oṃ manasaḥ kāmamākūtiṃ vācaḥ satyamaśīmahi |
paśūnāṃ rūpamannasya mayi śrīḥ śrayatāṃ yaśaḥ ||

oṃ yatpuruṣaṃvyadadhuḥ katidhāvya kalpayan |
mukhaṅkimasyāsītkibāhukimūrūpādā - ucyete ||

puṣpairnānāvidhairdivyaiḥ kumudairathacampakaiḥ |
pūjā te kriyate devi puṣpāṇi pratigṛhyatām ||

maṃdārapārijātādi pāṭalī ketakānaci ||
jātī caṃpaka puṣpāṇi gṛhāṇemāni śobhane ! ||

iti puṣpāṇi samarpayāmi namaḥ ||

durgā pradeya puṣpāṇi

kundamandāra punnāga pāṭalī nāga keśaram |
āragvadhaṃ karṇikāraṃ jayantī navamallikā || 1 ||

saugandhikaṃ sakaṃkolaṃ punnāgāśokamallikā |
anyānyapi sugandhīni puṣpapatrāṇideśikaiḥ || 2 ||

iti śakti pallave ||

alaṅkāram ||

hāra-kaṃkaṇa-keyūra-mekhalā kuṇḍalādibhiḥ |
ratnāḍhya kuṇḍalopetaṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ pratigṛhyatām ||

alaṅkārābhāve akṣatān samarpayāmi namaḥ ||

sugandhita itram

oṃ ahirivabhogaiḥ paryetivāhuṁ jyayāhetiṃparivādhamānaḥ | 
hastaghnoviśvāvvayunānidvānpumānpumā guṃsamparipātuviśvataḥ ||

candanāgaru karpūra kuṅkumaṃ rocanaṃ tathā |
kastūryādi sugandhāṃśca sarvāṃgeṣu vilepayet ||

iti parimala (itra) dravyaṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ ||

aṅga pūjāṃ

oṃ durgāyai namaḥ pādau pūjayāmi namaḥ || oṃ mahākālyai namaḥ gulphau 
pūjayāmi namaḥ || 

p. 45)

oṃ maṃgalāyai namaḥ jānudvayaṃ pūjayāmi namaḥ || kātyāyanyai namaḥ 
ūrūdvayaṃ pūjayāmi namaḥ || oṃ bhadrakālyai namaḥ kaṭiṃ pūjayāmi 
namaḥ || oṃ kamalavāsinyai namaḥ nābhiṃ pūjayāmi namaḥ || oṃ śivāyai 
namaḥ udaraṃ pūjayāmi namaḥ || oṃ kṣamāyainamaḥ hṛdayaṃ pūjayāmi 
namaḥ || oṃ kaumāryai namaḥ stanau pūjayāmi namaḥ || oṃ umāyainamaḥ 
hastau pūjayāmi namaḥ || oṃ mahāgauryai namaḥ dakṣiṇabāhuṃ pūjayāmi 
namaḥ || oṃ vaiṣṇavyai namaḥ vāmabāhuṃ pūjayāminamaḥ || oṃ ramāyai 
namaḥ skandhaupūjayāmi namaḥ || oṃ skandamātre namaḥ kaṃṭhaṃ 
pūjayāmi namaḥ || oṃ mahiṣa mardinyai namaḥ netre pūjayāmi namaḥ || oṃ 
siṃhavāhinyai namaḥ mukhaṃ pūjayāmi namaḥ || oṃ māheśvaryai namaḥ 
śiraḥ pūjayāmi namaḥ || oṃ kātyāyanyai namaḥ sarvāṅga pūjayāmi namaḥ 
|| ityaṅga pūjanam ||

dhūpa arpaṇam

oṃ yaḥ śuciḥ prayato bhūtvā juhuyādājyamanvaham |
sūktaṃpaṃca daśarcañca śrīkāmaḥ satataṃ japet ||

oṃ dhūrasi dhūrva dhūrvatandhūrvataṃ yo'smāndhūrvati taṃ dhūrvayaṃ 
vayaṃ dhūrvāmaḥ || devānāmasivahnitamaguṃ sasnitamaṃ 
papritamañjuṣṭatamandevahūtamam || daśāṅgaguggulaṃ dhūpaṃ 
candanāgaru saṃyutam || samarpitaṃ mayā bhaktyā mahādevi ! 
pragṛhyatām ||

dhūpa pātraṃ devatā vāme iti dhūpamāghrāpayāmi namaḥ ||

atha dīpaka balānā va akṣatam

oṃ sarasijanilayesarojahaste dhavalatarāṃśukagandhamālyaśobhe || bhagavati 
! harivallabhe ! manojñe ! tribhuvanabhūtikari ! prasīda mahyam || oṃ 
agnirjyotirjyotiragniḥ svāhā sūryo jyotirjyotiḥ sūryaḥ svāhā || 
agnirvarcojyotirvarcaḥ svāhā sūryo varco jyotirvarcaḥ svāhā || jyotiḥ sūryaḥ 
sūryo jyotiḥ svāhā || 

ghṛtavarti samāyuktaṃ mahātejomahojvalam |
dīpaṃ dāsyāmi deveśi ! suprītābhavasarvadā ||

p. 46)

iti dīpaṃ darśayet ghṛtadīpaṃ sitavarti yutaṃ devatādakṣabhāge |
taila dīpaṃ raktavarti yutaṃ devatā vāma bhāge ||

atha naivedyam ||

naivedyaṃ nivedayāminamaḥ | jalenābhyukṣya || 
gandhapuṣpābhyāmācchādya || dhenumudrayā amṛtīkṛtya || yonimudrāṃ 
pradarśya || satyantvartena paripiñcāmi iti prātaḥ (ṛtaṃtvāsatyena 
paripiñcāmi iti sāyaṃ) || ghaṇṭāṃvādayet ||

grāsamudrāṃ pradarśya || oṃ prāṇāya svāhā aṅguṣṭha anāmikā 
kaniṣṭhābhiḥ || oṃ apānāya svāhā aṅguṣṭha tarjanīmadhyamābhiḥ || oṃ 
udānāya svāhā aṅguṣṭha madhyamā'nāmikābhiḥ || oṃ vyānāya svāhā 
aṅguṣṭhatarjanīmadhyamābhiḥ || oṃ samānāya svāhā sarvāṃgulībhiḥ | oṃ 
ādrāṃ puṣkariṇī puṣṭiṃ suvarṇāṃ hemamālinīm || sūryāṃ hiraṇmayīṃ 
lakṣmīṃ jātavedo ma āvaha || oṃ nābhyā āsīdantarikṣa guṃ śīrṣṇo dyauḥ 
samavartata || padbhyāṃ bhūmirdiśaḥ śrotrāttathā lokāṃ 3 akalpayan || 
annaṃ caturvidhaṃ svādu rasaiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ samanvitam || naivedyaṃ 
gṛhyatāṃ devi ! bhaktiaṃ mehyacalāṃ kuru ||

naivedyaṃ nivedayāmi namaḥ ||

madhye madhye ācamanīyaṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ || uttarāpopṣaṇārthe punaḥ 
punarnaivedyaṃ nivedayāmi namaḥ || punarācamanīyaṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ 
||

ācamanam ||

oṃ dārdrāṃ yaḥ kariṇiṃ yaṣṭiṃ piṅgalāṃpadmamālinīm |
candrāṃ hiraṇmayīṃ lakṣmīṃ jātavedo ma āvaha ||
oṃ yatpuruṣeṇa haviṣā devā yajña matanvata ||
vasanto'syāsīdājyaṃ grīṣma-idhmaḥśaraddhaviḥ ||

p. 47)

ācamyatāṃ tvayā devi ! bhaktiṃ me hyacalāṃ kuru ||
īpsitaṃ me varaṃ dehi paratra ca parāṃ gatim ||

ācamanaṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ ||

karodvartanam 

karodvartanakaṃ devi ! sugandhaiḥ parivasitaiḥ |
īpsitaṃ me varaṃ dehi paratra ca parāṅgatim ||
karodvartanārthe gandhaṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ ||

hasta prakṣālanārthe jalam |

gandhatoyaṃ samānītaṃ suvarṇakalaśe sthitam |
hastaprakṣālanārthāya pānīyaṃ te nivedaye ||

ṛtu phalam ||

oṃ yāḥ phalinīryā aphalā apuṣpā yāścapuṣpiṇīḥ || bṛhaspati prasūtāstā 
nomuñcantvaguṃ hasaḥ || drākṣā kharjūra kadalī panasāmrakapitthakam || 
nārikelekṣujaṃvādi phalāni pratigṛhyatām || ṛtuphalāni samarpayāmi 
namaḥ ||

tāmbūla puṅgīphalam ||

oṃ tāṃma-āvaha jāta vedo lakṣmīmanapagāminīm || yasyāṃ hiraṇyaṃ 
prabhūtiṃ gāvo dāsyośvānvindeyaṃ puruṣānaham || oṃ saptāsyā 
sanparidhayastriḥ sapta samidhaḥ kṛtāḥ || devā yadyajñaṃ tanvānā##-
vīṭikāṃ mukhavāsārthamarpayāmi sureśvari ! ||

dakṣiṇādravyam ||

oṃ hiraṇyagarbhaḥ samavartatāgre bhūtasya jātaḥ patireka'-āsīt | 
sadādhāra pṛthvīṃdyāmutemāṃ kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema || 
pūjāphalasamṛddhyarthaṃ tavāgre svarṇamīśvari ! || sthāpitā tena me 
prītā pūrṇānkuru manorathān ||

p. 48)

dhyānam

durge ! smṛtā harasi bhītimaśeṣajantoḥ svasthaiḥ smṛtā matimatīva 
śubhāṃ dadāsi || dāridryaduḥkhabhayahāriṇi kā tvadanyā 
sarvopakārakaraṇāya sadārdracittā || iti natvā || oṃ devā āyāntu yātudhānā 
apayāntu || durge ! devi ! yajanaṃ rakṣasveti bhūmau prādeśaṃ kṛtvā 
praṇamet ||

nava durgā pūjanam || prathamaṃ śailaputrī pūjanam ||

oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ śailaputri ! ihāgaccha ihatiṣṭha || śailaputryai namaḥ 
śailaputrīmāvāhayāmi sthāpayāmi namaḥ || pādyādibhiḥ pūjanaṃ vidhāya || 
oṃ jagatpūjye jagadvandye sarvaśaktisvarūpiṇi ! || pūjāṃ gṛhāṇa 
kaumāri ! jaganmātarnamostute || 1 ||

brahmacāriṇī pūjanam ||

oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ brahmacāriṇi ! ihāgaccha ihatiṣṭha brahmacāriṇyai 
namaḥ || brahmacāriṇīmāvāhayāmi sthāpayāmi namaḥ || pādyādibhiḥ 
pūjanaṃ vidhāya || oṃ tripurāṃ triguṇādhārāṃ mārgajñānasvarūpiṇīm || 
trailokyavaṃditāṃ devīṃ trimūrtiṃ pūjayāmyaham || 1 ||

candraghaṇṭā pūjanam ||

oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ candraghaṃṭe ihāgaccha ihatiṣṭha candraghaṃtāyai 
namaḥ || candraghaṃtāmāvāhayāmi sthāpayāmi namaḥ || pādyādibhiḥ 
pūjanaṃvidhāya || oṃ kālikāṃ tu kalātītāṃ kalyāṇahṛdayāṃ śivām || 
kalyāṇajananīṃ nityaṃ kalyāṇīṃ pūjayāmyaham || 3 ||

kūṣmāṇḍā pūjanam ||

oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ kūṣmāṇde ihāgaccha ihatiṣṭha kūṣmāṇḍāyai namaḥ || 
kūṣmāṇḍāmāvāhayāmi sthāpayāminamaḥ || pādyādibhiḥ pūjanaṃ vidhāya 
|| oṃ aṇimādiguṇodārāṃ makarākāracakṣupam || ananta śakti bhedāṃ tāṃ 
kāmākṣīṃ pūjayāmyaham || 4 ||

p. 49)

skandamātā pūjanam ||

oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ skandamāta ! ihāgaccha ihatiṣṭha skandamātra namaḥ | 
skanda mātaramāvāhayāmi sthāpayāmi namaḥ || pādyādibhiḥ 
pūjanaṃvidhāya || caṇḍavīrāṃ caṇḍamāyāṃ caṇḍamuṇḍa prabhañjinīm || 
tāṃ namāmi ca deveśīṃ caṇḍikāṃ pūjayāmyaham || 5 ||

kātyāyanī pūjanam ||

oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ kātyāyani ! ihāgaccha ihatiṣṭha kātyāyanyai namaḥ || 
kātyāyanīmāvāhayāmi sthāpayāmi namaḥ || pādyādibhiḥ pūjanaṃ vidhāya || 
oṃ sukhānanda karīṃ śāntāṃ sarva devairnamaskṛtām || 
sarvabhūtātmikāṃ devīṃ śāmbhavīṃ pūjayāmyaham || 6 ||

kālarātrī pūjanam ||

oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ kālarātri ! ihāgaccha ihatiṣṭha kālarātryai namaḥ || 
kālarātrīmāvāhayāmi sthāpayāminamaḥ || pādyādibhiḥ pūjanaṃ vidhāya || 
caṇḍavīrāṃ caṇḍamāyāṃ raktabīja prabhañjinīm || tāṃ namāmi ca 
deveśīṃ gāyatrīṃ pūjayāmyaham || 7 ||

mahāgaurī pūjanam ||

oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ mahāgauri ! ihāgaccha ihatiṣṭha || mahāgaurye namaḥ || 
mahāgaurīmāvāhayāmi sthāpayāmi namaḥ || pādyādibhiḥ pūjanaṃ vidhāya 
|| oṃ sundarīṃ sarṇavarṇāṅgīṃ sukha saubhāgyadāyinīm || santoṣa jananīṃ 
devīṃ subhadrāṃ pūjayāmyaham || 8 ||

siddhidā pūjanam ||

oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ siddhide ! ihāgaccha ihatiṣṭha siddhidāyai namaḥ || 
siddhidāmāvāhayāmi sthāpayāminamaḥ || pādyādibhiḥ pūjanaṃ vidhāya || 
oṃ durgame dustarekārye bhayadurga vināśini ! || pūjayāmi sadā bhaktyā 
durgāṃ durgatināśinīm || 9 ||

p. 50)

iti nava durgā pūjanam ||

jyotiḥ pūjanam ||

oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ ādyāsavasuraujodbhava tejasvarūpa śrī durgāyai 
namaḥ || oṃ jātaveda se sunavāmasomarātīyato nidahāti vedaḥ || sanaḥ 
parṣadati durgāṇi viśvānāveva sindhuṃ duritātyagniḥ || pādyādibhiḥ 
pūjanaṃ vidhāya ||

dhyānam ||

pradhāna sādhāra vikalpa sattā svabhāva bhāvādbhuvana trayasya || sā 
vidyayā vyaktamapīha māyā jyotiḥ parā pātu jaganti nityam || (yoginī 
taṃtre)

deśa kālau saṃkīrtya nava durgā mahotsave || śrī durgāprasāda siddhi dvārā 
sarvāpacchānti pūrvakaṃ mamābhīṣṭa siddhaye nirvighnatāpūrvakaṃ 
samāptyarthaṃ vaṭuka gaṇeśādi sahitaṃ kumārī pūjanaṃ kariṣye ||

gaṇeśa vaṭuka sahita nava kumārī pūjanam [atha kumārī pūjā 
tatprakāraśca devīpurāṇe || brahmovāca || na tathā tuṣyate śakra ! homa dāna 
japena tu || kumārī bhojanenātra yathā devī prasīdati || atra navarātre - prakṣālya 
pādau sarvāsāṃ kumārīṇāṃ ca vāsava | sulipte bhūtale ramye tatra tā āsane 
sthitāḥ || pūjayedgandhapuṣpaiśca sragbhiścāpi manoramaiḥ || pūjayitvā 
vidhānena bhojanaṃ tāsu dāpayet || khaṇḍaṃ laḍḍu guḍaṃ sarpi dadhikṣīraṃ 
samākṣikam || tāsāṃ deyaṃ kumārīṇāṃ sanaissaṃbhojayettu tāḥ || pānīyaṃ 
yācitaṃ deyamannaṃ vā yācitaṃ śubham || tāstṛptāstu yadā sarvāstadā 
tvācamanaṃdadet || ācamya cākṣatāndatvā tvayā kṣantavya mityuta || dātuḥ śirasi 
dātavyāḥ kanyakābhirathākṣatāḥ || tenāpi praṇipātastu kartavyo bhakti pūrvakaḥ || 
anena vidhinā śakra ! devī kṣipraṃ prasīdati || dadāti 
vividhākāmānmanobhīṣṭānsurādhipa ! || rājyaṃ kṛtvā tataḥ 
paścāddevīlokañca gacchati || skānde'pi || ekaikāṃ pūjayetkanyāmekavṛddhyā 
tathaiva ca || dviguṇaṃ triguṇaṃ vāpi pratyekaṃ navakaṃ varam || navabhirlabhate 
bhūmimaiśvaryaṃ dviguṇena tu || eka vṛddhyā 
labhetkṣemamekaikenaśriyaṃlabhe || ekavarṣā tu yā kanyā pūjārthaṃ tāṃ tu 
varjayet || gandha puṣpa phalādīnāṃ prītistasyā na vidyate || yathoktā'lābhetu 
vivāhitāpi yā puṣpiṇī tāvat pūjyā vivāhāntaramapi kanyātvamupajāyate || 
tāvatsaṃpūjyate kanyā tāvatpuṣpaṃ na dṛṣyate || iti bhagavanta bhāskara kṛta 
devī purāṇavacanāt || kāmanā paratvena āsāṃ krameṇa pūjāyāṃ viśeṣa uktaḥ || 
tatraiva || duḥkha dāridrya nāśāya śatrūṇāṃ nāśanāya ca || āyuṣe bala 
vṛddhyarthaṃ kumārīḥ pūjayennaraḥ || āyuṣkāmastrimūrtiṃ tu trivargasya 
phalāptaye || apamṛtyu vyādhi pīḍā duḥkhānāmapanuttaye || saukhya 
dhānyadhanārogya putra pautrādi vṛddhaye || kalyāṇīṃ pūjayeddhīmānnityaṃ 
kalyāṇa vṛddhaye || ārogya sukha kāmī tu dhana kāmastathaiva ca || yaśaskāmī 
naronityaṃ rohiṇīṃ paripūjayet || vidyārthī ca jayārthī ca rājyārthī ca viśeṣataḥ || 
śatrūṇāṃ ca vināśārthī kālikāṃ pūjayennaraḥ || saṃgrāme jaya kāmaśca 
caṃḍikāṃ paripūjayet || duḥkha dāridrya nāśāya nṛpa sammohanāya ca || 
mahāpāpa vināśāya śāmbhavī ca prapūjayet || sarva lokeṣu 
śatraṇāmugrasādhana karmaṇi || durgāṃ durgārti nāśāya pūjayedyatnato budhaḥ 
|| saubhāgya dhana dhānyādi vāṃchitārtha phalāptaye || subhadrāṃ pūjayenmartyo 
dāsī dāsa vivṛddhaye || pūjā prakāraśca tatraiva || prātaḥ kāle viśeṣaṇa 
kṛtābhyaṅgo viśeṣataḥ ||

atha varjya kanyā āha ||

hīnādhikāṅgī kuṣṭhādi vikārāṃ kukulāṃ tathā || granthi sphuṭata sarvāṅgā 
rakta pūya vraṇāṅkitāṃ || jātyandhāṃ kekarāṃ kāṇāṃ kurūpāṃ tanu 
romaśām || saṃtyajedrogiṇīṃ kanyāṃ dāsī garbha samudbhavām ||

atha jñāti bhedena kāmanā bhedeṣu tatpūjyātāmāha || brahmāṇīṃ sarva kāryeṣu 
jayārthe nṛpa vaṃśajām || lābhārthe vaiśya vaṃśotthāṃ sutārthe śūdra 
vaṃśajām || dāruṇe cāntya jātīyāṃ pūjayedvidhanā naraḥ ||

artha varṇa bhedena pūjābhedaḥ || gaurīṃ sarveṣṭa saṃsiddhyai pītāṅgī jaya 
kīrtaye || lābhārthe'ruṇavargāṅgīmasitāmāraṇādiṣviti kvacit || eka vaṃśa 
samudbhūtāṃ kanyāṃ samyak prapūjayediti || kaulāvalī tantre ||]

p. 51)

gaṇeśa pūjanam ||

oṃ gaṃ gaṇapataye namaḥ || pādyādi pūjanaṃ vidhāya ||

dhyānam ||

oṃ udyaddineśvara ruciṃ nija hasta padmaiḥ |
pāśāṃkuśābhaya varāndadhataṃ gajāsyam ||
raktāmbaraṃ sakala duḥkha haraṃ gaṇeśaṃ |
dhyāyetprasannamakhilābharaṇābhirāmam || 1 ||

oṃ kharvaṃ sthūlatanuṃ gajendravadanaṃ lambodaraṃ sundaraṃ
prasyandanmada gandhalubdha madhupa vyālola gaṇḍasthalam
dantāvāt vidāritārirudhiraiḥ sindūra śobhākaraṃ
vande śailasutā sutaṃ gaṇapatiṃ siddhipradaṃ kāmadam || 2 ||

vaṭuka pūjanam ||

oṃ vaṃ vaṭukāya namaḥ || pādyādi pūjanaṃ vidhāya ||

p. 52)

dhyānam ||

oṃ kara kālata kapālaḥ kuṇḍalī daṇḍapāṇistaruṇa timira nīla vyāla 
yajñopavītī || kratu samaya saparyā vighnaviccheda heturjayati vaṭukanāthaḥ 
siddhidaḥ sādhakānām ||

tatravidhiḥ ||

yajamānaḥ pūjayecca kanyānāṃ navakaṃ śubham || dvi 
varṣādyādaśābdāntāḥ kumārīḥ pari pūjayet || 1 || arthādeka 
hāyanālpavayaskā varjyāḥ || tā āsane upaveśyāvāhayet mantreṇa || atha 
āvāhana mantraḥ || oṃ mantrākṣara mayīṃ lakṣmīṃ mātṝṇāṃ rūpa 
dhāriṇīm || navadurgātmikāṃ sākṣātkanyāmāvāhayāmyaham || anenaiva 
mantreṇa navāpi āvāhayet || aśaktau yathāśakti ekāmapi pūjayet || pādyādi 
pūjanaṃ vidhāya ||

p. 53)

dvivarṣā kumārī saṃjñā ||

sarvasvarūpe ! sarveśe ! sarvaśakti svarūpiṇi ! | 
pūjāṃ gṛhāṇa kaumāri ! jaganmātarnamostu te || 1 ||

trihāyanā trimūrti saṃjñā ||

tripurāṃ tripurādhārāṃ trivarga jñāna rūpiṇīm | 
trailokya vanditāṃ devīṃ trimūrtiṃ pūjayāmyaham || 2 ||

caturabdā kalyāṇī ||

kalātmikāṃ kalātītāṃ kāruṇya hṛdayāṃ śivām | 
kalyāṇa jananīṃ devīṃ kalyāṇīṃ pūjayāmyaham || 3 ||

paṃcavarṣā rohiṇī ||

aṇimādi guṇādhārāmakārādyakṣarātmikām | 
anantāṃ śaktikāṃ lakṣmīṃ rohiṇīṃ pūjayāmyaham || 4 ||

ṣaḍabdā kālikā ||

kāmacārāṃ śubhāṃ kāntāṃ kālacakra svarūpiṇīm | 
kāmadāṃ karuṇodārāṃ kālikāṃ pūjayāmyaham || 5 ||

saptahāyanā caṇḍikā ||

caṇḍavīrāṃ caṇḍamāyāṃ caṇḍa muṇḍa prabhaṃjanīm | 
pūjayāmi sadā devīṃ caṇḍikāṃ caṇḍavikramām || 6 ||

aṣṭavarṣā śāṃbhavī ||

sadānandakarīṃ śāntāṃ sarva deva nāskṛtām | 
sarva bhūtātmikāṃ lakṣmīṃ śāmbhavīṃ pūjayāmyaham || 7 ||

navahāyanā durgā ||

durgame dustare kārye bhavaduḥkha vināśinīm | 
pūjayāmi sadā bhaktyā durgāṃ durgārti nāśinīm || 8 ||

daśavarṣā subhadrā ||

sundarīṃ svarṇa varṇābhāṃ sukha saubhāgyadāyinīm | 
subhadra jananīṃ devīṃ subhadrāṃ pūjayāmyaham || 9 ||

p. 54)

nitya āratī

kumārī pūjanānte taddhastādakṣatādikaṃ svaśirasi vidhāya bhaktyā 
anuvrajet suvāsinīṃ - brāhmaṇān bhojayet paścāt iṣṭamitra 
bāṃdhavādinā saha svayamapibhuṃjīta śeṣa kālaṃ gīta vādyādibhirnayet ||

iti kumārī pūjanam ||

aṣṭa rātre na doṣo'yaṃ navarātre tithikṣaye | 
sūtake pūjanaṃ proktaṃ japadānaṃ viśeṣataḥ ||

devī muddiśya kartavyaṃ tatra doṣo na vidyate rajasvalāṃ tathāśauce 
brāhma'niśca supūjayet || sabhartṛkāṇāṃ strīṇāṃ navarātre gaṃdhādi 
sevanaṃ na doṣāya || taduktaṃ hemādrau || gaṃdhālaṃkāra tāmbūla 
puṣpamālyānulepanaiḥ ||

kumārī pūjane viśeṣaḥ kaulāvalī tantre 

evaṃ praṇavayogena caitanyaṃ tattumarcayet |
vāṇī māyā tathā lakṣmīrmāyā kūrcadvayaṃ tataḥ ||

ete ca praṇavāḥ jñeyā kumāryāḥ pari pūjane |
caturdaśa svareṇāḍhyo bhṛguvindvindu saṃyutaḥ ||

caitanya bījaṃ kathitaṃ sādhakānāṃ samṛddhidam |
evaṃ dvābhyāṃ tribhiścaiva saptadhānavadhā punaḥ ||

nitya krameṇa niyataṃ pūjayedvidhi pūrvakam |
vāgbhavena jalaṃdeyaṃ māyayā pādaśaucakam ||

lakṣmyācārgyaṃ pradadyāttu kūrcabījena candanaṃ |
śakti bījena puṣpāṇi dhūpaṃ ṣaṣṭhena dāpayet ||

vāgbhavena purakṣobhaṃ māyayā ca guṇāṣṭakam |
śrī bījena śriyolābhaḥ māyayā śatru saṃkṣayaḥ ||

bhairaveṇa tu bījena ṣaṇḍatvamanugacchati |
nyāsādikaṃ prakurvīta ādausvīya krameṇa tu ||

kumāryāṅge tataḥ paścādviśeṣanyāsamuttamam ||

tato'khaṇḍa dīpadānam || dīpādi vicāro ḍāmara tantra ||

sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ kāṃsyaṃ lohaṃ ca mārtikam |
godhūma māpa mudgānāṃ cūrṇena ghaṭitaṃ tathā ||

p. 55)

sauvarṇe kāryasiddhiḥ syādraupye vaśyaṃjagadbhavet |
tāmraṃ tayorabhāve'pi kāṃsye videveṣaṇaṃ bhavet ||

māraṇaṃ lauhapātre syāduccāṭo mṛṇmaye tathā |
godhūma-cūrṇaṃ ghaṭite vivāde vijayo bhavet ||

māṣaje śatrusaṃstaṃbho maudgesyācchāntisattamā |
sandhikārye nadīkūladvayamṛtsnā samudbhavam ||

alābhe sarva pātrāṇāṃ kuryāttāmraṃ ca mārtikam ||

suvarṇādije dīpe suvarṇādimānaṃ tatraiva ||

sahasra pala saṃkhyāyāṃ pātraṃ śatapalaiḥ smṛtam |
śatārddhapalamānetu triṃśatāpātramuttamam ||

pādonaśata saṃkhyāyāṃ ṣaṣṭikaṃ pātra mucyate |
śatamānetadarddhaṃtadadhikaṃ pala saṃyutam ||

sahasra saṃkhya ke proktaṃ digpale digpalaṃ smṛtam |
nitya dīpe pramāṇaṃ hi palaiḥ saptabhirambike ! ||

atha dīpa svarūpam ||

budhneṣaḍaṃgulaṃ proktamucchrāye ca ṣaḍaṃgulam |
ṣoḍaśāṃgula māyāmaṃ sundaraṃ pātramuttamam ||

nitya dīpetadarddhārddhaṃ mānaṃ sarveṣu karmasu |
(budhnaṃmūlam) atha ghṛta tailayorviśaṣastatraivoktaḥ ||

goghṛtena prakartavyo dīpaḥ sarvārtha siddhaye |
māraṇemāhiṣaṃ proktamuṣṭraṃ vidveṣaṇe bhavet ||

āvikaṃ śāntike proktamājaṃ coccāṭane bhavet |
tilatailena vā dīpaḥ kāryaḥ sarvārtha siddhaye ||

ghṛtābhāve maheśāni ! māraṇe sārṣapeṇa ceti ||

atha vartiḥ ||

ayugmā vartikā grāhyā ekottara śatāvadhi |
gurukārye'dhikā proktā alpe alpā matā priye ! ||

sūtraṃ śvetaṃ tathā pītaṃ māṃjiṣṭhaṃ ca kusumbhakam |
kṛṣṇaṃ ca karvuraṃ ceti ṣaṭkarmasu niyojayet ||

savābhāve sitenaiva kuryādvarttīḥ pṛthak pṛthak ||

p. 56)

atha cālanārthaṃ śalākāpi tatraiva ||

ṣoḍaśāṃgula mānā ca sauvarṇītu śalākikā |
rājataudumbarī vāpi sulakṣā budhankā tathā ||

tīkṣṇogrā saralā madhye triśūlenāṅkitā tatheti ||

atha dīpamukhaṃ tatraiva ||

pūrvābhimukhe tu sarvāptiḥ stambhoccāṭanayostathā |
rakṣā vidveṣayoḥ kāryaṃ paścimāsyaṃ pradīpakam ||

lakṣmī prāptāvuttarāsyaṃ māraṇe dakṣiṇāmukhamiti ||

atha dīpa dāne pratijñā ||

tatra pūrva kalaśāgre [ghaṭārgala yantra prakāraḥ || śāradāyāṃ 9 paṭale 
95 śloke ||] ghaṭārgala yantraṃ ṣaṭkoṇa yantraṃ vā vilikhya || tithi 
vārādyuccārya || adyehaitaddīpa śikhā sama saṃkhya varṣa sahasrāvacchinna 
samayaparicchinna durgānucaratva prāpti pūrvaka bhagavatī prīti 
kāmo'dyārabhya navamyantamaharniśa vātādi doṣa rahitamimaṃ dīpaṃ śrī 
durgā devatākaṃ śrī durgāyāḥ purataḥ prajvālayiṣye || iti pratijñāya || ukta 
kāmeṣu tattatkāmanāmuccaryokta vidhinā dīpaṃ datvā taṃ 
gaṃdhākṣatādibhiḥ pūjayet ||

dīpa sthāpane śakuna vighnādayo ḍāmara tantre || tathāhi ||

dīpasya śakunānvicmi śṛṇi devi ! yathākramam |
yane vijñāta mātreṇa jāyate ca phalāphalam ||

dīpārambhe sureśāni ! navadedaśubhaṃ vacaḥ |
tasminkāle yaduktaṃ hi tattathaiva bhaveddhruvam ||

varjayedaśubhāṃ vāṇīṃ tasminkāle viśeṣataḥ |
raktāmbarī dvijo'vyaṅgo raktamālyānulepanaḥ ||

dīpārambhe samāyāti tasya siddhirna saṃśayaḥ |
śudra varṇaḥ samāyāti siddhaḥ proktātu madhyamā ||

mlecchasya darśane proktaṃ baṃdhanaṃ dīpadasya vai |
mārjāra mūṣakādīnāṃ madhyamaṃ darśanaṃ smṛtam ||

p. 57)

kṛte dīpa vare devi ! vīkṣyate ca śubhāśubham |
dīpajvālā samāślakṣṇā jāyate yadi sundari ! ||

aṣṭabhirdivasaistasya kāryasiddhirbhaveddhruvam |
dīpa jvālā tu deveśi ! yadi vakrā bhavettadā ||

nāśastasya ca bandhūnāṃ proktaḥ sarvārtha nāśakaḥ |
kharakaṅka prabhājvālā yadi syāccasureśvari ! ||

dīpakartuḥ savighnovai maraṇaṃ jāyate dhruvam |
dīpa jyotsnāmbike ! kṛṣṇā jāyate ca surārcite ! ||

śatrūṇāṃ jāyate kāryaṃ dīpakarturnirarthakam |
kṛte dīpeyadānāśastatkṣaṇājjāyate'mbike ! ||

kāryasiddhirvilambena bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ |
kṛta dīpasya nāśaḥ syātpraharatraya madhyata ||

māse varṣe tathā proktā kārya siddhirhi sundari ! |
dīpavaryasya nāśaḥ syādyadi rātrau kadācana ||

tasya gehe dhanaṃ vastu naṣṭaṃ bhavati niścitam |
datte dīpe yadi punaścaṭa caṭeti dhruvambhaet ||

tadā tasya ca kāryaṃ ai naṣṭaṃyāti tathādiśet |
vamatedīpavaryaśceccauratobhayamādadet ||

dīpapātraṃ yadi punaḥ sravate devi ! sundari ! |
gonāśo jāyate tasya dīpa karturna saṃśayaḥ ||

dīpavaryasya pātraṃ vai bhagnaṃ vai dṛśyate yadi |
aṣṭādaśa dinādarvāgyajamānaḥ sabāṃdhavaḥ ||

śrāddhadevasya sadanaṃ gacchati priya kāmini ! |
dīpenaṣṭe punardīpaṃjvālayenmūḍha cetanaḥ ||

dīpa dātā kartā manda cakṣurbhavetsadā |
kṛte dīpe punarvārtā kārayedyadi mānavaḥ ||

dīpavaryamaśucirmānavaḥ spṛśet |

dīpakartuḥ śarīre tu vyādhirvai jāyate nṛṇām |
dīpakāṣṭhāmume ! śvāno mārjāro mūṣakādayaḥ ||

yadi spṛśanti kalyāni ! tāḍanaṃ rājato diśet |
evaṃ dīpavare vighnāḥ bahavaḥ saṃbhavanti hi ||

tasmāddīpaṃ sureśāni ! vilokyaṃ tu pade pade ||

p. 58)

atha dīpavighne śāntiḥ ||

tatra śarkarājya tilataṃḍulaissaghṛtaḥ kamalairvā jayaṃtī maṃtreṇa

mantrāṇāmanyatamena pūrvokta dravyeṇa homaḥ kārya iti sāmpradāyikaḥ ||

kalaśa visarjana vidhiḥ ||

sa yajamāno svasti vācana pūrvakaṃ saṃkalpaṃ vidhāya || deśa kālau 
saṃkīrtya pratipadi gaṇapatyādi sthāpitānāṃ devānāṃnārikela valisahita 
uttara pūjana mahaṃ kariṣye || iti pratijñāṃ kṛtvā yathopacāra sahitaṃ 
gaṇapatyādi devān prapūjya || tato śuddha nārikelaṃ kūṣmāṃḍaṃ vā 
gṛhītvā taṃ saṃpūjya tatra jīva nyāsādikaṃ kṛtvā || oṃ mahāmāye ! 
jaganmātaḥ sarva kāma pradāyini ! dadāmi nārikela (kūṣmāṃḍa) 
baliḥ prasīda varadābhava || arddha bhāgaṃ devyagre saṃsthāpya punaḥ oṃ 
prāṇāya svāhā || oṃ apānāya svāhā || oṃ udānāya svāhā || oṃ vyānāya 
svāhā || oṃ samānāya svāhā || ebhiḥ svāhā || oṃ vyānāya svāhā || oṃ 
samānāya svāhā || ebhiḥ svāhānta mantraiḥ paṃcāhutiṃ jyotiragnau juhuyāt 
||

oṃ uttiṣṭha vbrahmaṇaspate devayantastvemahe || upaprayantu marutaḥ 
sudānava'-indra prāśūbhavāsacā || 1 ||

oṃ brahmaṇaspate tvamasya yantāsūktasya bodhitanayaṃca jijva ||

atha balidānam || 1 ||

atra pakṣatrayaṃ pratyahañca balindadyādityekaḥ || 

kanyā saṃsthe ravau śakraḥ ! śuklāṣṭamyāṃ prapūjya ca | 
droṇa puṣpaiśca bilvāmra jātī punnāga campakaiḥ ||

vidhivatkāli ! kālītijaptvā khaḍgena ghātayediti || 

devī purāṇa vacanādaṣṭamī navamyoriti dvitīyaḥ || 

p. 59)

vviśvantadbhadraṃ yadavantidevābṛhadvademavvidathe suvīrāḥ || ya'##-

atradeśācārātkulācārādvā pakṣatrayānyatamaḥ pakṣa ādaraṇīyaḥ || atrāpi

homena sarvasiddhi syāttasmāttritayamarcayet ||] pūjājapa homa [balihīne tu

dhanakṣayam || -bhaviṣye |] balidānādyanuṣṭhānamityekaḥ ||

sahomamiti dvitīyaḥ || pratipatoṣṭamyantaṃ pratyahaṃ kevalaṃ japa

gaccha gaccha paraṃ sthānaṃ svasthānaṃ devi ! caṇḍike ! |

paśurutpādito devairyajñārtheṣu vidhānataḥ || dharmārtha kāmakokṣārtha paśo ! 
tvāṃ dhātayāmyaham || iti taṃ saṃprārthya || tata utrarābhimukhastatkartā dakṣiṇa

svayameva svayambhuvā || atastvāṃ ghātayiṣyāmi tasmādyajñe vadhovadhaḥ || aiṃ 
hrīṃ śrīṃ iti mantreṇamatsvarūpaṃ vicintayitvātanmūrddhnipuṣpaṃ nyasya tatra

p. 61)

brajasroto jalaṃvṛddhyai sthīyatāṃ ca jaletviha || 4 ||

imāṃ pūjāṃ mayādevi ! yathā śaktyopapāditām |

sukhasantati prāpti kāmaḥ kātyayanīya gotrāyai bhagavatyai mahāmūrtyai śrī

viprahīyatāmitipaṭhet ||

tataḥ khaḍga pūjā ||

tatra pratijñāṃ pūravatkṛtvā || nandakasya parāmūrte mamaśatru nivarhaṇa || 
nīlotpaladala śyāmakṛtsna duḥsvapna nāśana || asirviśasanaḥ

maheśvaraḥ || rohiṇyaśca śarīraṃ te davataṃ ca janārdanaḥ || pitā pitā 
mahodevastvāṃ māṃ pālayatātsadā || iyaṃ yena dhṛtā kṣoṇī hataśca 
mahiṣāsuraḥ || tīkṣṇadhārāya śuddhāya tasmai khaḍgāya te namaḥ || agryaḥ 
praharaṇānāṃ tvaṃ khaḍgo mādravatīsutaḥ || oṃ hrīṃ hrīṃ khaḍga itidhyātvā || 
tatorghyādi datvā || oṃ kāli ! kāli ! yajñeśvari ! lohadaṇḍāya namaḥ || muṣṭi

prasaṅgātstotra pāṭha vidhi |

na ca svayaṃ kṛtaṃ stotraṃ tathānyena ca yatkṛtam |
yataḥ kalau praśaṃsaṃti ṛṣibhirbhāṣitaṃ tu yat ||

sarasvatī stotram ||

śrī bhairava uvāca ||

śṛṇudevipravakṣyāmi stotraṃ parama durlabham |
vāgīśyā māntra garbhaṃtu bhuktimukti phalapradam ||

oṃ asya śrī vāgvādinī śāradā stotra mantrasya mārkaṇḍeyāśvalāyana 
ṛṣiḥ sragdharānuṣṭupa chandaḥ śrī sarasvatī devatā hrīṃ bījaṃ oṃ 
śakti aiṃ kīlakam āśu vāgvivṛddhayejape viniyogaḥ || 

dhyānam ||

śuklāṃ brahma vicāra sāra paramāmādyāṃ jagadvyāpinīm |
vīṇā pustaka dhāriṇīmabhayadāṃ jāḍyāndhakārāpahām ||

haste sphāṭika mālikāṃ vidadhatīṃ padmāsane saṃsthitām |
vande tāṃ parameśvarīṃ bhagavatīṃ buddhi pradāṃ śāradām || 1 ||

brahmovāca ||

hrīṃ hrīṃ hṛdyaikabīje śaśi ruci kamalā kalpa viṣpaṣṭa śobhe |
padme padmopaviṣṭe praṇatajanamano modasaṃpādayitri ||
protpluṣṭājñānakūṭeharinijadayitedevisaṃsāra sāre || 2 ||

aiṃ aiṃ aiṃ iṣṭa mantre kamalabhavamukhāṃbhojarūpa svarūpe |
*? rūpa prakāśe sakala guṇamaye nirguṇe nirvikā * *? 
sthūlenaiva sūkṣmepyavidita viṣaye nāpivijñātatatve |
viśve viśvāntarāle suravara namiteniṣkale nitya śuddhe || 3 ||

hrīṃ hrīṃ hrīṃ jāpa tuṣṭe himaruci mukuṭe vallakī vyagrahaste |
mātarmātarnamaste daha daha jaḍatāṃ dehi buddhi praśastāṃ ||

vidye vedānta gīte śruti pari paṭhite mokṣade mukti mārge |
mārgātīta prabhāve bhava mama varadā śārade śubhrahāre || 4 ||

p. 78)

dhīṃ dhīṃ dhīṃ dhāraṇākhye dhṛtimatinutibhirnāmabhiḥ kīrtanīye 
nitye'nitye nimitte munigaṇa namite nūtane vai purāṇe || puṇyepuṇyaprabhāve 
harihara namite nitya śuddhe suvarṇe mantre mantrārtha tatve mati ! mati ! 
matide mādhava prīti nāde || 5 ||

hrāṃ kṣrīṃ dhrīṃ hrīṃ svarūpe daha daha duritaṃ pustaka vyagrahaste || 
saṃtuṣṭākāracitte smitamukhi subhage jṛṃbhiṇī staṃbhavidye || mohe 
mugdha prabhāve mama kuru kumatiṃ dhvāṃta vidhvaṃsamīḍye || gīrgīrvāg 
bhāratītvaṃ kavivṛṣarasanā siddhidā siddha vidyā || 6 ||

staumi tvāṃ tvāṃ ca vande bhaja mama rasanāṃ mā kadācityajethāḥ || mā me 
buddhirviruddhā bhavatu na ca mano devi me jātu pāpam || māme duḥkhaṃ 
kadācidvipadi ca samaye'pyastu me nākulatvaṃ || śāstrevāde kavitve prasaratu 
mamadhirmāstu kuṃṭhā kadācit || 8 ||

ityetaiḥ ślokaḥ mukhyaiḥ prati dina mupasi stauti yo bhakta namro vāṇīṃ 
vācaspate rapyabhi mata vibhavo vāk paṭurmṛṣṭapaṃkaḥ || 
sasyādiṣṭārthalābhaḥ suta miva satataṃ pātitaṃ sā ca devī || saubhāgyaṃ 
tasya loke prasarati kavitā vighnamastaṃ prayāti || 9 ||

brahmacārī vratī maunī trayodaśyāṃ nirāmiṣaḥ || sārasvato naraḥ 
pāṭhātsasyādiṣṭārtha lābhavān || 17 ||

pakṣadvaye'piyobhaktyātrayodaśyekaviṃśatim || avicchedaṃpaṭheddhīmān 
dhyātvā devīṃ sarasvatīm || 11 ||

śuklāṃvara dharāṃdevī śuklābharaṇabhūṣitām ||
vāñchitaṃ phalamāpnoti salokenātrasaṃśayaḥ || 12 ||

iti brahmāsvayaṃ prāhasarasvatyāḥ stava śubham prayatnena 
paṭhennityaṃsomṛtatvaṃ ca gacchati || 13 ||

yathāśakti japa ||

oṃ śrīṃ sauṃ hrīṃ klīṃ dhīṃ mahālakṣmī mahāsarasvatyainamaḥ || 
itimanuḥ itiśrīrudrayāmaletantre daśavidyārahasye sarasvatī stotraṃ 
sampūrṇam ||

p. 79)

sūta pūjana vidhiḥi ||

viśvasāre || mahāvidyāṃ gṛhītvā ca japejjīvāvadhi priye |
mahāgurunipātādau na pūjāyāṃ vikalpanā ||

mohādvā yadivādaivātpūjayenna ca sādhaka |
tasya sarva vināśaḥ syānmārayettaṃ sadā śivaḥ ||

aśucauvāśucauvāpisarvakālepisarvadā |
pūjayetparayābhaktyānātrakāryāvicāraṇā ||

rudrayāmale ||

pūjayenmṛtake'pi syājjanane sarujopivā ||
sarvatraiva vidhiḥ proktaḥ sarva kāmaphala pradaḥ ||
atha sūtakinaḥ pūjāṃ vakṣyāmyāga macoditām ||
vāhyapūjā krameṇaivadhyānayogenapūjayet ||
devī viṣaye vāhya pūjā kartavyā viśeṣa vidhānāt ||

tathā coktaṃvārāhī tantre ||

tārāyāścaiva kālyāśca tripurāyāścasuvrate ! ||
sūtakemṛtakecaivanatyajeyurjapārcanam ||

yāmale ||

aśucirvāśucirvāpigacchaṃstiṣṭhansvapannapi ||
na doṣomānasejāpyesarvadeśeṣusarvadā ||

viśvasāre ||

jāgracchayāna-uttiṣṭhanbhuṃjānogamanepivā ||
siddhamantre na doṣaḥsyādaśaucaniyamepi ca ||
nakalpanādivārātrau na ca sandhyāvasānake ||

śrutau ||

tadanantaramaśauca mapinaprativandhakam ||
vratayajñavivāheṣuśrāddhehome'rcanejape ||
ārabdhe sūtakaṃnasyādanārabdhetusūtakam ||
ārambhovaraṇaṃyajñe saṃkalpovratajāpayoḥ ||

p. 80)

nāndīśrāddhaṃvivāhādauśrāddhe pākapariṣkriyā ||
nimaṃtraṇantu vā śrāddhe ārambhaḥsyāditiśrutiḥ ||

rudrayāmale -

jāta sūtakamāsai syādaste ca mṛtasūtakam || sūtaka dvaya saṃyukto na 
mantraḥ siddhidāyakaḥ ||

japādau japānte ca sūtakadvayamityarthaḥ || brahma bījaṃ manordatvā 
cādyañca parameśvari ! || sapta vāraṃ japenmantraṃ sūtaka dvaya mucyate || 

iti rāghava bhaṭṭa kṛta viṣṇu vacanāt || dīkṣā śabdārtha māha yāmale ||

dīkṣā śabdārthaḥ

divya jñānaṃ yato dadyātkuryātpāpakṣayaṃ yataḥ |
tena dīkṣeti lokesminkīrtitā tantrapāragaiḥ ||

kulārṇave 15 ullāse || mantrajape pāṭhe ca bhedaḥ ||

manasā yaḥ smaretstotraṃ vacasā vā manuṃ japet |
ubhayaṃ niṣphalaṃ devi ! bhinna bhāṇḍodake yathā || guruśabdārthaḥ ||

guśabdastvandhakāraḥ syādruśabdastannirodhakaḥ ||
andhakāra nirodhitvādgururityabhidhīyate ||

gakārād jñāna saṃpattī rephaḥ pāpasya dāhakaḥ |
ukārā cchivatādātmyaṃ dadyāditi guruṃ smṛtaḥ || kula cūḍāmaṇī ||

udāsīno hyudāsīnāṃ vanasthāḥ vanavāsinaḥ |
yatīnāñca yatīprokto gṛhasthānāṃ gurugṛhī ||

vaiṣṇave vaiṣṇavo grāhyaḥ śaive śaivastathā punaḥ |
śākta ke tritayaṃ vidyāddīkṣāsvāmī na saṃśayaḥ || gururapi gṛhastha eva 
kulārṇave ||

sarva śāstrātha vettā ca gṛhastho guru rucyate |

p. 81)

guru śabdārthaḥ yāmale ||

gakāraḥ siddhidaḥ prokto rephaḥ pāpasya dāhakaḥ |
ukāraḥ śakti ityuktastritayātmā guruḥ smṛtaḥ ||

mantra śabda vyutpatti māha ||

mananaṃ viśva vijñānaṃ trāṇaṃ saṃsāra baṃdhanāt |
yataḥ karoti saṃ siddho mantra ityucyate tataḥ || piṃgalāmate ||

mananāttrāṇanāccaiva madrūpasyāvabodhanāt |
mantra ityucyate samyaṅ madadhiṣṭānataḥ priye || rūdrayāmale ||

guptopadeśa to mantrī mananāt trāṇanādapi || tantrāntare ||

toḍala tantrokta mantra caitanya vidhiḥ ||

sarva maṃtrasya caitanyaṃ śṛṇu pārvati sādaraṃ | 
sahasrāre mahāpadme bindurūpaṃ paraṃ śivaṃ ||

kuṇḍalinīṃ samutthāpya haṃsena manunā sudhīḥ |
nāsāgre yā sthirā dṛṣṭirjāyate parameśvari ||

tadaiva mantra caitanyaṃ kuṇḍalī cakragaṃ bhavet |
sahasrāre mahāpadme kuṇḍalyā sahitaṃ guruṃ ||

bhāvayetsarva maṃtrāṇāṃ caitanyaṃ jāyate priye |
tadeva prajapenmantraṃ siddhidaṃ nātrasaṃśayaḥ ||

devī pratimāsthāpane viśeṣaḥ ||

yāmyāsyā śubhadā durgā pūrvāsyā jaya varddhinī paścimābhi mukhī 
nityaṃ na sthāpyā saumyadiṅ mukhī ||

devī bhakti taraṅgiṇyāṃ devī purāṇe ca toḍalatantre ||

śrīśiva uvāca || 

mūlādhārekāma rūpaṃ hṛdijālaṃdharaṃ priye ! |
pūrṇa girimadhobhāge uḍḍiyānaṃtadūrdhvake ||

vārāṇasī bhruvormadhye jvalantī locanatraye |

p. 82)

māyāvatī mukhavṛtte kaṇṭhecāṣṭa purītathā nābhimūlemaheśāni ! 
ayodhyāpurī saṃsthitā || kāṃcī pīṭhaṃkaṭīdeśe śrīcakraṃ 
pṛṣṭhadeśake || mūladhārāt śatāścaiva atalaṃparikīrtitam || sutalaṃ ca 
varṣaśataṃ talātalaśataṃ priye || ṛṣivāṇendu varṣāntaṃ saṃsthitaṃ ca 
mahātalam || śatadvayāntaṃ pātālaṃ dviśataṃ vai rasātalam || 
mūlādhārācca deveśi ! dveṃgulī cāntikesthite || tayormadhya ca pātāla 
stiṣṭhanti parameśvari ! || iti te kathitaṃkānte ! yogasāraṃsamāsataḥ || 
navaktavyaṃpaśoragre prāṇāntepi kadāca na ||

toḍalatantre 10 ullāse ||

tārādevī nīlarūpā kamalā kūrma caṃḍikā |
dhūmāvatī varāhaḥ syāt chinnamastā nṛsiṃhikā ||

bhuvaneśvarīvāmanaḥ syānmātaṃgī rāma mūrtikā |
tripurājāmadagnyaḥ syādvalabhadrastubhairavī ||

mahālakṣmīrbhavedbuddhodurgāsyād kalkirūpiṇī |
svayaṃ bhagavatī kālī kṛṣṇa mūrtiḥ samudbhavāḥ ||

iti te kathitaṃ devyavatāraṃ daśamevahi |
etāsāṃ pūjanāddevi mahādeva samobhavet ||

gandharva tantre ||

na dadyādbhāskarāyārghyaṃ śaṃkhatoyairmaheśvari |
yāvannadīyate cārgho bhāskarāya maheśvari ||

tāvanna pūjayedviṣṇuṃ śaṅkaraṃ vā sureśvarīm |
sūryaḥ somo yamaḥ kālo mahābhūtāni pañcavai ||

ete śubhāśubhasyeha karmaṇo nava sākṣiṇaḥ |
sarve devā śarīrasthāḥ mama mantrasya sākṣiṇaḥ ||

pūrva janmārjitāṃ vidyāṃ mama haste pradāpaya ||

japa phalaṃ kulārṇave ||

gṛhe śataguṇaṃ vidyād goṣṭhe lakṣaguṇaṃ bhavet |

p. 83)

koṭirdevālaye puṇyamamantaṃ śivasannidhau ||

upacāra śabdartho jñāna mālāyām ||

bhaktyā caite kṛtā deve sādhakaṃ deva sānnidhim |
cārayanti yatastasmā ducyante hyupacārakāḥ ||

samīpe cāraṇādvāpi phalānānte tathoditāḥ |
aṣṭatriṃśat ṣoḍaśo'rka daśa pañcopacārakāḥ ||

tānvibhajya pravakṣyāmi ke ke te taiḥ kṛtaiścakim ||

āsanaṃ prathamaṃ teṣāmāvāhanamupasthitiḥ ||

snānaṃ nīrājanaṃ vastra mācāmaṃcopavītakam |
punarācāma bhūpe ca darpaṇālokanaṃ tataḥ ||

gandha puṣpe dhūpa dīpanaivedyaṃ ca tataḥ kramāt |
pānīyaṃ toya mācāmaṃ hastavāsastataḥ param ||

tāmbūlamanulepañca puṣpa dānaṃ punaḥ punaḥ |
gītaṃ vādyaṃ tathā nṛtyaṃ stutiṃ caiva pradakṣiṇam ||

puṣpāñjaliṃ namaskārāvaṣṭa triṃśatsamīritā |
puṣpāñjali namaskārau viṣṇu prītyaibhavantyamī ||

tantroktopacārāḥ ||

upacāraṃ pravakṣyāmiśṛṇu pārvati ! sādaram |
vinopacārairyā pūjā sā pūjā na prasīdati ||

aṣṭā daśopacārāstu sarveṣāmuttamāḥ priye ! ||

ṣoḍaśīti pradhānā ca daśadhātadanusmṛtā |
pañcadhātadanuproktā kartavyābhūti micchatā ||

phetkāriṇī tantre || aṣṭādaśopacārāḥ ||

āsanā vāhanañcārghyaṃ pādyamācamanantathā |
snānaṃ vāsopavītañca bhūṣaṇāni ca sarvaśaḥ ||

p. 84)

gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ tathā dīpaṃ dhūponnaṃcāpi tarpaṇam |
mālyānulepanaṃ caiva namaskāro visarjanam ||

aṣṭādaśopacāraistu mantrī pūjāṃsamācaret ||

ṣoḍaśopacārāḥ tantre ||

āsanaṃ svāgataṃ pādyamarghyamācanīyakam |
madhuparkācamanaṃ snānaṃ vasanaṃ bharaṇāni ca ||

gandhapuṣpe dhūpadīpe naivedyaṃ vandanastathā |
prayojayedarcanāyāmupacārāṃśca ṣoḍaśaḥ ||

daśopacārāḥ ||

pādyārghyācamanīyañca madhuparkācamanastathā |
gandhādayo naivedyāntā upacārāḥ daśātmakāḥ ||

pañcopacārāḥ ||

gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ ca dhūpaṃ ca dīpaṃ naivedyameva ca |
pradadyātparameśāni ! pūjā paṃcopacārikā ||

pūjane varjya padārthāḥ ||

sarvaṃ paryuṣitaṃ varjyaṃ patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ jalam |
avarjyaṃ jānhavī toyamavarjyaṃ tulasīdalam ||

avarjyaṃ vilvapatraṃ syādavarjyaṃ jalajaṃ tathā |
puṣpaiḥ paryuṣitairdevi nārcayetsvaṇajairapi ||

bilvapatraṃcamārghyaṃ ca tamālāmalakīdalam |
kalhāraṃ tulasī patraṃ padmaṃca maṇi puṣpakam ||

etatparyuṣitaṃ na syāt yaccānyatkalikātmakam |
tiṣṭheddinatrayaṃ śuddhaṃ padmamāmalakantathā ||

dinaikaṃ karavīrāṇi ye'nyāni ca tapodhana |
padmāni sita raktāni kusumānyutpalāni ca ||

p. 85)

eṣāṃparyuṣitā śaṅkā kāryā pañcadināddhataḥ ||

gaṇeśa stutiḥ saddharma cintamaṇau ||

prātaḥ smarāmi gaṇanātha manātha bandhuṃ sindūrapūrṇa pariśobhita 
gaṇḍa yugmam || uddaṇḍa vighna pari khaṇḍana caṇḍa daṇḍamākhaṇḍalādi 
suranāyaka vṛnda vandyam || 1 ||

prātarnamāmi caturānana vandyamānamicchānukūlamakhilaṃ ca varaṃ 
dadhānam |
tantundilaṃ dvirasanādhipa yajña sūtraṃ putraṃ vilāsa caturaṃ śivayoḥ 
śivāya || 2 ||

prātarbhajāmyabhayadaṃ khalu bhakta śoka dāvānalaṃ gaṇa vibhuṃ vara 
kuṃjarāsyam |
ajñānakānana vināśana havyavāhamutsāha vardhanamahaṃ 
sutamīśvarasya || 3 ||

ślokatrayamidaṃ puṇyaṃ sadā sāmrājyadāyakam |
prātarutthāya satataṃ yaḥ paṭhetprayataḥ pumān ||

devī stutiḥ saddharma cintāmaṇau ||

prātaḥ smarāmi śaradindu karo jvālābhāṃ sadratnavanmakara kuṇḍala 
hārabhūṣām |
divyāyudhorjita sunīla sahasrahastāṃ raktotpalābha caraṇāṃ bhavatīṃ 
pareśām || 1 ||

prātarnamāmi mahiṣāsura caṇḍa muṇḍa śumbhāsura pramukha daitya 
vināśa dakṣām |
brahmendra rudra muni mohana śīlalolāṃ caṇḍīṃ samasta suramūrtimaneka 
rūpām || 2 ||

prātarbhajāmi bhajatāmabhilāṣa dātrīṃ dhātrīṃ samastajagatāṃ duritāpa 
hantrīṃ |
saṃsāra bandhana vimocanahetu bhūtāṃ māyāṃ parāṃ samadhi gamya 
parasya viṣṇoḥ || 3 ||

śloka traya midaṃ devyāścaṇḍikāyāḥ paṭhennaraḥ |
sarvānkāmānavāpnoti devī loke mahīyate ||

tathā ca śāradāyāṃbhuvaneśvarīṃprati śivavākyam || adyāpya śeṣa 
jagatāṃ navayauvanāsi śailādhirāja tanayāpyati komalāsi || 

p. 86)

samayātantre ||

kadācitkasyamuktiḥ syātkadācidbhuktirevaca |
etasyāḥ sādhakasyātha bhuktirmuktiḥ kare sthitā ||

rudrayāmale ||

yatrāsti bhogo na ca tatra mokṣo yatrāsti mokṣaḥ na ca tatra bhogaḥ || 
śivāpadāmbhoja yugārcakānāṃ bhogaśca mokṣaśca karastha eva || 
yo'nyebhyo darśanebhyaśca bhuktiṃ muktiṃ ca kāṃkṣati || 
svapnalabdhaghnenaiva dhanavānsabhavedyadi || śuktorajatavibhrāntiryathā 
jāyeta pārvati ! || tathānya darśanebhyaśca bhuktiṃ muktiṃ ca kāṃkṣati ||

śāktānandataraṃgiṇyāṃ ṣaṣṭholāse ||

ātmasthāṃ devatāṃ tyaktvā vahirdevaṃ vicinvate |
karasthaṃ kaustubhaṃ tyaktvā bhramate kāca tṛṣṇayā ||

pratyakṣaṃ kṛtva hṛdaye vahiḥ sthāṃ pūjayecchivāṃ |
yasya-yasya ca devasya tathā bhūṣaṇa vāhanam ||

tadeva pūjanetasya cintayetparameśvari |
athāntaryajanaṃvakṣye yena devamayobhavet ||

sukhāsane samāsīnaḥ prāṅmukho vā udaṅmukhaḥ |
svakīya hṛdaye dhyāyet sudhāsāgara muttamam ||

ratna dvīpañcatanmdhye suvarṇa vālukamayam |
mandārapārijātādyaiḥ kalpavṛkṣaiḥ su puṣpitaiḥ ||

sarvatolaṃkṛtairdivyarnitya puṣpa phaladrumaiḥ |
nāna sugandha kusuma gandhāmotidiṅmukhaṃ ||

utphullakusumāmodaprahṛṣṭabhṛṅga saṃkulam |
kūjatkokila saṃgena vācālita digantaram ||

sarvatolaṃkṛtaṃ divyaṃ lasatkāṃcana paṅkajam |
mauktikaiḥ kusumaiḥ sragbhirdukūlaiḥ svarṇatomaraiḥ ||

tanmadhye saṃsmareddevikalpavṛkṣaṃ manoharam |
catuḥśākhācaturvedaṃguṇatraya samanvitam ||

pītaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ tathā śvetaṃ raktaṃ puṣpaṃ ca sundari |
haritaṃ ca vicitraṃ ca nānā puṣpa virājitam ||

kokilarbhramarardevi śobhitaṃ bahu pakṣibhiḥ |
evaṃ kalpadrumaṃ dhyātvātadadhoratnavedikām ! ||

tatroparimahadvyāptaṃ cintayedraktamaṇḍalam |
udyadāditya saṃkāśaṃ ratna sopāna maṇḍitam ||

dhvajāvalī samākīrṇaṃ caturdvāra samanvitam |
nānā ratnādi śobhāḍhyaṃ ratnaprākara maṇḍitam ||

sva svasthāna sthitāvasthirlokapālairadhiṣṭhitam |
siddha cāraṇa gaṃdharvairvidyādhara mahoragaiḥ ||

kinnarairapsarobhiśca krīḍadbhiḥ paridiṅ mukham |
nṛtya vāditra niratairamarastrīgaṇairyutam ||

candanāguru kastūrītathāmṛgamadavilepitam ||

tanmadhye saṃsmareddevimahāmāṇikya vedikām |

p. 87)

udyadarkendukiraṇaścatuṣkoṇapraśobhitam ||

dhyāyetsiṃhāsanaṃtatra brahma viṣṇu śivatmakam |

ratna siṃhāsane dadyātsvāgataṃ kuśalaṃvadet |
pādyaṃ ca pādayordevi śirasyārghyaṃ nivedayet ||

parāmṛtamācamanīyaṃ pradadyānmukha paṃkaje |
madhuparkaṃ mukhedadyāttridhā ācamanaṃmukhe ||

hemapatra gataṃ divyaṃ paramānnaṃ paristatam |
kapilā ghṛta saṃyuktamannaṃ vyañjana saṃyutam ||

sudhāmbudhiṃ māṃsa śailaṃ matsyarāśiṃ phalāni ca |
bhakṣaṃ bhojyaṃ tathā lehyaṃ carvyaṃ coṣyaṃ tathaiva ca ||

sakarpūraṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ mānasaṃ parikalpayet |
āvaraṇastatodevyāḥ pūjanaṃmanasaivahi ||

itthamantaḥ samārādhya manasaivajapenmanum |
sahasrādi japaṃ kṛtvā devyai sodakamarpayet ||

brahmā viṣṇuśca rudraśca īśvaraśca sadāśivaḥ |
etadeva mahādevyāḥ paryaṃkaṃsamudāhṛtam ||

payaḥ phena nibhāṃśayyāṃ nānā puṣpopaśobhitāṃ |
puṣpa śaiyāṃ ca saṃ kuryāttatradevīṃ sureśvarīm ||

cintayetsādhakoyogīnānāsukha vilāsinīm |
nṛtya gītaiḥ sa vādyaiśca toṣayetparameśvarīm ||

tato homaṃ prakurvīta pūjā sārthakya hetave ||

durgā 16 upacārāḥ mānasika pūjane ||

udyaccandana kuṃkumāruṇapayo dhārābhirāplāvitam ||

p. 88)

nānānargha maṇi pravāla ghaṭitāṃ dattāṃ gṛhāṇāmbike ! |
āmṛṣṭāṃ sura sundarībhirabhito hastāmbujairbhaktitaḥ
mātaḥ sundari ! bhakta kalpa latike ! śrīpādukāmādarāt || 1 ||

devendrādibhirarcitaṃ suragaṇairādāya siṃhāsanam |

gandhodvartanamādareṇa taruṇī dattaṃgṛhāṇāmbike ! || 2 ||

paścāddevi ! gṛhāṇa śambhu gṛhiṇi ! śrī sundari ! prāyaśaḥ |

sa candana sukuṃkumāgurutareṇa vibhrājitām ||
mahāparimalojvalāṃ sarasa śuddha kastūrikām |
gṛhāṇa varadāyini ! tripura sundari ! śrīpade || 4 ||

gandharvāmara kinnara priyatamā santāna hastāmbuje |

cainaṃ nirmalamātanotu vasanam śrī sundari ! tvanmude || 5 ||

svarṇākalpita kuṇḍale śrutiyuge hastāmbuje mudrikā |
madhyesārasanā nitamba phalade maṃjīramaṃghridvaye ||
hāro vakṣasi kaṅkaṇokvaṇa raṇatkārau kara dvandvake |
vinyastaṃ mukuṭaṃ śirasyanudinaṃ dattonmadaṃ stūyatām || 6 ||

kalhārotpala nāgakeśara sarojākhyāvalī mālatī |

dhūpo'yaṃ surakāmini viracitaḥ śrīcaṇḍike ! tvanmukhe || 11 ||

ghṛta drava parisphuradrucira ratna yaṣṭyānvito ||

suvarṇa caṣaka sthitaḥ saghana sāra vartyānvitaḥ ||
tava tripura sundari ! sphurati devi ! dīpomude || 12 ||

pakvānnena sapāyasena madhunā dadhyājya saṃmiśritaḥ ||
naivedyaṃ surakāminī viracitaṃ śrī caṇḍike ! tanmukhe || 13 ||

p. 90)

lavaṅga kalikojvalaṃ vahulanāga vallī dalaṃ ||

|| atha sandhyā vidhiḥ || tataḥ śveta vāsasī paridhāya || oṃ maṇi dharaṇi

siṃdūreṇa tilakaṃ kṛtvācamya || mūlena prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ vidhāya || 
ṛṣyādi ṣaḍaṃga nyāsaṃ vidhāya vāma haste jala mādāya dakṣa hastena 
pidhāya || laṃ haṃ yaṃ raṃ vaṃ iti paṃ bhautika bījairabhimantrya śirasi 
mantreṇāṃgulyāntargata tadudakaviṃdubhirmūlamuccaran saptadhā tatva 
mudrayāmūrddhni prokṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā jala rekhāṃ dakṣiṇe kṛtvā nāsā 
mupanīya vāma nāsayākṛṣya dehāntarvarti samastaṃ pāpaṃ tenaprakṣālya 
kṛṣṇa varṇaṃtajjalaṃ vāmanāsā puṭenahasta praviṣṭaṃ saṃcintya puraḥ 
kalpita vajra pāṣāṇe triḥ astrāya phaṭ iti kṣiptyā ācamya mūlena niḥśvasan 
sūryāyāṃjalitrayaṃ datvā || oṃ mahā devyai vidmahe durgāyai dhīmahi tanno 
devī pracodayāt || iti gāyatrī maṣṭottara śatāvṛtyaṣṭā viṃśatidhā 
vāṣṭadhā vā prajapediti sandhyā vidhiḥ || 

atha tarpaṇam ||

jale yaṃtraṃ vibhāvya tarpaṇīya devatā iha yācitvāvāhya || oṃ brahmā

sahitāṃ śrīcaṇḍikāṃ tarpayāmi namaḥ || daśadhā tridhā vā tarpayet || 
evaṃ sandhyā tarpaṇāśaktāvapi trikāladevīṃ dhyātvā yathāśakti mūlaṃ 
vā gāyatrīṃ japet || tataḥ oṃ hrīṃ haṃsaḥ mārtaṇḍa bhairavāya prakāśa 
śaktisahitāya idamarghyaṃ svāheti triḥ sūryārghyaṃ datvā sūryamaṇḍale 
devīṃ vibhāvya mūlamuccārya udyadāditya maṇḍala vartinyai śia caitanya 
mayyai brahmā viṣṇu rudra sahitāyai caṇḍikāyai idamarghyaṃ svāheti 
maṃtreṇa rakta candana javāpuṣpa kuśajalākṣata pūripūrṇena 
tāmrapātreṇa sūryamaṇḍalasthāyai devyai arghyaṃ datvā gāyatrīṃ yathā 
śaktiṃ prajapya guhyeti maṃtreṇa samarpya sūryamaṇḍale devīṃ 
visarjayediti saṃdhyāvidhiḥ ||

atha pūjā vidhiḥ ||

|| ete śubhāśubhasyeha karmaṇo nava sākṣiṇaḥ ||

p. 101)

[bhūtaśuddhau || sarvāsu vāhyapūjāsu antaḥ pūjā vidhīyate |

bhūta śuddhiṃ kuryāt ||

yathā hūṁkāraṇa mūlādhārātkuṇḍalinīmutthāpya jīvātmanā saṃyojya

bhavasaṃcitaṃ || brahmahatyā śiraḥ skandhaṃ sarṇasteya bhujadvayam ||

recayet || tato vamiti bījajapāt lalāṭe candrānmātṛkā varṇamayīmamṛta

[atha yāmalokta bhūtaśuddhiḥ prārabhyate ||

oṃ sūryaḥ somo yamaḥ kālaḥ saṃdhyā bhūtāni paṃca |
ete śubhāśubhasyeha karmaṇomama (nava) sākṣiṇaḥ || 1 ||

bho deva ! prākṛtaṃ cittaṃ pāpākrāṃtamabhūnmama |
tanniḥsāraya cittānme pāpaṃ testu namo namaḥ || 2 ||

iti prārthya svadakṣiṇabhāge oṃ guṃgurubhyo namaḥ || svavāma bhāge oṃ gaṃ

bhūtaśuddhiṃ kuryāt || tathā ca kumbhaka prāṇāyāme mūlādhārāt kuṇḍalanīṃ 
paradevatāṃ visataṃtunibhāṃ samutthāpya brahmaraṃdhragatāṃramṛtvā

brahmaraṃdhraṃ gatvā haṃsaḥ sohaṁ iti maṃtreṇa jīvaṃ brahmaṇi saṃyojayet ||

pṛtvhīmaṃḍalaṃ (oṃ laṃ) iti bhūvījāḍhyaṃ smaret || 1 ||
