maṃḍalāya nama iti gaṃdha puṣpābhyāṃ saṃpūjya tadupari anna 
vyañjanayutamādhāraṃ baliṃcanidhāya || sarvabhūtavighnakṛtbalidravyāya 
namaḥ || itigaṃdhapuṣpābhyāṃsaṃpūjya || balimupanīya oṃ hrīṃ 
sarvavighnakṛdbhyaṃ sarva bhūtebhya imaṃ baliṃ gṛhṇa 2 huṁ phaṭ svāhā || 
iti sarvāṅgulibhiḥ balimutsṛjet || dakṣena jalaṃ tyajet || 

prārthanā ||

bhūtā ye vighna kartārāḥ divibhūmyantarikṣagāḥ ||
pātālasthalasaṃsthāśca śivayogena bhāvitāḥ ||
krūrādyāḥ śata saṃkhyākāḥ pākhaṃḍādyā vyavasthitāḥ || 
dhruvādyāḥ satyasakhyāśca indrādyāśā vyavasthitāḥ ||
tṛpyantu prītimanaso bhūtā gṛhṇantvimaṃbaliṃ ||
nagarevāthasaṃgrāme aṭavyāṃvaisarittaṭe ||
vāpī kūpeṣu vṛkṣeṣu śmaśāne ca catuṣpathe ||
nānārūpadharā ye ca bahurūpadharāśca ye ||
te sarve caiva santuṣṭā baliṃ gṛhṇantu me sadā || iti puṣpāñjaliṃ dadyāt ||

pradhāna baliḥ ||

devatāgre madhye bhūmau sindūreṇa vindu-trikoṇa-vṛtta##-
annavyañjanayutamādhāraṃ baliṃ ca nidhāya || oṃ mūlaṃ durgābalidravyāya 
namaḥ iti gandha puṣpābhyāṃ sampūjya || mūlaṃ sāṃgāyai sāyudhāyai 
savāhanāyai saparivārāyai saśaktikāyai brahmā viṣṇu rudra sahitāyai 
triguṇātmikācaṇḍikādevyainamaḥ iti saṃpūjya || balimupanīya || mūlam ehyehi 
jagatāṃ janani ! imamāmiṣānnabaliṃ gṛhṇa 2 siddhiṃ dehi 2 śatrukṣayaṃ kuru 2 
hrīṃ hrīṃ huṁ phaṭ svāhā eṣa baliḥ sāṅgāyai sāyudhāyai savāhanāyai 
saparivārāyai saśaktikāyai brahmā-viṣṇu-rudra sahitāyai triguṇātmikā 
śrī durgā devyai namaḥ || iti vāmāṅguṣṭhānāmikābhiḥ balimutsṛjet dakṣa 
hastena jalantyajet ||

prārthanā || oṃ śaraṇāgatadīnārtaparitrāṇa parāyaṇe ! ||
sarvasyārti hare devi nārāyaṇi ! namostu te ||

1. siṃha balimaṃtroyam śāradātaṃtre oṃ vajranakhadaṃṣṭrāyudhāyasiṃhāya 
huṁphaṭ namaḥ || 2. oṃ sauṃ vanaspti putrāyasiṃhāya imaṃ baliṃ gṛhṇa 2 
svāhā || mahiṣabali maṃtraḥ || oṃ bhūṃ mahiṣaśṛṃgebhyo mahiṣebhyaḥ imaṃ 
baliṃ gṛhṇa 2 svāhā || mahiṣa bali maṃtraḥ ||

oṃ sarvebhyo balidevatābhyo namaḥ || iti sarvamabhyarcya || (dhyāyetkuṇḍalinī 
śaktiṃ visatantu-svarūpiṇīm || prasuptabhujagākārāṃ sārddhatrivalyānvitām 
|| mukhemukhaṃ tu saṃyojya svayambhūliṅga veṣṭinīm || 
vahnindvarkataḍitpuñjaprabhāṃ caitanyarūpiṇīm || iti tantrāntare ||) nārāca 
sudrāṃ vaddhvā || balidānena saṃtuṣṭāḥ kṣamadhvaṃ balidevatāḥ || 
yathāsukhaṃvitarantu yatheṣṭamuditāvarāḥ || vaṭukādyāḥ surāḥ sarve 
sarvasiddhiṃvidhāyinaḥ || śāntiṃ puṣṭiṃ prayacchantu tvatprasādānmaheśvari || 
stutvā mudrāṃvisṛjya prokṣaṇī jalenātmānaṃ prokṣaṇediti ||] 
paṃcamahābhū) baliṃdadyāt || tatra mantrāḥ ||

p. 130)

ye raudrāḥraudrakarmāṇo raudrasthānanivāsinaḥ |

kṣetrakīlanam ||

p. 132)

|| śūlaṃ pāśa sudarśane ca dadhatīṃ hastaiḥ prasannānana

nirgandhaṃ keśa kīṭādi dūṣitaṃ cogragaṃdhakam || 41 ||

guptasādhana tantre 1 paṭale ||

oṃ brahma sthāna saroja madhya vila sacchītāṃśu pīṭha sthitam ||

naite tārayituṃ bhavanti kuśalāḥ yasya prasādaṃ vinā ||

revā sindhu sarasvatī tripathagā sūryātmajā kauśikī ||

taṃ vande śivarūpiṇaṃ nija guruṃ sarvārtha siddhi pradam || 5 ||

viṣṇubhakti rahaṃ tena viṣṇumāyā ca vaiṣṇavī || 29 ||

nārāyaṇasya māyāhaṃ tena nārāyaṇī smṛtā ||
tavājñayā pañcadhāhaṃ pañca prakṛtirūpiṇī || 30 ||

kalā kalāṃśayāhaṃ ca devapatnyorgṛhe gṛhe ||
prathamaṃ pūjitā sā ca kṛṣṇena paramātmanā ||
madhukaiṭabha bhītena brahmaṇā sā dvitīyataḥ || 31 ||

tripura preṣitenaiva tṛtīye tripurāriṇā ||
bhraṣṭa śriyā mahendreṇa śāpāddurvāsasaḥ purā || 38 ||

caturthe pūjitā devī bhaktyā bhagavatī satī ||
tatomunīndraiḥ siddhendrairdrevaiśca manu manumānavaiḥ || 39 ||

pūjitā sarva viśveṣu babhūva sarvataḥ sadā ||
tejaḥsu sarva devānāmāvirbhūtā purā mune || 40 ||

sarva devādadustasyai śastrāṇi bhūṣaṇāni ca |
durgādayaśca daityāśca nihatā durgayā mayā || 41 ||

dattaṃ svarājyaṃ devebhyo varañca yadabhīpsitam |
kālāntare pūjitā sā surathena mahātmanā || 42 ||

rājñāṃmedhasa śiṣyeṇa mṛṇmayyāṃ ca sarittaṭe ||
meṣādibhiśca mahiṣaiḥ kṛṣṇa sāraiśca gaṇḍakaiḥ || 43 ||

chāgairmīnaiśca kūṣmāṇḍairbhaktibhiḥ pūjitā mune ||
vedoktāni ca datvaivamupacārāṇi ṣoḍaśa || 44 ||

p. 169)

dhṛtvā ca kavacaṃ dhyātvāsaṃpūjyaivaṃ vidhānataḥ ||

muktiṃ saṃprāpya vaiśyaśca saṃpūjya ca sarittaṭe ||
puṣkare dustaraṃ taptvātapo durgāprasādataḥ || 46 ||

vaiśyojagāma golokaṃ śrīkṛṇenānumoditaḥ ||
rājāpisvarājyaṃ gatvā vividhānbhogān ṣaṣṭhivarṣa sahasrakaṃ 
niṣkaṇṭakaṃ bubhuje tataḥ paraṃ bhāryā putre sannayasya puṣkare 
tapastaptvā sāvarṇirnāma manurbabhūva || iti brahmavaivarte ||

śaratkāle vārṣikī durgāpūjā kriyate sātridhā || sātvikī rājasī tāmasī 
cetiviśrutiḥ || sātvikī nirāmiṣairnivedyairjapayajñādyaiśca || māhātmyaṃ 
bhagavatyāstu purāṇādiṣukīrtitam || pāṭhastasyajapaḥ proktaḥ 
paṭheddevīmanāstathā || devī sūkta japaścaiva yajñovahniṣu tarpaṇam || 
rājasī balidānaiśca naivedyaiḥ sāmiṣaistathā || 
surāmāṃsādyupāhāraijapayajñairvinā tu yā || vinā maṃtraistāmasī 
syātkirātānāṃ tu sammatā ||

pāṭhakramo yathā argalaṃ kīlakaṃ cādau paṭhitvā kavacaṃ paṭhet ||
japetsaptaśatīṃpaścātkrama eṣaḥ śivoditaḥ || 1 ||

argalaṃduritaṃ hanti kīlakaṃ phaladaṃ tathā ||
kavacaṃ rakṣayennityaṃ caṇḍikā tritayaṃ tathā || 2 ||

ādau ca praṇavaṃ japtvā stotraṃ vā saṃhitāṃ paṭhet ||
ante ca praṇavaṃ dadyādityuvācādipūruṣaḥ || 3 ||

paṭhat || karmāsaktavanā na paṭhet prayataḥ paṭhet ādhāredhṛtvā 
paṭhennakaratala grahaṇena paṭhet phalābhāvāt ||

bhaviṣye ||

agni hotrādi karmāṇi vedayajñāḥ sadakṣiṇāḥ |
caṇḍikārcārcanasyaite lakṣāṃśenāpi no samāḥ || 1 ||

oṃ asya śrī argalā stotra mantrasya viṣṇur-ṛṣi anuṣṭup chandaḥ śrī 
mahālakṣmīrdevatā śrī jagadambāprītaye saptaśatī pāṭhāṃge 
japeviniyogaḥ ||

oṃ namaścaṇḍikāyai || mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||

oṃ jayantīmaṅgalā kālī bhadrakālī kapālinī |
durgā kṣamā śivā dhātrī svahā svadhā namostute || 1 ||

jaya tvaṃ devi cāmuṇḍe jayabhūtārthāriṇi |
jaya sarvagate devi kālarātri namostute || 2 ||

madhukaiṭabhavidrāvividhātṛvarade namaḥ |
rūpaṃ dehi jayaṃ dehi yaśo dehi dviṣo jahi || 3 ||

mahiṣāsuranirnāśa bhaktānāṃ sukhade namaḥ |

p. 196)

rūpaṃ dehi jayaṃ dehi yaśo dehi dviṣo jahi || 4 ||

raktabījavadhe devi caṇḍamuṇḍavināśini |
rūpaṃ dehi jayaṃ dehi yaśo dehi dviṣo jahi || 5 ||

vaṃditāṃghriyugedevi sarvasaubhāgyadāyini |
rūpaṃ dehi jayaṃ dehi yaśo dehi dviṣo jahi || 6 ||

acintyarūpacarite sarvaśatru vināśini |
rūpaṃ dehi jayaṃ dehi yaśo dehi dviṣo jahi || 7 ||

p. 197)

natebhyaḥ sarvadā bhaktyā caṇḍike duritāpahe |
rūpaṃ dehi jayaṃ dehi yaśo dehi dviṣo jahi || 8 ||

stuvadbhyobhaktipūrvaṃ tvāṃ caṇḍike vyādhināśini |
rūpaṃ dehi jayaṃ dehi yaśo dehi dviṣo jahi || 9 ||

caṇḍikesatataṃ ye tvāmarcayantīha bhaktitaḥ |
rūpaṃ dehi jayaṃ dehi yaśo dehi dviṣo jahi || 10 ||

dehi saubhāgyamārogyaṃ dehi me paramaṃ sukham |
rūpaṃ dehi jayaṃ dehi yaśo dehi dviṣo jahi || 11 ||

vidhehi dviṣatāṃ nāśaṃ vidhehi balamuccakaiḥ |
rūpaṃ dehi jayaṃ dehi yaśo dehi dviṣo jahi || 12 ||

vidhehidevi kalyāṇaṃvidhehiparamāṃśriyam |

p. 198)

rūpaṃ dehi jayaṃ dehi yaśo dehi dviṣo jahi || 13 ||

surāsuraśiroratnanighṛṣṭacaraṇe'mbike |
rūpaṃ dehi jayaṃ dehi yaśo dehi dviṣo jahi || 14 ||

vidyāvantaṃyaśasvantaṃ lakṣmīvantaṃjanaṃ kuru |
rūpaṃ dehi jayaṃ dehi yaśo dehi dviṣo jahi || 15 ||

pracaṇḍadaityadarpaghni caṇḍike praṇatāya me |
rūpaṃ dehi jayaṃ dehi yaśo dehi dviṣo jahi || 16 ||

caturbhujecaturvaktrasaṃstute parameśvari |
rūpaṃ dehi jayaṃ dehi yaśo dehi dviṣo jahi || 17 ||

kṛṣṇenasaṃstutedeviśaśvadbhaktyā sadāmbike |
rūpaṃ dehi jayaṃ dehi yaśo dehi dviṣo jahi || 18 ||

p. 199)

himācalasutānāthasaṃstute parameśvari |
rūpaṃ dehi jayaṃ dehi yaśo dehi dviṣo jahi || 19 ||

indrāṇī patisadbhāvapūjite parameśvari |
rūpaṃ dehi jayaṃ dehi yaśo dehi dviṣo jahi || 20 ||

devi pracaṇḍadordaṇḍadaityadarpavināśini |
rūpaṃ dehi jayaṃ dehi yaśo dehi dviṣo jahi || 21 ||

devibhaktajanoddāmadattānandodaye'mbike |
rūpaṃ dehi jayaṃ dehi yaśo dehi dviṣo jahi || 22 ||

patnīṃ manoramāṃ dehi manovṛttānusāriṇīm |
tāriṇīṃ durgasaṃsārasāgarasyakulodbhavām || 24 ||

idaṃstotraṃ paṭhitvātu mahāstotraṃ paṭhennaraḥ |
sa tu saptaśatīsaṃkhyāvaramāpnoti sampadāṃ oṃ || 25 || 

iti śrī mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe argalāstotraṃ samāptam ||


p. 200)

asya śrī kīlaka mantrasya śiva ṛṣiḥ anuṣṭup chandaḥ śrī mahāsarasvatī 
devatā śrī jagadambā prīyartthaṃ saptaśatī pāṭhāṃga jape viniyogaḥ ||

oṃ namaścaṇḍikāyai || mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||

oṃ viśuddhajñānadehāya trivedīdivyacakṣuṣe |
śreyaḥ prāpti nimittāya namaḥ somārddhadhāriṇe || 1 ||

sarvametadvijānīyānmantrāṇāmapi kīlakam |
so'pi kṣemamavāpnoti satataṃ jāpyatatparaḥ || 2 ||

sidhyantyuccāṭanādīni vastūni sakalānyapi |

p. 201)

etena stuvatāṃ devī stotramātreṇasidhyati || 3 ||

na mantro nauṣadhaṃ tatra na kiñcidapi vidyate |
vinā jāpyena sidhyeta sarvamuccāṭanādikam || 4 ||

samagrāṇyapi sidhyanti lokaśaṅkāmimāṃ haraḥ |
kṛtvā nimantrayāmāsa sarvamevamidaṃ śubham || 5 ||

stotraṃ vai caṇḍikāyāstu tacca guptañcakāra saḥ |
samāpnoti supuṇyena tāṃ yathāvanniyantraṇām || 6 ||

so'pikṣema mavāpnoti sarvameva na saṃśayaḥ |
kṛṣṇāyāṃ vā caturdaśyāmaṣṭamyāṃ vā samāhitaḥ || 7 ||

dadātipratigṛhṇāti nānyathaiṣāprasidhyati |

p. 202)

itthaṃrūpeṇa kīlena mahādevena kīlitam || 8 ||

yo niṣkīlāṃ vidhāyaināṃ nityaṃ japati saṃsphuṭam |
sasiddhaḥ sagaṇaḥ so'pi gandharvo jāyate naraḥ || 9 ||

na caivāpyaṭatastasya bhayaṃ kvāpīha jāyate |
nāpamṛtyuvaśaṃ yātimṛto mokṣamavāpnuyāt || 10 ||

jñātvā prārabhya kurvīta na kurvāṇo vinaśyati |
tato jñātvaiva sampannamidaṃ prārabhyate budhaiḥ || 11 ||

saubhāgyādi ca yatkiñcid dṛśyate lalanājane |

p. 203)

tatsarvaṃ tvatprasādena tena jāpyamidaṃ śubham || 12 ||

śanaistu japyamāne'smin stotre sampattiruccakaiḥ |
bhavatyeva samagrāpi tataḥ prārabhyameva tat || 13 ||

aiśvaryaṃ yatprasādena saubhāgyārogyasampadaḥ |
śatruhāniḥ paromokṣaḥ stūyate sā na kiṃ janaiḥ oṃ || 14 ||

iti bhagavatyāḥ kīlaka stotraṃ samāptaṃ śubhambhūyāt || 3 ||


atha navārṇa vidhiḥ 

śrī gaṇapatirjayati || oṃ asya śrīnavārṇamantrasya brahmaviṣṇurudrā##-
śrīmahākālīmahālakṣmīmahāsarasvatyo devatāḥ nandā śākambharī 
bhīmāḥ śaktayaḥ raktadantikā durgā bhrāmaryo bījāni 
agnivāyusūryāstatvāni ṛgyajuḥsāmavedāḥ dhyānāni 
śrīmahākālīmahālakṣmī mahāsarasvatīprītyarthaṃ japeviniyogaḥ |

p. 204)

atha ṛṣyādinyāsaḥ ||

oṃ brahmaviṣṇvurudra ṛṣibhyo namaḥ śirasi || oṃ 
gāyatryuṣṇiganuṣṭupchandobhyo namo mukhe || oṃ mahākālī mahālakṣmī 
mahāsarasvatī devatābhyo namo hṛdi || oṃ nandāśākambharī bhīmāḥ 
śaktibhyo namo dakṣiṇastane || oṃ raktadantikā durgābhrāmarī 
bījebhyonamovāmastane | oṃ agnivāyusūryatatvebhyo namo nābhau || iti 
ṛṣyādi nyāsaḥ || athavā tantrāntare || (aiṃ bījam hrīṃśaktiḥ klīṃ 
kīlakam || oṃ aiṃ bījāya namo guhye || oṃ hrīṃ śaktaye namaḥ pādayoḥ || oṃ 
klīṃ kīlakāya namo nābhau || iti pāṭhāntaram) || mūlena karau 
saṃśodhya || mantra mahodadhau 18 taraṃge 111 ślokataḥ || navārṇa 
ekādaśa nyāsa prakāraḥ tatrādau śuddhamātṛkā nyāsaḥ prathamaḥ || 

kṛtenayena devasya sārūpyaṃ yāti mānavaḥ ||

sārasvata nyāsaḥ || 2 ||

asminsārasvate nyāsekṛte jāḍyaṃ vinaśyati ||

oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ klīṃ namaḥ kaniṣṭhayoḥ || oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ klīṃ namaḥ 
anāmikayoḥ ||

p. 205)

oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ klīṃ namaḥ madhyamayoḥ || oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ klīṃ namaḥ 
tarjanyoḥ || oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ klīṃ namaḥ aṃguṣṭhayoḥ || oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ klīṃ 
namaḥ karatalayoḥ || oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ klīṃ namaḥ karapṛṣṭhayoḥ || oṃ aiṁ 
hrīṃ klīṃ namaḥ maṇibandhayoḥ || oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ klīṃ namaḥ kūrparayoḥ || 
oṃ e hrīṃ klīṃ namaḥ hṛdayāyanamaḥ || oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ klīṃ namaḥ śirase 
svāhā || oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ klīṃ namaḥ śikhāyai vaṣaṭ || oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ klīṃ namaḥ 
kavacāyahuṃ || oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ klīṃ namaḥ netratrayāya vauṣaṭ || oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ 
klīṃ namaḥ astrāyaphyaṭ || iti dvitīyaḥ sārasvata nyāsaḥ ||

atha tṛtīya mātṛkā gaṇa nyāsaḥ ||

tṛtīye'sminkṛte nyāse trailokyavijayī bhavet ||

oṃ hrīṃ brāhmī pūrvasyāṃ māṃ pātu || oṃ hrīṃ māheśvarī āgneyyāṃ 
māṃ pātu || oṃ hrīṃ kaumārī dakṣiṇasyāṃ māṃ pātu || oṃ hrīṃ vaiṣṇavī 
nair-ṛtyām māṃ pātu || oṃ hrīṃ vārāhī paścimāyām māṃ pātu || oṃ 
hrīṃ indrāṇī vāyavyām māṃ pātu || oṃ hrīṃ cāmuṇḍā uttarasyāṃ māṃ 
pātu || oṃ hrīṃ mahālakṣmī īśānyām māṃ pātu || oṃ hrīṃ vyomeśvarī 
ūrdhve māṃ pātu || oṃ hrīṃ saptadvīpeśvarī bhūmau māṃ pātu || oṃ hrīṃ 
kāmeśvarī pātāle māṃ pātu || iti tṛtīya nyāsaḥ ||

p. 206)

atha caturthaḥ ṣaḍdevī nyāsaḥ ||

turyaṃ nyāsaṃ naraḥ kuryājjarā mṛtyuṃ vyapohati |

oṃ kamalāṅkuśamaṇḍitā tandajā pūrvāṅgaṃ me pātu || oṃ khaḍgapātra 
dharā raktadantikā dakṣiṇāṅgaṃ me pātu || oṃ puṣpapallava 
saṃyutāśākambharī pṛṣṭhāṃgaṃ me pātu || oṃ dhanurbāṇakarā 
durgārtihāriṇī durgā vāmāṃgaṃ me pātu || oṃ śiraḥ pātrakarā bhīmā 
mastakāccaraṇāntaṃ me pātu || citrakānti bhṛdbhrāmarī caraṇābhyāṃ 
śiraḥ paryantaṃ me pātu || iti caturtha nyāsaḥ ||

atha brahmākhya nāmaka pañcama nyāsaḥ ||

kṛtesminpañcame nyāse sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt ||

oṃ brahmā sanātanaḥ pādādi nābhi paryantaṃ māṃ pātu || oṃ janārdanaḥ 
nābherviśuddhi paryantaṃ nityaṃ māṃ pātu || oṃ rudrastrilocanaḥ 
viśuddhebrahmaraṃdhrāntaṃ māṃ pātu || oṃ haṃ saḥ pādadvayaṃ me 
pātu || oṃ vainateyaḥ kara dvayaṃ me pātu || oṃ vṛṣabhaścakṣuṣā me pātu 
|| oṃ gajānanaḥ sarvāṅgāni me pātu || oṃ sarvānandamayo hariḥ parāparau 
deha bhāgau me pātu || iti pañcamo brahmādi nyāsaḥ ||

p. 207)

atha mahālakṣmyādi ṣaṣṭhanyāsaḥ ||

oṃ aṣṭādaśabhujāyuktamahālakṣmīrmadhye me pātu || oṃ aṣṭabhujāyukta 
mahāsarasvatī ūrdhvaṃ me pātu || oṃ daśabhujā maṇḍitā mahākālī adhaḥ 
me pātu || oṃ siṃhāhastadvayaṃmepātu || oṃ parahaṃsokṣiyugmam me pātu || 
oṃ divyamahiṣārūḍhoyamaḥ padadvayaṃmepātu || oṃ 
maheśaścaṇḍikāsahitaḥ sarvāṅgānimepātu || itiṣaṣṭhanyāsaḥ ||

atha saptama bījamantra nyāsaḥ ||

vinyaset saptamenyāse kṛte rogakṣayo bhavet ||

oṃ aiṃ namaḥ śikhāyām || oṃ hrīṃ namo dakṣanetre || oṃ klīṃ namo 
vāmanetre || oṃ cāṃ namo dakṣakarṇe || oṃ muṃ namo vāmakarṇe || oṃ ḍāṃ 
namo dakṣanāsāpuṭe || oṃ yaiṃ namo vāma nāsāpuṭe || oṃ viṃ namo mukhe || 
oṃ cceṃ namo gude || iti bījanyāsaḥ saptamaḥ ||

athāṣṭama viloma bījanyāsaḥ ||

kṛtesminnaṣṭame nyāse sarva duḥkhaṃ vinaśyati ||

oṃcceṃ namo gude || oṃ viṃ namo mukhe || oṃ yaiṃ namo vāma nāsāpuṭe || 
oṃ ḍāṃ namo dakṣanāsāpuṭe ||

p. 208)

oṃ muṃ namo vāma karṇe || oṃ cāṃ namo dakṣa karṇe || oṃ klīṃ namo vāma 
netre || oṃ hrīṃ namo dakṣa netre || oṃ aiṃ namaḥ śikhāyām || iti aṣṭamaḥ 
nyāsaḥ ||

kurvīta navamaṃ nyāsaṃ mantra vyāpti svarūpakam ||

oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ klīṃ cāmuṇḍāyai vicce namaḥ mastakāccaraṇaparyantaṃ 
pūrvāṅge || oṃ mūlaṃ 9 namaḥ mastakāccaraṇāvadhi dakṣiṇāṅge || oṃ 
mūlaṃ 9 namaḥ mastakāccaraṇāvadhi pṛṣṭhe || oṃ mūlaṃ 9 namaḥ 
mastakāccaraṇāvadhi vāmāṅge || oṃ mūlaṃ 9 namaḥ mastakātpādāntam || 
oṃ mūlaṃ 9 namaḥ pādādi śirontam iti navamonyāsaḥ ||

atha daśama ṣaḍaṅga nyāsaḥ 

kṛtesmindaśame nyāse trailokyaṃ vaśagaṃ bhavet ||

oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ klīṃ cāmuṇḍāyaivicce namaḥ hṛdayāya namaḥ || oṃ mūlaṃ 9 
namaḥ śirase svāhā || oṃ mūlaṃ 9 namaḥ śikhāyaivaṣaṭ || oṃ mūlaṃ 9 
namaḥ kavacāya hum || oṃ mūlaṃ 9 namaḥ netratrayāya vauṣaṭ || oṃ mūlaṃ 
9 namaḥ astrāya phaṭ || daśamaṣaḍaṃga nyāsaḥ ||

p. 209)

atha ekādaśanyāsaḥ ||

oṃ khaḍginī śūlinī ghorā gadinī cakriṇī tathā |
śaṃkhinī cāpinī bāṇa bhuśuṇḍī parighāyudhā || 1 ||

saumyā saumyatarāśeṣasaumyebhyastvatisundarī |
parāparāṇāṃ paramātvameva parameśvarī || 2 ||

yacca kiñcitkvacidvastu sadasadvākhilātmike |
tasya sarvasya yā śaktiḥ sātvaṃ kiṃ stūyase mayā || 3 ||

yayā tvayājagatsraṣṭā jagatpātāttiyo jagat |
sopi nidrāvaśaṃ nītaḥ kastvāṃ stotumiheśvaraḥ || 4 ||

viṣṇuḥ śarīra grahaṇamahamīśāna eva ca |
kāritāste yato'tastvāṃ kaḥ stotuṃ śaktimānbhavet || 5 ||

ādyaṃ aiṃ bījaṃ kṛṣṇataraṃdhyātvā sarvāṃge vinyasāmi ||

oṃ śūlenapāhinodevi pāhi khaḍgena cāmbike |
ghaṃṭā svanena naḥ pāhi cāpajyāniḥ svanena ca || 1 ||

prācyāṃ rakṣa pratīcyāṃ ca caṇḍike rakṣa dakṣiṇe |
bhrāmaṇenātmaśūlasya uttarasyāṃ tatheśvari ! || 2 ||

saumyāni yāni rūpāṇi trailokye vicarantite |
yānicātyartha ghorāṇitairakṣāsmāṃstathā bhuvam || 3 ||

khaḍga śūla gadādīni yāni cāstrāṇi tembike |
karapallava saṃgīni tairasmānrakṣasarvataḥ || 4 ||

p. 210)

dvitīyaṃ hrīṃ bījaṃ sūrya sadṛśaṃ dhyātvā sarva (pṛṣṭha) 
tonyaset ||

oṃ sarvasvarūpe sarveśe sarvaśakti samanvite |
bhayebhyastrāhino devi durge ! devi namostute || 1 ||

etatte vadanaṃ saumyaṃ locanatraya bhūṣitam |
pātunaḥ sarva bhūtebhyaḥ kātyāyani ! namostute || 2 ||

jvālākarālamatyugramaśeṣāsurasūdanam |
triśūlaṃpātutobhīterbhadrakālī namostute || 3 ||

hinastidaityatejāṃsi svanenāpūryayājagat |
sā ghaṇṭā pātuno devi ! pāpebhyo naḥsutāniva || 4 ||

asurāsṛgvasā paṃkacarcitaste karojvalaḥ |
śubhāya khaḍgo bhavatu caṇḍike ! tvāṃ natāvayam || 5 ||

tṛtīyaṃ klīṃ bījaṃ sphaṭikābhaṃ dhyātāsarvāṃge vinyaset ||

mūlaṣaḍaṃganyāsaḥ 

oṃ aiṃ aṃguṣṭhābhyāṃ (hṛdayāya) namaḥ || oṃ hrīṃ 
tarjanībhyāṃ namaḥ (śirase svāhā) || oṃ klīṃ madhyamābhyāṃ 
namaḥ (śikhāyaivaṣaṭ) || oṃ cāmuṇḍāyai anāmikābhyāṃ namaḥ 
(kavacāyabhuṃ) || oṃ vicce kaniṣṭhakābhyāṃ namaḥ 
(netrābhyāṃvauṣaṭ) || oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ klīṃ cāmuṇḍāyai vicce 
karatalapṛṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ (astrāyaphaṭ) || evaṃ 
vinyasyāṣṭavāraṃ mūlena vyāpakaṃ kuryāt ||

p. 211)

athākṣara nyāsaḥ tantrāntare 

oṃ aiṃ namaḥ śirasi || oṃ hrīṃ namaḥ netrayoḥ || oṃ klīṃ namaḥ lalāṭe || 
cāṃ namaḥ bhruvoḥ || oṃ muṃ namaḥ karṇayoḥ || oṃ ḍāṃ namaḥ 
gaṇḍayoḥ || oṃ yaiṃ namaḥ mukhe (vadane) || oṃ viṃ namaḥ 
dantapaṃktyoḥ || oṃ cceṃ namaḥ jihvāyāṃ || oṃ aiṃ namaḥ skandhayoḥ || 
oṃ hrīṃ namaḥ kaṇṭhe || oṃ klīṃ namaḥ bhujayoḥ || oṃ cāṃ namaḥ hṛdi || 
oṃ muṃ namaḥ pārśvayoḥ || oṃ ḍāṃ namaḥ pṛṣṭhe || oṃ yaiṃ namaḥ 
nābhau || oṃ viṃ namaḥ liṃge || oṃ cceṃ namaḥ kaṭyoḥ || mū0 oṃ 9 namaḥ 
guhye || mū0 oṃ 9 namaḥ karayoḥ || mū0 oṃ 9 namaḥ pādayoḥ || mū0 oṃ 
namaḥ sarvāṃge ||

atha diṅnyāsaḥ 

oṃ aiṃ prācyai namaḥ || oṃ aiṃ āgneyyai namaḥ || oṃ hrīṃ dakṣiṇāyai 
namaḥ || oṃ hrīṃ nair-ṛtyai namaḥ || oṃ klīṃ pratīcyai namaḥ || oṃ 
klīṃ vāyavyai namaḥ || oṃ cāmuṇḍāyai udīcyai namaḥ || oṃ vicce īśānye 
namaḥ || oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ klīṃ cāmuṇḍāyai vicce ūrdhvāyai namaḥ || oṃ aiṃ 
hrīṃ klīṃ cāmuṇḍāyai vicce bhūmyai namaḥ || mānasopacāraiḥ saṃpūjya ||

p. 212)

atha dhyānam ||

śaṃkhaṃ cakraṃ gadāṃ vāṇānpāśaṃ parigha śūlake |
bhuśuṇḍīṃ ca śiraḥ khaḍgaṃ dadhatīṃ daśavaktrakām || 1 ||

tāmasīṃ siddhidāṃnaumi mahākālīṃ daśāṃghrikām |
mālāṃ ca paraśuṃ vāṇān gadāṃ kuliśa mevaca || 2 ||

padmaṃ dhanuḥ kuṇḍikāṃ ca daṃḍaṃ śaktimasiṃ tathā |
kheṭakaṃ jalajaṃ ghaṃṭāṃ surāpātraṃ ca śūlakam || 3 ||

pāśaṃ sudarśanaṃ caiva dadhatīṃ lohita prabhām |
padmasthitāṃmahālakṣmīṃ bhaje mahiṣamardinīm || 4 ||

p. 213)

p. 214)

evaṃ pratiṣṭhitāyāṃ tu anyenaivajapenmanum |

anyatrāpi ||

anya mantraja vā viddhānakāryākarhicidbudhaiḥ ||

evaṃ sarva parijñāya mālāyāṃ japamārabhet ||

nityaṃ japa kare kuryānnatu kāmyaṃ kadācana |
kāmyamapi kare kuryānmālābhāve ca sundari ! ||

vidyuddāmaṃsamaprabhāṃmṛgapati skandhasthitāṃbhīṣaṇām || 
kanyābhiḥ karavāla kheṭa vilasaddhastābhirāsevitām || 
hastaiścakragadāsikheṭa viśikhāṃścāpaṃ guṇaṃ tarjanīm || 
bibhrāṇāmanalātmikāṃ śaśidharāṃ durgāṃ trinetrāṃ bhaje || 1 ||

itidhyātvāmānasopacāraiḥ sampūjya gurudevatātmaikyaṃ vibhāvya pāṭhaṃ 
(japaṃ) kuryāt ||

1. atha vakṣyamāṇa kāmyaprayogopayogī saṃpuṭa vyavasthā | 
yathā pārvati praśnaḥ || 

devyuvāca || 

saṃpuṭaṃ katidhā svāmin vettumicchāmi tatvataḥ | 
kathayasvasureśāna ! yadyahaṃ tava vallabhā || 1 ||

īśvarovāca ||

saṃpuṭaṃ dvividhaṃ jñeyamudayāstakaraṃ priye ! |
śrṇūdayaṃ tvamatrādaupaścādastaṃ vadāmite || 2 ||

maṃtramādaupunaḥ ślokamaṃtemaṃtraṃ punaḥ paṭhet |
punarmaṃtraṃpunaḥ ślokaṃ kramo'yamudaye śubhaḥ || 3 ||

udayotkarṣa lābhāya saṃpuṭoyamudāhṛtaḥ |
atra sarvatra ślokamaṃtropalakṣakamiti ||
astaṃ cikitsā śāstreṣu śarāvābhyāṃ kṛtaṃ bhavet ||
tattehaṃ pravadāmyatra ekāgrakṛtamānasaḥ || 4 ||

maṃtramādau punaḥ ślokamaṃte maṃtra viparyayaṃ |
māraṇoccāṭanebaṃdhesaṃpuṭoyamudāhṛtaḥ ||

prakāroyamanādṛtya kurvantyātma prakalpitaṃ |
rauravādiṣu pacyante yāvadābhūtasaṃplavaḥ || iti marīcikalpe ||

p. 221)

prathama adhyāyaḥ

oṃ atrādya vartamāna kāle caṃḍīśaptaśatī ādya caritrasya brahmā ṛṣiḥ 
mahākālī devatā gāyatrīchandaḥ 

caṇḍī pāṭhaḥ ṣaṣṭha saṃvādaḥ

medhāśca kathayāmāsa surathāya samādhaye |
sā kathā kathitā paścānmārkaṇeyena bhāguroḥ ||

bhāmerakathayāmāsuḥ pakṣiṇo jaminiṃ prati |
anenaiva vidhānena kathāḥ ṣaḍ vidhikā matāḥ ||

p. 222)

nandajā śaktiḥ raktadantikābījaṃ agnistattvaṃ ṛgveda svarūpaṃ 
śrīmahākālīprasādāt ātmano'bhīṣṭaphala prāpti hetave dharmārtha kāma 
mokṣārthre prathama caritra pāṭhe (havane) viniyogaḥ ||

p. 223)

athā māhātmya nyāsaḥ ||

oṃ madhukaiṭabha badha māhātmyāya namaḥ brahmarandhre || oṃ 
mahiṣāsura sainya badha māhātmyāya namaḥ sīmante || oṃ mahiṣāsura badha 
māhātmyāya namaḥ bhruvormadhye || oṃ śakrādi māhātmyāya namaḥ 
netrayoḥ || oṃ devyā dūta saṃvāda māhātmyāya namaḥ mukhe || oṃ 
dhūmralocana badha māhātmyāya namaḥ karṇayoḥ || oṃ caṇḍa muṇḍa badha 
māhātmyāya namaḥ hṛdi || oṃ rakta bīja badha māhātmyāya namaḥ 
nābhau || oṃ niśumbha badha māhātmyāya namaḥ liṅge || oṃ śumbha 
badha māhātmyāya namaḥ mūlādhāre || oṃ stuti māhātmyāya namaḥ 
jānvoḥ || oṃ phala māhātmyāya namaḥ gulphayoḥ || oṃ varadāna 
māhātmyāya namaḥ pādayoḥ ||

p. 224)

anantara

hrīṃ māyā bījena sapta bāraṃ vyāpakaṃ nyāsaḥ ||

atha mahākālī dhyānam ||

oṃ bhīmāṃ bhīmogradaṃṣṭrāñjanagiri vilasattulyakāntiṃ daśāsyāṃ 
triṃśallolākṣimālāṃ daśa lulitabhujāṃ paṃkti pādāṃstathaiva || śūlaṃ 
vāṇaṃ gadāṃ vai dhanurathadadhatīṃ śaṃkha cakre bhuśuṃḍīṃ vande 
kālīṃ karāgraiḥ parighamasi yutaṃtāmasīṃ śīrṣakañca || iti dhyātvā ||

p. 225)

atha prathamādhyāya prārambhaḥ ||

oṃ namaścaṇḍikāyai || oṃ aiṃ mārkaṇḍeya uvāca [udaya saṃpuṭasya 
lakṣaṇaṃ || oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ klīṃ cāmuṇḍāya vicce oṃ aiṃ mārkaṇḍeya uvāca aiṃ 
hrīṃ klīṃ cāmuṇḍāyai vicce namaḥ || oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ klīṃ cāmuṇḍāyai vicce 
sāvarṇiḥ sūrya tanayo yomanuḥ kathyate'ṣṭamaḥ || niśāmaya tadutpattiṃ 
vistārādgadatomama aiṃ hrīṃ klīṃ cāmuṇḍāyai vicce amaḥ || asta sampuṭa || huṁ 
phaṭ oṃ aiṃ mārkaṇḍeya uvāca phaṭ huṁ || huṁ phaṭ sāvarṇiḥ sūryatanayo 
yomanuḥ kathyateṣṭamaḥ || niśāmaya tadutpattiṃ vistarādgadato mamaphaṭhum ||
] || 1 || 

sāvarṇiḥ sūryatanayo yo manuḥ kathyate'ṣṭamaḥ |
niśāmaya tadutpattiṃ vistarādgadatomama || 2 ||

mahāmāyānubhāvena yathā manvantarādhipaḥ |

p. 226)

sa bhabhūva mahābhāgaḥ sāvarṇistanayo raveḥ || 3 ||

svārociṣe'ntare pūrvaṃ caitravaṃśasamudbhavaḥ |
suratho nāma rājā bhūtsamaste kṣitimaṇḍale || 4 ||

tasya pālayataḥ samyk prajāḥ putrānivaurasān |
babhūvuḥ śatravo bhūpāḥ kolāvidhvaṃsinastadā || 5 ||

tasya tairabhavadyuddhamaṃtiprabaladaṇḍinaḥ |

p. 227)

nyūnairapi sa tairyuddhe kolāvidhvaṃsibhirjitaḥ || 6 ||

tataḥ svapuramāyāto nijadeśādhipo'bhavat |
ākrāntaḥ sa mahābhāgastaistadā prabalāribhiḥ || 7 ||

amātyairvalibhiduṣṭairdurbalasya durātmabhiḥ |
kośā balaṃ cāpahṛtaṃ tatrāpi svapure tataḥ || 8 ||

tato mṛgayāvyājena hṛtasvāmyaḥ sa bhūpatiḥ |
ekākī hayamāruhya jagāma gahanaṃ vanam || 9 ||

sa tatrāśramamadrākṣīd dvijavaryasya medhasaḥ |

p. 228)

praśāntaśvāpadākīrṇaṃ muniśiṣyopaśobhitam || 10 ||

tasthau kañcitsa kālaṃ ca muninā tena satkṛtaḥ |
itaścataśca vicaraṁstasminmunirāśrame || 11 ||

so'cintayattadā tatra mamatvākṛṣṭacetanaḥ |
matpūrvaiḥ pālitaṃ pūrvaṃmayāhīnaṃ puraṃ hi tat || 12 ||

madbhṛtyaistairasadvṛttairdharmataḥ pālyate na vā |
na jāne sapradhāno me śūrahastī sadāmadaḥ || 13 ||

mama vairivaśaṃ yātaḥ kānbhogānupalapsyate |

saptaślokī durgā prāraṃbhyate ||

śiva uvāca ||

devi tvaṃ bhaktasulabhe savakāryavidhāyini |
kalau hi kāryasiddhyarthamupāyaṃ brūhiyatnataḥ ||

devyuvāca ||

śṛṇudeva ! pravakṣyāmi kalau sarveṣṭasādhanam |

p. 229)

ya mamānugatatā nityaṃ prasādadhanabhojanaiḥ || 14 ||

anuvṛttiṃ dhruvaṃte'dya kurvaṃtyanyamahībhṛtām |
asamyagvyayaśīlaistaiḥ kurvadbhiḥsatataṃ vyayam || 15 ||

sañcitaḥ so'tiduḥkhena kṣayaṃ kośo gamiṣyati |
etaccānyacca satataṃ cintayāmāsa pārthivaḥ || 16 ||

tatra viprāśramobhyāse vaiśyamekaṃ dadarśasaḥ |
sa pṛṣṭastena kastvaṃ bho hetuścāgamane'tra kaḥ || 17 ||

saśoka iva kasmāttvaṃ durmanā iva lakṣyase |
ityākarṇya vacastasya bhūpateḥ praṇayoditam [praṇato pā] || 18 ||

mayātavaivasnehenāpyambāstutiprakāśyate |

oṃ asya śrīdurgā saptaślokī stotramaṃtrasya nārāyaṇa ṛṣiḥ anuṣṭupchandaḥ 
śrī mahākālī mahālakṣmī mahāsarasvatyo devatāḥ durgāprītyarthaṃ saptaślokī 
durgā pāṭhe viniyogaḥ ||

oṃ jñānināmāpi cetāṃsi devi bhagavatī hi sā |
balādākṛṣyamohāya mahāmāyāprayacchati || 1 ||

durgesmṛtāharasi bhītimaśeṣajantoḥ svasthaḥ smṛtāmatimatīva śubhāṃ 
dadāsi |
dāridryaduḥkhabhayahāriṇi kā tvadanyā sarvopakārakaraṇāyasadārdracittā || 2 ||

p. 230)

pratyuvāca sa taṃ vaiśyaḥ praśrayāvanato nṛpam || 19 ||

vaiśya uvāca || 20 ||

samādhirnāma vaiśyo'hamutpanno dhanināṃ kule || 21 ||

putradārairnirastaśca dhanalobhādasādhubhiḥ |
vihīnaśca dhanairdāraiḥ ;vihīnaḥsvajanairdāraiḥ pā0] putrairādāya me 
dhanam || 22 ||

vanamabhyāgato duḥkhī nirastaścāptabandhubhiḥ |
so'haṃ na vedmi putrāṇāṃ kuśalākuśalātmikām || 23 ||

pravṛttiṃ svajanānāṃca dārāṇāṃ cātra saṃsthitaḥ |

sarvamaṃgalamaṃgalye śive sarvārthasādhike |
śaraṇye tryambake gauri nārāyaṇi namostu te || 3 ||

śaraṇāgatadīnārta paritrāṇaparāyaṇe |
sarvasyārtihare devi nārāyaṇi namostute || 4 ||

sarvasvarūpe sarveśe sarvaśaktisamanvite |
bhayebhyastrāhino devi durgedevi namostu te || 5 ||

rogānaśeṣānapahaṃsituṣṭā ruṣṭā tu kāmānsakalānabhīṣṭān |

p. 231)

kiṃ nu teṣāṃ gṛhe kṣemamakṣemaṃ kiṃ nu sāmpratam || 24 ||

kathaṃ te kiṃ nu sadvṛttaḥ durvṛttāḥ kiṃ nu me sutāḥ || 25 ||

rājovāca || 26 ||

yairnirasto bhavāṁllubdhaiḥ putradārādibhirdhanaiḥ || 27 ||

teṣu kiṃ bhavataḥ sneha manubadhnāti mānasam || 28 ||

vaiśya uvāca || 29 ||

evametadyathā prāha bhavānasmadgataṃ vacaḥ || 30 ||

kiṃ karomi na badhnāti mama niṣṭhuratāṃ manaḥ |
yaiḥ santyajya pitṛsnehaṃ dhanalubdhairnirākṛtaḥ || 31 ||

tvāmāśritānāṃ na vipannarāṇāṃ tvāmāśritāhyāśrayatāṃ prayānti || 6 ||

sarvābādhāpraśamanaṃ trailokyasyākhileśvari ! |
evameva tvayā kāryamasmadvairivināśanam || 7 ||

etatparama guhyaṃ sarva rakṣā viśāradam |
devyā saṃbhāṣitaṃ stotraṃ sadāsāmrājyadāyakam || 8 ||

śṛṇuyā dvāpaṭhe dvāpipāṭhayedvāpiyatnataḥ parivāra yotobhūtvā trailokya 
vijayī bhavet || iti śrī saptaślokī durgā samāptā ||

atha caṇḍikā dala prārambhaḥ ||

oṃ namaścaṇḍikāyai || 

athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi caṇḍikā dala muttamam |

p. 232)

patisvajanahārdaṃ ca hārditeṣveva me manaḥ |
kimetannābhijānāmi jānanapi mahāmate || 32 ||

yatpremapravaṇaṃ cittaṃ viguṇeṣvapi bandhuṣu |
teṣāṃ kṛte me niḥśvāso daurmanasyaṃ ca jāyate || 33 ||

karomi kiṃ yanna manasteṣvaprītiṣuniṣṭhuram || 34 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || 35 ||

tatastau sahitau vipra taṃ muniṃ samupasthitau || 36 ||

mantraṃ vinā tu japtvā vaitatsarvaṃ niṣphalaṃ bhavet || 1 ||

oṃ namo bhagavati jaya jaya cāmuṇḍe caṇḍi caṇḍeśvari caṇḍāyudhe || 
caṇḍarūpadhāriṇi tāṇḍava priye || kuṇḍalībhūtadiṅ nāgamaṇḍalī 
kṛtagaṇḍasthale samastajagadaṇḍasaṃhāra kāriṇi pare anantānantarūpe | śive 
naraśiromālālaṃkṛta vakṣasthale mahākapāla bhālojvalanmaṇi mukuṭa 
cūḍāvataṃsa candra khaṇḍe mahābhīṣaṇe devi || 
mahāmāyeṣoḍaśakalāparivṛtollāsitemahādevāsurasamaranihatarudhirārdrīkṛt

yajñopavītini || mahāśmaśāna bhasmoddhūlita sarvagātre || kāli || kaṃkāli 
mahākāli kālāgnirudrakāli || 

p. 233)

samādhirnāma vaiśyo'sau sa ca pārthivasattamaḥ |
kṛtvā tu tau yathānyāyaṃ yathārhaṃ tena saṃvidam || 37 ||

upaviṣṭau kathāḥ kāściccakraturvaiśyapārthivau || 38 ||

rājovāca || 39 ||

bhagavaṃstvāmahaṃ praṣṭumicchāmyekaṃ vadasva tat || 40 ||

duḥkhāya yanme manasaḥ svacittāyattattāṃ vinā |

kāla saṃkarṣiṇi || kālanāśini kālarātrinamoduṣṭabhakṣiṇi || nānābhūtapreta 
piśācagaṇa sahasra saṃcāriṇi nānā vyādhi praśamani || sarva duṣṭa pramathini 
sarva dāridrya nāśini dhaga dhagetya svādita māṃsakhaṇḍe | gātra vikṣepa kala 
kalāyamānakaṃkāladhāriṇi madhu māṃsa rudhirāvasikta vilāsini sakala surāsura 
gandharvaṃ yakṣa vidyādhara kinnara kimpuruṣādibhiḥ stūyamāna carite 
sarvamantrādhikāriṇi sarvaśakti pradhāne || sakalaloka pāvani || sakala durita 
prakṣālini || sakalalokaijanani || brāhmi || māheśvari || kaumāri ! vaiṣṇavi | 
śaṃkhini | vārāhi | nārasiṃhīndrāṇi || cāmuṇḍe mahālakṣmī svarūpe || 
mahāvidye || yogeśvari yogini || caṇḍike || mahāmāye || viśvarūpiṇi || 
sarvābharaṇabhūṣite || atala vitala sutala rasātala talātala mahātala pātālādi 
bhūrbhuvaḥ svarmaharjanastapaḥ satyākhya caturdaśabhuvanaikanāthe mahākrūre 
prasannarūpadhāriṇi ||

p. 234)

mamatvaṃ gatarājyasya [saptāṅga kāmandakīye || svāmyamātyaśca

||

kailāsa śikharāsīnaṃ śaṃkaraṃ varadaṃ śivaṃ |
devī papraccha sarvajña devadevaṃ maheśvaram || 1 ||

devyuvāca ||

bhagavandeva deveśa devanāṃ mokṣada prabho |
prabrūhime mahābhāga gopyaṃ yadyapi ca prabho || 2 ||

śatrūṇāṃ yena nāśaḥ syādātmano rakṣaṇaṃ bhavet |
paramaiśvarya matulaṃ labhedyena hitaṃ vada || 3 ||

bhairava uvāca ||

vakṣyāmite mahādevi sarvadharmahitāyaca |
adbhutaṃ kavaca devyāssarva rakṣākaraṃ nṛṇām || 4 ||

sarvāriṣṭa praśamana sarvopadravanāśanam |
sukhadaṃ bhogadaṃ caiva vaśyākarṣaṇamadbhutam || 5 ||

śatrūṇāṃ saṃkṣayakaraṃ sarva vyādhinivāraṇam |
duḥkhinojvariṇaścaivasvābhīṣṭaprahatāstathā || 6 ||

bhogamokṣa pradaṃ caiva kālikā kavaca paṭhet |
śatrava stasya naśyanti ......... oṃ asyaśrīkālikā kavacasya śrībhairava##-
|| oṃ dhyāyetkālīṃ mahāmāyāṃ trinetrāṃ bahurūpiṇīm ||

p. 259)

so'pi kruddho dhutasaṭo devyā vāhanakesarī || 51 ||

caturbhujāṃ lalajjihvāṃ pūrṇacaṃdranibhānanāṃ |
nīlotpaladalaprakhyāṃ śatrusaṃghavidāriṇīm || 8 ||

naramuṇḍaṃ tathā khaḍgaṃkamala ca varaṃtathā |
bibhrāṇāṃ raktavasanāṃdaṃṣṭyālīṃghorarūpiṇīm || 9 ||

aṭṭāṭṭahāsa niratāṃsarvadā ca digambarām |
śavāsanasthitāṃ devī muṇḍamālā vibhūṣitām || 10 ||

iti dhyātvā mahādevīṃ punastu kavacaṃpaṭhet ||
oṃ kālikā ghorarūpāḍhyā sarvakāmapradāśubhā || 11 ||

sarvadevastutādevī śatrunāśaṃ karotu me |
hrīṃ hrīṃ svarūpiṇīcaiva hrīṃ hrīṃ hūṁ rūpiṇī tathā || 12 ||

hrīṃ hrīṃ kṣeṃ 2 svarūpā sā sadāśatrūnvidārayet |
śrīṃ hrīṃ aiṃ rūpiṇīdevī bhavabaṃdhavimocanī || 13 ||

hasakla hrīṃ hrīṃ ripūnsāharatudevī sarvadā |
yayāśumbho hato daityo niśumbhaśca mahāsuraḥ || 14 ||

vairināśāya vaṃde tāṃ kālikāṃ śaṃkarapriyām |
brāhmī śaivī vaiṣṇavī ca vārāhī nārasiṃhikā || 15 ||

kaumāryaindrī ca cāmuṇḍā khādyantu mamadviṣaḥ |
sureśvarī ghorarūpāḥ caṇḍamuṇḍa vināśinī || 16 ||

muṇḍamālāvṛtāṃgī ca sarvataḥ pātumām sadā |
hrīṃ hrīṃ kālike ghoradaṃṣṭre rudhira priye rudhira pūrṇa vaktre 

p. 260)

nāśayanto'suragaṇāndevīśaktyupabṛṃhitāḥ |

hastaṃ datvātad hṛdaye kavacaṃ tu svayaṃ paṭhet |

hanyādastra prahāreṇa śatrugacchedyamālayam |
jvaladaṃgāra tāpena bhavantijvariṇo'rayaḥ || 23 ||

iti rudrayāmale kālīkavacaṃ sampūrṇam ||

p. 262)

tāntrika āhuti ||

atha tṛtīyodhyāyaḥ 

atha dhyānam ||

oṃ uśradbhānusahasrakāntimaruṇakṣaumāṃ śiromālikāṃ 
raktāliptapayodharāṃ japavaṭīṃ vidyāmabhītiṃ varām ||

ṛiruvāca || 1 ||

oṃ nihanyamānaṃ tatsainyamavalokya mahāsuraḥ |
senānīścikṣuraḥ kopādyayau yoddhumathāmbikām || 2 ||

p. 265)

sa devīṃ śaravarṣeṇa vavarṣa samare'suraḥ |
yathā merugireḥ śṛṅgaṃ toyavarṣeṇa toyadaḥ || 3 ||

tasyacchitvā tato devī līlayaiva śarotkarān |
jaghāna turagānbāṇairyantāraṃ caiva vājinām || 4 ||

ciccheda ca dhanuḥ sadyo dhvajaṃ cātisamucchritam |
vivyādha caiva gātreṣu chinnadhanvānamāśugaiḥ || 5 ||

sacchinnadhanvā viratho hatāśvo hatasārathiḥ |
abhyadhāvata tāṃ devīṃ khaḍgacarmadharo'suraḥ || 6 ||

p. 266)

siṃhamāhatya khaḍgena tīkṣṇadhāreṇa mūrdhani |
ājaghāna bhuje savye devīmapyativegavān || 7 ||

tasyāḥ khaḍgo bhujaṃ prāpya paphāla nṛpanandana |
tato jagrāha śūlaṃ sa kopādaruṇalocanaḥ || 8 ||

cikṣepa ca tatastattu bhadrakālyāṃ mahāsuraḥ |
jājvalyamānaṃ tejobhīravibimbamivāmbarāt || 9 ||

dṛṣṭavātadāpatacchūlaṃdevīśūlamamuñcata |
tacchūlaṃ śatadhā tena nītaṃ sa ca mahāsuraḥ || 10 ||

hate tasminmahāvīrye mahiṣasya camūpatau |

p. 267)

ājagāma gajārūḍhaścāmarastridaśārdanaḥ || 11 ||

so'pi śaktiṃ mumocātha devyāstāmambikā drutam |
huṅkārābhihatāṃ bhūmau pātayāmāsa niṣprabhām || 12 ||

bhagnāṃ śaktiṃ nipatitāṃ dṛṣṭvā krodhasamanvitaḥ |
cikṣepa cāmaraḥ śūlaṃ bāṇaistadapi sācchinat || 13 ||

tataḥ siṃhaḥ samutpatya gajakumbhāntaresthitaḥ |
bāhuyuddhena yuyudhe tenoccaistridaśāriṇā || 14 ||

yuddhyamānau tatastau tu tasmānnāgānmahīṃ gatau |
yuyudhātetisaṃrabdhau prahārairatidāruṇaiḥ || 15 ||

tato vegātkhamutpatya nipatya ca mṛgāriṇā |
karaprahāreṇa śiraścāmarasya pṛthakkṛtam || 16 ||

p. 268)

udagraśca raṇe devyā śilāvṛkṣādibhirhataḥ |

ugrāsyamugravīryaṃ ca tathaiva ca mahāhanum |

biḍālasyāsinā kāyātpātayāmāsa vai śiraḥ |
durdharaṃ durmukhaṃ cobhau śarairninye yamakṣayam || 20 ||

evaṃ saṃkṣīyamāṇe tu svasainye mahiṣāsuraḥ |
māhiṣeṇa svarūpeṇa trāsayāmāsa tāngaṇān || 21 ||

p. 269)

kāṁścittuṇḍaprahāreṇa khurakṣepaistathāparān |

niḥśvāsapavanenānyānpātayāmāsa bhūtale || 23 ||

so'pi kopānmahāvīryaḥ khurakṣuṇṇamahītalaḥ |

dhutaśṛṅgavibhinnāścakhaṇḍa khaṇḍaṃ yayurghanāḥ |

p. 270)

iti krodha samāghmātamāpatantaṃ mahāsuram |
dṛṣṭvā sā caṇḍikā kopaṃ tadbadhāya tadākarot || 28 ||

sā kṣiptvā tasya vai pāśaṃ taṃ babaṃdha mahāsuram |

tataḥ siṃho'bhavatsadyo yāvattasyāmbikā śiraḥ |
chinatti tāvatpuruṣaḥ khaḍgapāṇiradṛśyata || 30 ||

tata evāśu puruṣaṃ devī ciccheda sāyakaiḥ |
taṃ khaḍgacarmaṇā sārddhaṃ tataḥ so'bhūnmahāgajaḥ || 31 ||

kareṇa ca mahāsiṃhaṃ taṃ cakarṣa jagarja ca |
karṣatastu karaṃ devī khaḍgena nirakṛntata || 32 ||

p. 271)

tato mahāsuro bhūyo māhiṣaṃ vapurāsthitaḥ [rāśritaḥ śā0] ||

||

caturtho'dhyāyaḥ ||

p. 274)

siṃhaskandhādhirūḍhāṃ tribhuvanamakhilaṃ tejasā pūrayantīṃ dhyāye

ṛṣiruvāca || 1 ||

oṃ śakrādayaḥ suragaṇā nihate'ti vīrye tasmindurātmani surārivale ca devyā |

p. 275)

devyā yayā tatamidaṃ jagadātmaśaktyā

p. 276)

kiṃ varṇayāmatava rūpamacintyametatkiñcātivīryamamurakṣayakāribhūri |

atha durgāśatanāma stotram ||

durgāyāḥ śatanāmāni śṛṇu tambhavagehini |
durgābhavānī deveśī viśvanāthapriyā śivā || 1 ||

ghoradaṃṣṭrākarālāsyā muṇḍamālā vibhūṣaṇā |
rudrāṇī tāriṇī tārā māheśī bhavavallabhā || 2 ||

nārāyaṇī jagaddhātrī mahādevapriyā jayā |
vijayā ca jayā'rādhyā śarvāṇī haravallabhā || 3 ||

asitā cāṇimā devī laghimā garimā tathā |
maheśaśaktirviśveśī gaurī parvatanandinī || 4 ||

nityā ca niṣkalaṃkā ca nirīhā nityanūtanā |
raktā raktamukhīvāṇī vasuyuktā vasupradā || 5 ||

rāmapriyā rāmaratā raghunāthavarapradā |
rājyeśvarī rājyaratā kṛṣṇā kṛṣṇavarapradā || 6 ||

yaśodā rādhikā caṇḍī draupadī rukmiṇī tathā |
guhapriyā guharatā guhavaṃśa vilāsinī || 7 ||

gaṇeśa jananī mātā viśvarūpā ca jāhnavī |
gaṅgā kālī ca kāśī ca bhairavī bhuvaneśvarī || 8 ||

nirmalā ca sugandhā ca devakī devapūjitā |
dakṣajā dakṣiṇā dakṣā dakṣayajñavināśinī || 9 ||

suśīlā sundarī saumyāmātaṃgī kamalā kalā |
niśumbhanāśinī śumbhanāśinī caṇḍanāśinī || 10 ||

dhūmralocanasaṃhartrī mahiṣāsuramardinī |
umā gaurī karālā ca kāminī viśvamohinī || 11 ||

ityevaṃ śatanāmāni kathitāni varānane |
nāmasmaraṇāmātreṇa jīvanmuktobhavennaraḥ || 12 ||

yaḥ paṭhet prātarutthāya smṛtvā durgāpadadvayam |
mucyate janmabandhebhyo nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 13 ||

sandhyākāle divābhāge niśāyāṃ vā niśāmukhe |

nirjitākhiladaityāriḥ sayadāha śṛṇuṣva tat || 107 ||

p. 301)

mama trailokyamakhilaṃ mama devā vaśānugāḥ |
yajñabhāgānahaṃ sarvānupāśnāmi pṛthak pṛthak || 108 ||

trailokye vararatnāni mama vaśyānyaśeṣataḥ |
tathaiva gaja ratnaṃ ca hṛtvā devendra vāhanam || 109 ||

kṣīrodamathanodbhūtamaśvaratnaṃ mamāmareḥ |
uccaiḥ śravasasaṃjñaṃ tatpraṇipatya satarpitam || 110 ||

yāni cānyāni deveṣugandharveṣūrageṣuca |
ratnabhūtānibhūtānitāni mayyeva śobhane || 111 ||

strīratnabhūtāṃ tvāṃ devi loke manyāmahe vayam |
sā tvamasmānupāgaccha yato ratnabhujo vayam || 112 ||

māṃ vā mamanujaṃ vāpi niśumbha muruvikramam |
bhaja tvaṃ caṃcalāpāṅgi ratnabhūtāsi vai yataḥ || 113)

p. 302)

paramaiśvaryamatulaṃ prāpsyase matparigrahāt |
etadbuddhyā samolocya matparigrahatāṃvraja || 114 ||

ṛṣiruvāca || 115 ||

ityuktvā sā tadā devī gambhīrā ntaḥ smitā jagau |
durgā bhagavatī bhadrā yayedaṃ dhāryate jagat || 116 ||

devyuvāca || 117 ||

satyamuktaṃ tvayā nātramithyā kiñcitvayoditam |
trailokyādhipatiḥ śumbho niśumbhaścāpi tādṛśaḥ || 118 ||

kiṃ tvatra yatpratijñātaṃ mithyā tatkriyate katham |
śrūyatāmalpabuddhitvātpratijñā yā kṛtā purā || 119 ||

yo māṃ jayati saṃgrāme yo me darpaṃ vyapohati |
yo me pratibalo loke sa me bhartā bhaviṣyati || 120 ||

p. 303)

tadāgacchatu śumbho'tra niśumbho vā mahāsuraḥ |
māṃ jitvā kiṃ cireṇātra pāṇiṃ gṛhṇātu me laghu || 121 ||

dūta uvāca || 122 ||

avaliptāsi maivaṃ tvaṃ devi brūhi mamāgrataḥ |
trailokye kaḥ pumāṃstiṣṭhedagre śumbhaniśumbhayoḥ || 123 ||

anyeṣāmapi daityānāṃ sarve devā na vai yughi |
tiṣṭhanti-sammukhe devi kiṃ punaḥ strī tvamekikā || 124 ||

indrādyāḥ sakalā devāstasthuryeṣāṃ na saṃyuge |
śumbhādīnāṃ kathaṃ teṣāṃ strī prayāsyasi saṃmukham || 125 ||

sā tvaṃ gaccha mayaivoktā pārśvaṃśumbhaniśumbhayoḥ |

p. 304)

keśākarṣaṇanirddhūtagauravāmā gamiṣyasi || 126 ||

devyuvāca || 127 ||

evametad balī śumbho niśambhaścātivīryavān |
kiṃ karomi pratijñā me yadanālocitāpurā || 128 ||

sa tvaṃ gaccha mayoktaṃ te yadetatsarvamādṛtaḥ |
tadācakṣvā'surendrāya sa ca yuktaṃ karotu yat oṃ || 129 ||

iti śrī mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe sāvarṇike manvantare devīmāhātmye devyādūta 
saṃvādo nāma pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ || 5 || uvāca 9 tripānmaṃtrāḥ 66 śloka 54 
evam 129 evamāditaḥ || 388 ||

oṃ jaya jaya mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇesāvarṇikemanvantare devī mahātmye satyāḥ santu 
(yajamānasya kāmāḥ) jagadambārpaṇamastu ||

tāntrika āhuti ||

klīṃ jayantī sāṃgāyai sāyudhāyai saśaktikāyai saparivārāyai savāhanāyai 
dhūmrākṣyai viṣṇumāyādi caturviṃśaddevatābhyo mahāhutiṃ samarpayāmi 
namaḥ svāhā ||


atha ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ

atha dhyānam ||

oṃ nāgādhīśvaraviṣṭarāṃ 
phaṇiphaṇottaṃsoruratnāvalīṃbhāsvaddehalatāṃ divākaranibhāṃ 
netratrayodbhāsitām |
mālākumbhakapālanīrajakarāṃ candrārdhacūḍāṃ parāṃ sarvajñeśvara 
bhairavāṅkanilayāṃ padmāvatīṃ cintaye || 5 ||

p. 306)

ṛṣiruvāca || 1 ||

oṃ ityākarṇya vacodevyāḥ sa dūto'marṣapūritaḥ |
samācaṣṭa samāgamya daityarājāya vistarāt || 2 ||

tasya dūtasya tadvākyamākarṇyāsurarāṭ tataḥ |
sakrodhaḥ prāha daityānāmadhipaṃ dhūmralocanam || 3 ||

he dhūmralocanāśu tvaṃ svasainyaparivāritaḥ |
tāmānaya balādduṣṭāṃ keśākarṣaṇavihvalām || 4 ||

tatparitrāṇadaḥ kaścidyadivottiṣṭhate'paraḥ |
sa hantavyo'maro vāpi yakṣo gandharva eva vā || 5 ||

ṛṣiruvāca || 6 ||

tenājñastataḥ śīghraṃ sa daityo dhūmralocanaḥ |
vṛtaḥ ṣaṣṭhyāsahasrāṇāmasurāṇāṃ drutaṃ yayau || 7 ||

sa dṛṣṭvā tāṃ tato devīṃ tuhinācalasaṃsthitām |

p. 307)

jagādoccaiḥ prayāhīti mūlaṃ śumbhaniśumbhayoḥ || 8 ||

na cetprītyādya bhavatī madbhartāramupaiṣyati |
tato balānnayāmyeṣa keśākarṣaṇavihvalām || 9 ||

devyuvāca || 10 ||

daityeśvareṇa prahito balavān balasaṃvṛtaḥ |
balānnayasi mā mevaṃ tataḥ kiṃ te karomyaham || 11 ||

ṛṣiruvāca || 12 ||

ityuktaḥ so'bhyadhāvattāmasuro dhūmralocanaḥ |
huṅkāreṇaiva taṃ bhasma sā cakārāmbikā tataḥ || 13 ||

atha kruddhaṃ mahāsainyamasurāṇāṃ tathāmbikā |
vavarṣa sāyakaistīkṣṇaistathā śaktiparaśvadhaiḥ || 14 ||

tato dhutasaṭaḥ kopātkṛtvā nādaṃ subhaivaram |
papātāsurasenāyāṃ siṃho devyāḥ svavāhanaḥ || 15 ||

p. 308)

kāṃścit karaprahāreṇadaityānāsyena cāparān |
[ākrāntyā0 ca] ākramya cādhareṇānyān sajaghāna mahāsurān || 16 ||

keṣāñcitpāṭayāmāsa nakhaiḥ koṣṭhāni kesarī |
tathā talaprahāreṇa śirāṃsi kṛtavān pṛthak || 17 ||

vicchinnabāhuśirasaḥ kṛtāstena tathāpara |
papauca rudhiraṃ koṣṭhādanyeṣāṃ dhutakesaraḥ || 18 ||

kṣaṇena tadbalaṃ sarvaṃ kṣayaṃ nītaṃ mahātmanā |
tena kesariṇā devyā vāhanenātikopinā || 19 ||

śrutvā tamasuraṃ devyā nihataṃ dhūmralocanam |
balañca [kṣapitaṃ0 śā0] kṣayitaṃ kṛtsnaṃ devī kesariṇā tataḥ || 
20 ||

cukopa daityādhipatiḥ śumbhaḥ - prasphuritādharaḥ |

p. 309)

ājñāpayāmāsa ca tau caṇḍa muṇḍau mahāsurau || 21 ||

he caṇḍa he muṇḍa balairbahulaiḥ parivāritau |
tatra gacchata gatvā ca sā samānīyatāṃ laghu || 22 ||

keśeṣvākṛṣya baddhvā vā yadivaḥ saṃśayo yudhi |
tadā śeṣāyudhaiḥsarvairasurairvinihanyatām || 23 ||

tasyāṃ hatāyāṃ duṣṭāyāṃ siṃhe ca vinipātite |
śīghramāgamyatāṃ baddhvā gṛhītvā tāmathāmbikām || oṃ || 24 ||

iti śrī mārkaṇḍeya purāṇe sāvarṇike manvantare

p. 310)

oṃ jaya jaya mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇesāvarṇikemanvantare devī māhātmye satyāḥ santu

atha saptamo'dhyāyaḥ 

atha dhyānam ||

p. 311)

ṛṣiruvāca || 1 ||

oṃ ājñaptāste tato daityāścaṇḍamuṇḍapurogamāḥ |

dadṛśuste tato devīmīṣaddhāsāṃ vyavasthitām |
siṃhasyopari śailendraśṛṅge mahati kāñcane || 3 ||

tataḥ kopaṃ cakāroccairambikā tānarīnprati |

kālī karālavadanā viniṣkrāntāsipāśinī || 6 ||

p. 312)

dvīpicarmaparīdhānā śuṣkamāṃsātibhairavā || 7 ||

ati vistāravadanā jihvālalana bhīṣaṇā |

p. 313)

tairmuktāni ca śastrāṇi mahāstrāṇi tathāsuraiḥ |

p. 314)

babhuryathārkabimbāni subahūni ghanodaram || 18 ||

p. 315)

p. 317)

adya sarvabalairdaityāḥ ṣaḍaśītirudāyudhāḥ |

p. 318)

p. 319)

p. 321)

tataḥ parivṛtastābhirīśāno devaśaktibhiḥ |
hanyantāmasurāḥ śīghraṃ mama prītyāha caṇḍikām || 22 ||

dūta tvaṃ gaccha bhagavanpārśvaṃ śumbha niśumbhayoḥ || 24 ||

brūhi śumbhaṃ niśumbhaṃ ca dānavāvatigarvitau |
ye cānye dānavāstatra yuddhāya samupasthitāḥ || 25 ||

trailokyamindro labhatāṃ devāḥ santu havirbhujaḥ |
yūyaṃ prayāta pātālaṃ yadi jīvitumicchatha || 26 ||

p. 322)

balāvalepādatha cedbhavanto yuddhakāṃkṣiṇaḥ |

yato niyuktodautyena tayā devyā śivaḥ svayam | [śivadūtī dhyānam ||

śivadūtīti sākhyātā caṇḍī pheruśatairvṛtā |
caturbhujāṃ mahākāyāṃ sindūra sadṛśa dyutim ||

raktadantāṃ muṇḍamālāṃ jaṭājūṭārdha candradhṛk |

p. 323)

sā ca tānprahitānbāṇañchūlaśaktiparaśvadhān |

pātavidāritān |

p. 324)

aindrī kuliśapātena śataśodaityadānavāḥ |

iti mātṛgaṇaṃ kruddhaṃ mardayantaṃ mahāsurān |

p. 325)

dṛṣṭvābhyupāyairvividhairneśurdevārisainikāḥ || 39 ||

p. 326)

p. 327)

p. 328)

p. 330)

vibhrāṇaminduśakalābharaṇāṃ trinetrāmardhāmbikeśamaniśaṃ

rājovāca || 1 ||

p. 331)

p. 332)

p. 336)

p. 339)

balābalepādduṣṭetvaṃ mādurge garvamāvaha |

p. 342)

sa muṣṭiṃ pātāyāmāsa hṛdaye daityapuṃgavaḥ |

p. 343)

tāmāyāntaṃ tato devī sarvadaityajaneśvaram |

p. 344)

jajvaluścāgnayaḥ śāntāḥ [śānta diglakṣaṇamāha varāhaḥ || aṅgāriṇī

dineśaḥ śeṣāścaśāntāḥ śubhadāśca tāḥ syuḥ ||] 
śāntadigjanitasvanāḥ oṃ || 32 ||

[kṣīrikā || (khīra) bhāvaprakāśe pūrva khaṇḍe kṛtānnavarge |

p. 346)

p. 347)

p. 351)

oṃ vidyuddāmasamaprabhāṃ mṛgapatiskandhasthitāṃ

devyuvāca || 1 ||

p. 360)

tasmānmamaitanmāhātmyaṃ paṭhitavyaṃ samāhitaḥ |

pāṭhaḥ ||

utpatatyakasmādityutpātoduḥkhaṃtasya traividhyaṃ

krodha lobha moherṣyā viṣāda darśana nibandhanam || sarvaṃ catadāntaropāya 
sādhyatvādādhyātmikaṃ ca || tatrādhibhautikaṃ mānuṣa paśu pakṣi sarīsṛpa 
sthāvara nimittaṃ || adhidaivikaṃ yakṣa rākṣasa vināyaka grahādinibandhanamiti ||
] hi tat || 7 ||

upasargānaśeṣāṃstu mahāmārīsamudbhavān |

p. 364)

ityuktvā sā bhagavatī caṇḍikā caṇḍavikramā || 32 ||

p. 365)

te'pi devā nirātaṅkāḥsvādhikārānyathā purā || 33 ||

p. 368)

tayā tvameṣa vaiśyaśca tathaivānye vivekinaḥ || 3 ||

khaḍgapātraśiraḥ kheṭairalaṃkṛtacaturbhujā |

p. 385)

kabandhahāraṃśirasā bibhrāṇāhi śiraḥ srajam || 9 ||

sā provāca mahālakṣmīṃtāmasīpramadottamā |
nāma karma ca me mātardehi tubhyaṃ namonamaḥ || 10 ||

tāṃ provāca mahālakṣmīstāmasīṃ pramadottamām |
dadāmi tava nāmāni yāni karmāṇi tāni te || 11 ||

mahāmāyā mahākālī mahāmārī kṣudhā tṛṣā |
nidrā tṛṣṇā caikavīrā kālarātrirduratyayā || 12 ||

imāni tava nāmāni pratipādyāni karmabhiḥ |
ebhiḥ karmāṇi te jñātvā yo'dhīte so'śnute sukham || 13 ||

p. 386)

tāmityuktvā mahālakṣmīḥ svarūpamaparaṃ nṛpa |
sattvākhyenātiśuddhenaguṇenenduprabhaṃdadhau || 14 ||

akṣamālāṃkuśadharā vīṇāpustakadhāriṇī |
sā babhūva varā nārī nāmānyasyai ca sā dadau || 15 ||

mahāvidyāmahāvāṇā bhāratī vāk sarasvatī |
āryā brāhmī kāmadhenurvedagarbhā ca dhīśvarī || 16 ||

athovāca mahālakṣmīrmahākālīṃ sarasvatīm |
yuvāṃ janayatāṃ devyau mithunesvānurūpataḥ || 17 ||

ityuktvāte mahālakṣmīḥ sasarja mithunaṃ svayam |
hiraṇyagarbhau rucirau strīpuṃsau kamalāsanau || 18 ||

brahmanvidheviriṃceti dhātarityāha taṃ naram |
śrīḥ padme kamale lakṣmītyāha mātā ca tāṃ striyam || 19 ||

p. 387)

mahākālī bhāratī ca mithune sṛjataḥ saha |
etayorapi rūpāṇi nāmāni ca vadāmi te || 20 ||

nīlakaṇṭhaṃ raktavāhuṃ śvetāṅgaṃ candraśekharam |
janayāmāsa puruṣaṃ mahākālī sitāṃ striyam || 21 ||

sa rudraḥ śaṅkaraḥ sthāṇuḥ kapardī ca trilocanaḥ |
trayī vidyā kāmadhenuḥ sā strī bhāṣākṣarā svarā || 22 ||

sarasvatī striyaṃ gaurīṃ kṛṣṇaṃ ca puruṣaṃ nṛpa |
najayāmāsa nāmāni tayorapi vadāmi te || 23 ||

viṣṇuḥ kṛṣṇo hṛṣīkeśo vāsudevo janārdanaḥ |
umā gaurī satī caṇḍī sundarī subhagā śivā || 24 ||

p. 388)

evaṃ yuvatayaḥ sadyaḥ puruṣatvaṃ prapedire |
cakṣuṣmanto nu paśyanti netare'tadvido janāḥ || 25 ||

brahmaṇe pradadau patnīṃ mahālakṣmīrnṛpatrayīm |
rudrāya gaurīṃ varadāṃ vāsudevāya ca śriyam || 26 ||

svarayā saha saṃbhūya virañcyo'ṇḍamajījanat |
bibheda bhagavān rudrastad gauryā saha vīryavān || 27 ||

aṇḍamadhye pradhānādi kāryajātamabhūnnṛpa |
mahā bhūtātmakaṃ sarvaṃjagatsthāvarajaṅgamam || 28 ||

pupoṣa pālayāmāsa tallakṣmyā saha keśavaḥ |
saṃjahāra jagatsarvaṃ saha gauryā maheśvaraḥ || 29 ||

p. 389)

mahālakṣmīrmahārāja sarvasatvamayīśvarī |
nirākārā ca sākārā saiva nānābhidhānabhṛt || 30 ||

nāmāntarairnirūpyaiṣā nāmnā nānyena kenacit oṃ || 31 ||

itiprādhānika rahasyaṃ sampūrṇam ||

atha vaikṛtirahasyārambhaḥ ||

ṛṣiruvāca ||

oṃ triguṇā tāmasī devī sāttvikī yā tridhoditā |
sā śarvā caṇḍikā durgā bhadrā bhagavatīryate || 1 ||

yoganidrā hareruktā mahākālī tamoguṇā |
madhukaiṭabha nāśārthaṃ yāṃ tuṣṭāvāmbujāsanaḥ || 2 ||

daśavaktrā daśabhujā daśapādāñjanaprabhā |
viśālayā rājamānā triṃśallocanamālayā || 3 ||

sphuraddaśanadaṃṣṭrā sā bhīmarūpāpibhūmipa |
rūpasaubhāgyakāntīnāṃ sā pratiṣṭhā mahāśriyaḥ || 4 ||

khaḍgabāṇagadāśūlaṃśaṃkhacakrabhuśuṇḍibhṛt |
parighaṃ kārmukaṃśīrṣaṃ niścyotadrudhirandadhau || 5 ||

eṣā sā vaiṣṇavī māyā mahākālī duratyayā |
ārādhitāvaśī kuryātpūjākartuścarācaram || 6 ||

sarvadevaśarīrebhyo yā'virbhūtā'mitaprabhā |
triguṇā sā mahālakṣmīḥ sākṣānmahiṣamardinī || 7 ||

śvetānanā nīlabhujā suśvetastanamaṇḍalā |
raktamadhyā raktapādā nīlajaṃghorurunmadā || 8 ||

p. 391)

sucitrajaghanā citramālyāmbaravibhūṣaṇā |
citrānulepanā kāntirūpasaubhāgyaśālinī || 9 ||

aṣṭādaśabhujā pūjyā sā sahasrabhujā satī |
āyudhānyatra vakṣyante dakṣiṇādhaḥ karakramāt || 10 ||

akṣamālā ca kamalaṃ bāṇoṣiḥ kuliśaṃ gadā |
cakraṃ triśūlaṃparaśuḥ śaṅkho ghaṇṭā ca pāśakaḥ || 11 ||

śaktirdaṇḍaścarma cāpaṃ pānapātraṃ kamaṇḍaluḥ |
alaṃkṛtabhujāmebhirāyudhaiḥ kamalāsanām || 12 ||

sarvadevamayīmīśāṃ mahālakṣmīmimāṃ nṛpa |
pūjayetsarvalokānāṃ sa devānāṃ prabhurbhavet || 13 ||

p. 392)

gaurādehātsamudbhūtā yā sattvaikaguṇāśrayā |
sākṣātsarasvatī proktā śumbhāsuranibarhiṇī || 14 ||

dadhaucāṣṭabhujābāṇamusalaṃśūlacakrabhṛt |
śaṅkhaṃ ghaṇṭāṃ lāṅgalaṃ ca kārmukaṃ vasudhādhipa || 15 ||

eṣā saṃpūjitā bhaktyā sarvajñatvaṃ prayacchati |
niśumbhamathinī devī śumbhāsuranibarhiṇī || 16 ||

ityuktāni svarūpāṇi mūrtīnāṃ tava pārthiva |
upāsanaṃ jaganmātuḥ pṛthagāsāṃ niśāmaya || 17 ||

mahālakṣmīryadā pūjyā mahākālī sarasvatī |
dakṣiṇottarayoḥ pūjye pṛṣṭhatomithunatrayam || 18 ||

viriñciḥ svarayā madhye rudro gauryā ca dakṣiṇe |
vāmelakṣmyāhṛṣīkeśaḥ puratodevatātrayam || 19 ||

p. 393)

aṣṭādaśabhujāmadhye vāme cāsyā daśānanā |
dakṣiṇe'ṣṭabhujā lakṣmīrmahatītisamarcayet || 20 ||

pūrvādidalataḥ pūjyā asitāṅgādibhairavāḥ |
aṣṭādaśabhujā caiṣā yadā pūjyā narādhipa || 21 ||

daśānanā cāṣṭabhujā dakṣiṇottarayostadā |
daśānanā yadā pūjyā dakṣiṇottarayostadā || 22 ||

kālamṛtyū ca saṃpūjyau sarvāriṣṭapraśāntaye |
yadā cāṣṭabhujā pūjyā śumbhāsuranibarhiṇī || 23 ||

p. 394)

navāsyāḥ śaktayaḥ pūjyāstadā rudravināyakau |
namodevyā iti stotrairmahālakṣmīṃ samarcayet || 24 ||

avatāratrayārcāyāṃ stotramantrāstadāśrayāḥ |
aṣṭādaśabhujā caiṣā pūjyā mahiṣamardinī || 25 ||

mahālakṣmīrmahākālī saiva proktā sarasvatī |
īśvarī puṇyapāpānāṃ sarvalokamaheśvarī || 26 ||

mahiṣāntakarī yena pūjitā sa jagatprabhuḥ |
pūjayejjagatāṃ dhātrīṃ caṇḍikāṃ bhaktavatsalām || 27 ||

arghyādibhiralaṃkārairgandhapuṣpaistathākṣataiḥ |
dhūpairdīpaiśca naivedyairnānābhakṣyasamanvitaiḥ || 28 ||

rudhirāktena balinā māṃsena surayā nṛpa |

p. 395)

balimāṃsādipūjeyaṃ vipravarjyā mayeritā || 29 ||

teṣāṃ kila surāmāṃsairnoktā pūjā nṛpa kvacit |
praṇāmācamanīyaiśca candanenasugandhinā || 30 ||

sakarpūraiśca tāmbūlairbhaktibhāvasamanvitaiḥ |
vāmabhāge'grato devyāśchinnaśīrṣaṃ mahāsuram || 31 ||

pūjayenmahiṣaṃ yena prāptaṃ sāyujyamīśayā |
dakṣiṇe purataḥ siṃhaṃ samagraṃ dharmamīśvaram || 32 ||

vāhanaṃ pūjayeddevyādhṛtaṃ yenacarācaram ||

atha kuñjikā stotram ||

īśvara uvāca ||

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi kuñjikā maṃtra (stotra) muttamam |
yena mantra prabhāveṇa caṇḍī japasusiddhidam || 1 ||

na kavacaṃ nārgalāstotraṃ kīlakaṃ narahasyakam |
na sūktaṃ nāpi dhyānaṃ ca na nyāsaṃ nāpicārcanaṃ || 2 ||

kuñjikā pāṭhamātreṇa durgā pāṭha phalaṃ labhet |
ati guhyataraṃ devi devānāmapidurlabhaṃ || 3 ||

gopanīyaṃ prayatnena sva yonimivapārvati |
māraṇaṃ mohanaṃ vaśyaṃ stambhanoccaṭanādikam || 4 ||

pāṭhamātreṇa sasiddhyetkuṃjikāstotra muttamam |
mantroyam || oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ klīṃ cāmuṇḍāyai vicce || oṃ glauṃ huṁ klīṃ jūṃ saḥ 
jvālaya 2 jvala 2 prajvala 2 aiṃ hrīṃ klīṃ cāmuṇḍāya vicce jvala haṃ saṃ laṃ 
kṣaṃ phaṭ svāhā || iti mantra ||

p. 396)

tataḥ kṛtāñjalirbhūtvā stuvīta caritairimaiḥ || 33 ||

ekena vā madhyamena naikenatarayoriha |
caritārdhaṃ tu na japeñjapañchidramavāpnuyāt || 34 ||

stotramantraiḥ stuvītemāṃ yadi vā jagadambikām |
pradakṣiṇānamaskārānkṛtvā mūrdhni kṛtāñjaliḥ || 35 ||

kṣamāpayejjagaddhātrīṃ muhurmuhuratandritaḥ |
pratiślokaṃ ca juhuyātpāyasaṃtilasarṣipā || 36 ||

juhuyātstotramantrairvā caṇḍikāyaiśubhaṃ haviḥ |

namaste rudra rūpiṇyai namaste madhu mardini |
namaḷh kaiṭabhahāriṇyai namaste mahiṣārdini || 1 ||

namaste śumbha haṃtryai ca niśumbhāsura vighātinī || 2 ||

jāgrataṃ hi mahādevi japasiddhiṃ kuruṣva me |
aiṃkārī sṛṣṭi rūpāya hrīṃkārī pratipālikā || 3 ||

klīṃkārī kāmarūpiṇyai bīja rūpe namostu te |
cāmuṇḍā caṇḍa ghātī ca yaikārī varadāyinī || 4 ||

vicce cā bhayadāyi ca namaste mantra rūpiṇī || 5 ||

dhāṃ dhīṃ dhūṃ dhūrjaṭeḥ patnī vāṃ vīṃ vūṁ vāgīśvarī tathā || krāṃ krīṃ 
krūṃ kraiṃkālikādevi śāṃ śīṃ śūṃ me śubhaṃ kuru || 6 ||

p. 397)

bhūyo nāmapadairdevī pūjayetsusamāhitaḥ || 37 ||

prayataḥ prāñjaliḥ prahvaḥprāṇānāropyacātmani |
suciraṃ bhāvayeddevīṃ caṇḍikāṃ tanmayo bhavet || 38 ||

evaṃ yaḥ pūjayedbhaktyā pratyahaṃ parameśvarīm |
bhuktvābhogānyathākāmaṃ devīsāyujyamāpnuyāt || 39 ||

yo na pūjayate nityaṃ caṇḍikāṃ bhaktavatsalām |
bhasmīkṛtyāsya puṇyāna nirdahetparameśvarā || 40 ||

tasmātpūjaya bhūpāla sarvalokamaheśvarīm |
yathoktena vidhānena caṇḍikāṃ sukhamāpsyasi oṃ || 41 ||

iti vaikṛti rahasyaṃ sampūrṇam ||

huṁ huṁ huṁkāra rūpiṇyai jaṃ jaṃ jaṃ jaṃbhanādinī || bhrāṃ bhrīṃ bhrūṃ 
bhairavī bhadre bhavānyai te namonamaḥ || 7 || aṃ kaṃ caṃ ṭaṃ taṃ paṃ yaṃ śaṃ 
vīṃ duṃ aiṃ vīṃ haṃ kṣaṃ dhijāgraṃ 2 troṭaya 2 dīptaṃ kuru 2 svāhā || pāṃ 
pīṃ pūṃ pārvati pūrṇā khāṃ khīṃ khūṃ khecarī tathā || 8 || 

sāṃ sīṃ sūṃ saptaśatī siddhiṃ kuru svajanamātrataḥ |
idaṃ tu kuṃjikāstotraṃ maṃtraṃ jāgarti hetave ||

bhaktenaiva tu dātavyaṃ gopite rakṣapārvati |
yastu kuñjikayā devi hīnaṃsaptaśatīṃ paṭhet ||

na tasya jāyatesiddhiḥ araṇyerodanaṃ yathā ||

rudrayāmale kuñjikā stotram ||

p. 398)

atha mūrtirahasya prārambhaḥ ||

ṛṣiruvāca ||

oṃ nandā bhagavatī nāma yā bhaviṣyati nandajā |
stutā sampūjitā bhaktyā vaśīkuryājjagattrayam || 1 ||

kanakottamakrāntiḥ sā sukāntikanakāmbarā |
devī kanakavarṇābhā kanakottamabhūṣaṇā || 2 ||

kamalāṅkuśapāśābjairalaṅkṛtacaturbhujā |
indirākamalā lakṣmīḥ sā śrī rukmāmbujāsanā || 3 ||

yāraktadantikā nāma devī proktā mayā'nagha |

p. 399)

tasyāḥ svarūpaṃ vakṣyāmi śṛṇu sarvabhayāpaham || 4 ||

yacchrutvā sarvapāpebhyomucyatenātrasaṃśayaḥ |
raktāmbarāraktavarṇā raktasarvāṅgabhūṣaṇā || 5 ||

raktāyudhāraktanetrāraktakeśātibhīṣaṇā |
raktatīkṣṇa nakhārakta rasanā raktadantikā || 6 ||

patiṃ nārīvānuraktādevībhaktaṃbhajejjanam |
vasudhevaviśālā sā sumeru yugalastanī || 7 ||

dīrgho lambāvatisthūlau tāvatīvamanoharau |
karkaśāvatikāntau tau sarvānandapayonidhī || 8 ||

bhaktānsampāyayeddevī sarvakāmadughau stanau |
khaḍga pātraśiraḥ kheṭairalaṃkṛtacaturbhujā || 9 ||

ākhyātā raktacāmuṇḍā devī yogeśvarīti ca |
anayā vyāptamakhilaṃ jagatsthāvarajaṅgamam || 10 ||

imāṃ yaḥ pūjayedbhaktyā sa vyāpnoti carācaram |
bhuktvā bhogānyathākāmaṃ devīsāyujyamapnuyāt || 11 ||

adhīte ya imaṃ nityaṃ raktadantyā vapuḥstavam |
taṃ sā paricareddevī patiṃ priyāmavāṃganā || 12 ||

śākambharī nīlavarṇā nīlotpalavilocanā |
gambhīra nābhistrivalīvibhūṣitatanūdarī || 13 ||

sukarkaśasamottuṅgavṛttapīnaghanastanī |
muṣṭiṃ śilīmukhāpūrṇaṃ kamalaṃ kamalālayā || 14 ||

puṣpapallavamūlādiphalāḍhyaṃ śākasañcayam |
kāmyānantarasairyuktaṃkṣuttṛṇmṛtyujarāpaham || 15 ||

p. 401)

kārmukaṃ ca sphuratkāntiṃ bibharti parameśvarī |
śākambharī śatākṣī sā saiva durgā prakīrtitā || 16 ||

umā gaurī satī caṇḍī kālikā sā ca pārvatī |
śākambharīṃ stuvandhyāyañjapansampūjayannaman || 17 ||

akṣayyamaśnute śīghramannāpānāmṛtaṃ jalam |
bhīmāpinīlavarṇāsādaṃṣṭrādaśanabhāsurā || 18 ||

viśālalocanā nārī vṛttapīnapayodharā |
candrahāsaṃ ca ḍamaruṃ śiraḥ pātraṃ ca bibhratī || 19 ||

ekavīrā kālarātriḥ saivoktā kāmadā studā |
tejomaṇḍaladudharṣā bhrāmarīcitrakāntibhṛt || 20 ||

citrabhramarasaṅkāśā mahāmārīti gīyate |
ityetā mūrtayo devyā vyakhyātā vasudhādhipa || 21 ||

p. 402)

jaganmātuścaṇḍikāyāḥ kīrtitāḥ kāmadhenavaḥ |
idaṃ rahasyaṃ paramaṃ na vācyaṃ kasyacittvayā || 22 ||

yākhyānandivyamūrtīnāmadhīṣvāvahitaḥ svayam |
etasyāstvaṃ prasādena sarvamānyo bhaviṣyasi || 23 ||

sarvarūpamayā devā sarvaṃ devīmayaṃ jagat |
ato'haṃ viśvarūpāṃ tvāṃ namāmi parameśvarīm || 24 ||

iti mūrtirahasyaṃ sampūrṇam ||

anugrahenava ślokāḥ

oṃ viśveśvarīṃ jagaddhātrīṃ sthitisaṃhārakāriṇīṃ |
karotu sā naḥ śubhaheturīśvarī śubhāni bhadrāṇyahaṃtu cāpadaḥ || 1 ||

oṃ stutāsuraiḥ pūrvamabhīṣṭa saṃśrayāttahā surendreṇa dineṣu sevitā |
karotu sā naḥ śubhaheturīśvarī śubhānī bhadrāṇyabhihaṃtu cāpadaḥ || 2 ||

oṃ yā sāṃprataṃ coddhata daityanāpitairasmābhirīśā ca surairnamasyate |
karotu sā naḥ śubhaheturīśvarī śubhāni bhadrāṇyabhi haṃtu cāpadaḥ || 3 ||

p. 403)

oṃ yā ca smṛtātatkṣaṇameva haṃti naḥ sarvāpadobhaktivinamra mūrtibhiḥ 
|
karotu sā naḥ śubhaheturīśvarī śubhāni bhadrāṇyabhihaṃtu cāpadaḥ || 4 ||

oṃ sarvābādhāpraśamanaṃ trailokyasyākhileśvari [atra vairi 
nāśanamityatraroganāśanamityādyūhaḥ | evaṃ daityatāpitairityapi ||] ||

karotu sā naḥ śubhaheturīśvarī śubhāni bhadrāṇyabhihaṃtu cāpadaḥ || 5 ||

oṃ sarvamaṅgalamaṃgalye śive sarvārthasādhike |
karotu sā naḥ śubhaheturīśvarī śubhāni bhadrāṇyabhihaṃtu cāpadaḥ || 6 ||

oṃ sṛṣṭi sthiti vināśānāṃ śakti bhūtesanātani |
karotu sā naḥ śubhaheturīśvarī śubhāni bhadrāṇyabhihaṃtu cāpadaḥ || 7 ||

oṃ śaraṇāgatadīnārta paritrāṇaparāyaṇe |
karotu sā naḥ śubhaheturīśvarī śubhāni bhadrāṇyabhihaṃtu cāpadaḥ || 8 ||

oṃ sarvasvarūpe sarveśesarvaśakti samanvite |
karotu sā naḥ śubhaheturīśvarī śubhāni bhadrāṇyabhihaṃtu cāpadaḥ || 9 ||

ete'nugrahe navaślokāḥ vaśye tu śeṣaḥ || oṃ hrīṃ raktacāmuṇḍe 
tūrṇamamukaṃ me vaśamānaya svāhā || anenapratyadhyāyamādyaṃtayoḥ 
pūjā sarvāṃte ayutamaṃtraśca (daśa sāhasram) japaḥ || 
homayetkuṭutailena rakta andana rājikāḥ sahasrāhuti mātreṇa rājānaṃ vaśa 
mānayet || madhunā cāśokapuṣpaiḥ rātrauhutvātu pūrvavat cakravartī 
bhavedvaśyaścaṇḍī maṃtra prabhāvataḥ ||

atha sarasvatī kavaca prārambhaḥ ||

brahmovāca ||

śṛṇu vatsa pravakṣyāmi kavacaṃ sarva kāmadam |
śrutisāraṃ śrutimukhaṃśrutyuktaṃ śrutipūjitam || 1 ||

uktaṃ kṛṣṇena goloke mahyaṃ vṛndāvane vane |
rāseśvareṇa vibhunā rāsane rāsamaṇḍale || 2 ||

atīvagopanīyaṃ ca kalpavṛkṣasamaṃ param |
aśrutādbhutamantrāṇāṃ samūhaiśca samanvitam || 3 ||

yaddhṛtvā paṭhanādbrahman buddhimāṁśca bṛhaspati |

p. 404)

yaddhṛtvā bhagavāñchukraḥ sarvadaityeṣu pūjitaḥ || 4 ||

paṭhanāddhāraṇādvāgmī kavīndro vālmiko muniḥ || 5 ||

svāyambhuvo manuścaiva yaddhṛtvā sarvapūjitaḥ |
kaṇādo gautamaḥ kaṇvaḥ pāṇiniḥ śākaṭāyanaḥ || 6 ||

graṃthaṃ cakāra yaddhṛtvā dakṣaḥ kātyāyanaḥ svayam |
dhṛtvā vedavibhāgaṃ ca purāṇānyakhilāni ca || 7 ||

cakāra līlāmātreṇakṛṣṇadvaipāyanaḥ svayam |
śātātapaśca saṃvarto vasiṣṭhaśca parāśaraḥ || 8 ||

pūjanam ||

saṃkalpa || tithi vārādyuccārya || yātrāyāṃ vijaya siddhyarthaṃ vāstu pūjana

parihṛtya maunaṃ |
kasmādimau prakaṭitau kila kālarūpau yadvā bhavāni hasituṃ nu kimicchase 
māṃ || 116 ||

jñātaṃ mayā tava viceṣṭamadbhutaṃ vai kṛtvākhilaṃ jagadidaṃ ramase 
svataṃtrā |
līnaṃ karoṣi sakalaṃ kila māṃ tathaiva hantuṃ svamicchasi bhavāni kimatra 
citraṃ || 117 ||

kāmaṃ kuruṣva vadhamadya mamaiva mātarduḥkhaṃ na me maraṇajaṃ 
jagadambiketra |
kartā tvameva vihitaḥ prathamaṃ sa cāyaṃ daityāhatotha mṛta ityayaśo 
gariṣṭhaṃ || 118 ||

uttiṣṭha devi kuru rūpamivādbhutaṃ tvaṃ māṃ vā tvimau jahi yathecchasi 
bālalīle |
nocetprabodhaya hariṃ nihanedimo yastvatsādhyametadakhilaṃ kila kārya 
jātaṃ || 119 ||

ṛṣiruvāca ||

niḥsṛtya haridehāttu saṃsthitā pārśvatastadā |

nirgatā yoganidrā sā nāśāya ca tayostadā |
vispaṃdita śarīrosau yadā jāto janārdana || 122 ||

p. 441)

dhātā paramikāṃ prāpto mudaṃ dṛṣṭvā hariṃ tataḥ |
yadā vinargatā nidrā dehāttasya jagadguroḥ || 123 ||

uttasthau ca0 ..... ..... ..... |
tadā paśyat sthitaṃ tatra bhayatrastaṃ prajāpatiṃ || 125 ||

uvāca ca mahātejā megha gambhīrayā girā |

viṣnuruvāca ||

kimāgatosi bhagavaṃstapastyaktvātra padmaja || 126 ||

kasmāccitāturosi tvaṃ bhayākulita mānasaḥ |

brahmovāca ||

hantuṃ māṃ samupāyātau ghorarūpau mahāvalau |

tiṣṭhādya nirbhayo jātastauhaniṣyāmyahaṃ kila || 128 ||

yuddhātā'jagmaturumūḍhau matsamīpaṃ gatāyuṣau |
ekārṇave hi0 ..... ..... ..... ..... || 129 ||

bhavetāmadya0 ..... ..... ..... ..... |

vicārya manasā tau tu dānavau viṣṇumūcatuḥ || 138 ||

prekṣya sarvaṃ jalamayaṃ bhūmisthala vivarjitaṃ |
nirjale vipule deśe hanasva madhusūdana || 139 ||

āvāṃ jahi0 ..... ..... ..... ..... |

hanmyadya vāṃ mahābhāgau nirjalaṃ vipule sthale || 141 ||

p. 442)

ityuktvā devadeveśa urū kṛtvāti vistarau |
darśayāmāsa tau tatra nirjalaṃ ca jalopari || 142 ||

bhagavān dviguṇaṃ cakre jaghanaṃ vismitau tadā |
śīrṣe saṃdadhatāṃ tatra jaghane paramādbhute || 143 ||

tathettyuktvā0 ..... ..... ..... || 144 ||

eva meṣā samutpunnā0 ..... ..... ..... |

iti prathamodhyāyaḥ || 1 ||

atha dvitīyodhyāyaḥ ||

madhyama caritaḥ || devā ūcuḥ ||

deva deva jagannātha sṛṣṭisthityaṃta kāraka |
dadyāsiṃdho mahārāja trāhi naḥ śaraṇāgatān || 4 ||

mahiṣeṇa mahārāja pīḍitāḥ pāpakarmaṇā |
asādhyenāti duṣṭena vara dṛptena pāpinā || 5 ||

yajñabhāgānasau bhuṃkte brāhmaṇaiḥ pratipāditān |
amarā giridurgeṣu bhramaṃti ca bhayāturāḥ || 6 ||

ṛṣiruvāca ||

śrutvā tadvacanaṃ viṣṇustānuvāca hasanniva |
yuddhaṃ kṛtaṃ purāsmābhistathāpi na mṛto hyasau || 11 ||

adya sarva surāṇāṃ vai tejobhī rūpasampadā |
utpannā cedvarārohā sāhanyāttaṃ raṇe balāt || 12 ||

hayāriṃ vara dṛptaṃca māyāgata viśāradaṃ |
hantu yogyā bhavennārī śaktyaṃśanirmitāhi naḥ || 13 ||

prāthayantu ca tejoṃśān striyosmākaṃ tathā punaḥ |
utpannastaiśca tejo'śaistejo rāśirbhavedyathā || 14 ||

āyudhāni vayaṃ dadmaḥ sarve rudrapurogamāḥ |
tasyai sarvāṇi divyāni triśūlādīni yāni ca || 15 ||

p. 443)

sarvāyudha dharā nārī sarvatejaḥ samanvitā |
haniṣyati durātmānaṃ taṃ pāpaṃ madgarvitaṃ || 16 ||

itthaṃ niśamya0 ..... ..... ..... |
cakārakopaṃ0 ..... ..... ..... || 17 ||

rakta varṇaṃ śubhākāraṃ padmarāga maṇiprabhaṃ |
kiñjicchītaṃ tathā coṣṇaṃ marīci jāla maṇḍitaṃ || 21 ||

niḥsṛtaṃ hariṇā dṛṣṭaṃ hareṇa ca mahātmanā |
vismitau tau mahārāja babhūvatururukramau || 22 ||

śaṃkarasya śarīrāttu niḥsṛtaṃmahadadbhutaṃ |
raupyavarṇamabhūttīvraṃ durdarśaṃ dāruṇaṃ mahat || 23 ||

bhayaṃkaraṃ ca daityānāṃ devānāṃ vismaya pradaṃ |
ghorarūpaṃ giriprakhyaṃ tamoguṇamivāparaṃ || 24 ||

tato viṣṇuśarīrāttu tejo rāśimivāparaṃ |
nīlaṃ satvaguṇopetaṃ prādurāsa mahādyutiḥ || 25 ||

tataścendra śarīrāttu citrarūpaṃ durāsadaṃ |
āvirāsītsusaṃvṛttaṃ tejaḥ sarvaguṇātmakaṃ || 26 ||

kuverayama vahnīnāṃ śarīrebhyaḥ samaṃtataḥ |
niścakrāma mahattejo varuṇasya tathaiva ca || 27 ||

anyeṣāṃ caiva devānāṃ śarīrebhyotibhāsvāṃ |
nirgataṃ tanmahotejo rāśirāsīnmahojjvalaḥ || 28 ||

taṃ dṛṣṭvā vismitāḥ sarve devā viṣṇu purogamāḥ |
tejorāśiṃ mahādivyaṃ himācalamivāparaṃ || 29 ||

paśyatāṃ tatra devānāṃ tejaḥ puṃjasamudbhavāḥ |
babhūvāti varā nārī sundarī vismayapradā || 30 ||

triguṇā sā mahālakṣmīḥ sarva deva śarīrajā |
aṣṭādaśa bhujā ramyā trivarṇā viśvamohinī || 32 ||

śvetānanākṛṣnānetrā saraktādharapallavā |
tāmrapāṇitalā kāṃtā divya bhūṣaṇa bhūṣitā || 33 ||

aṣṭādaśa bhujā devī sahasrabhujamaṇḍitā |
yadbhūcchāṃbhavaṃ ..... ..... ..... || 34 ||

tāṃ vilokya mudaṃ0 ..... ..... ..... |
svāyudhebhyaḥ samutpāṭya tejo yuktāni sattvarāḥ || 40 ||

p. 444)

tato devā daduntasyai svāni sānyāyudhāni ca |
ūcurjaya jayetyuccairjayantīti jayaiṣiṇaḥ || 41 ||

sāyudhāṃ bhūṣaṇairyuktāṃ dṛṣṭvā te vismayaṃ gatāḥ |
tuṣṭuvurmunayaścaināṃ0 ..... ..... ..... || 56 ||

namaḥ śivāyai kalyāṇyai śāntyai puṣṭyai namonamaḥ |
bhagavatyi namo devyai rudrāṇyai satataṃ namaḥ || 57 ||

kālarātrya tathāmbāyā indrāṇyai te namonamaḥ |
siddhyai buddhyai tathā vṛddhyai vaiṣnavyai te namonamaḥ || 58 ||

pṛthivyāṃyā sthitā pṛthvyāna jñātā pṛthivīti ca |
antaḥ sthitā prerayati vaṃde tāmīśvarīṃ parāṃ || 59 ||

kalyāṇaṃ kuru bho mātastrāhi naḥ śatrutāpitān |
jahi pāpaṃ hayāri tvaṃ tejasā svena mohitaṃ || 60 ||

khalaṃ māyāvina ghora strīvadhyaṃ vara darpitaṃ |
duḥkhadaṃ sarva devānāṃ nānā rūpadharaṃ śaṭhaṃ || 61 ||

tvamekā sarva devānāṃ śaraṇaṃ bhaktavatsale |
pīḍitāndānavenādya trāhi devi namostute || 62 ||

ṛṣiruvāca ||

evaṃ stutā tadā devī suraiḥ sarva sukhapradā |

devyuvāca ||

bhayaṃ tyajaṃtu gīrvāṇā mahiṣānmaṃda cetasaḥ |
haniṣyāmi raṇe'dyaiva vara dṛptaṃ vimohitaṃ || 64 ||

iti kṛtvā smitaṃ devī sāṭṭahāsaṃ cakāraha |
uccaiḥ śabdaṃ mahāghoraṃ dānavānāṃ bhayapradaṃ || 65 ||

cakaṃpe vasudhā tatra śrutvā tacchabda madbhutaṃ |
celuśca parvatāḥ sarve cukṣobhābdhiśca vīryavān || 66 ||

meruścapāla śabdena diśaḥ sarva prapūritāḥ |
bhayaṃ jagmustadā śrutvā dānavāstāṃ svanaṃ mahat || 67 ||

jaya pāhīti devāstāmūcuḥ parama harṣitaḥ |
mahiṣopi svanaṃ śrutvā cukopa madagarvitaḥ || 68 ||

kimetaditi tāndaityānpapraccha svanaśaṃkitaḥ |
gacchaṃtu tvaritā dūtā jñātuṃ śabda samudbhava || 69 ||

p. 445)

kutaḥ ke nāyamatyugraḥ śabdaḥ karṇa vyathākaraḥ |
devo vā dānavo vāpi yo bhavetsvana kārakaḥ || 70 ||

gṛhītvā taṃ durātmānaṃ matsamīpaṃ nayaṃtviha |
ahaṃgatvā haniṣyāmi taṃ pāpaṃ vitatha śramaṃ || 71 ||

ityuktāstena te dūtā devīṃ sarvāṅgasundarīṃ |
aṣṭādaśa bhujāṃ divyāṃ sarvābharaṇa bhūṣitāṃ || 72 ||

sarva lakṣaṇa saṃpannāṃ varāyudhadharāṃ śubhāṃ |
dadhatīṃ caṣakaṃ haste pibaṃtīṃ ca muhurmuhuḥ || 73 ||

saṃvīkṣya bhavabhītāste jagmustrastā suśaṃkitāḥ |
sakāśe mahiṣasyāśu tamūcuḥ svanakāraṇaṃ || 74 ||

dūtā ūcuḥ ||

devī daityeśvara prauḍhā dṛśyate kācidaṃganā |
sarvāṅga bhūṣaṇā nārī sarvaratnopaśobhitā || 75 ||

na mānuṣī nāsurī sā divya rūpā manoharā |

kimarthamāgatā cātra kiṃ cikīrṣati sundarī || 78 ||

draṣṭuṃ naiva samarthāḥ smastattejaḥ paridharpitāḥ |

sāmādibhirupāyaistvaṃ samānaya śubhānanāṃ |

ṛṣiruvāca ||

maṃtrī uvāca ||

sajetā sarvadevānāmavadhyastu naraiḥ kila |

p. 446)

vaśago'sau tavātyarthaṃ rūpasaṃśravaṇāttava |

ṛṣiruvāca ||

devyuvāca ||

ānītaṃ taṃ rathaṃ jñātvā dānavendro mahābalaḥ |

samīpametya devyāstu tāmuvāca hasanniva || 27 ||

devi saṃsāracakre'sminvartamāne janaḥ kila |
naro vātha tathā nārī sukhaṃ vāṃchati sarvathā || 28 ||

nārī puruṣayoḥ kāṃte samāna vayasoḥ sadā |
saṃyogo yaḥ samākhyātaḥ sa evātyuttamaḥ smṛtaḥ || 29 ||

taṃ cetkaroṣi saṃyogaṃ vīreṇa ca mayā saha |
atyuttama sukhasyaiva prāptiḥ syātte na saṃśayaḥ || 30 ||

iṃdrādayaḥ surā sarve saṃgrāme vijitā mayā |
ratnāni yāni divyāni bhavanesminmamādhunā || 31 ||

bhuṃkṣva tvaṃ tāni sarvāṇi yatheṣṭaṃ dehi vā yathā |
paṭṭarājñī bhavādya tvaṃ dāsosmi tava sundari || 32 ||

p. 449)

ṛṣiruvāca ||

iti buvāṇaṃ taṃ daityaṃ devī bhagavatī hi sā |
prahasya sasmitaṃ vākyamuvāca varavarṇinī || 33 ||

devyuvāca ||

nāhaṃ puruṣamicchāmi paramaṃ puruṣaṃ vinā |
tasya cecchāsmyahaṃ daitya sṛjāmi sakalaṃ jagat || 34 ||

sa māṃ paśyati viśvātmā tasyāhaṃ prakṛtiḥ śivā |
mūrkhastvamasi madātmanyatstrīsaṃgaṃ cikīrṣasi || 35 ||

narasya baṃdhanāthāya śṛṅkhalā strī prakīrtitā |
nārīsaṃge mahadduḥkhaṃ jānankiṃ tvaṃ vimuhyasi || 36 ||

tyaja vairaṃ suraiḥ sārdhaṃ yatheṣṭaṃ vicarāvanau |
pātālaṃ gaccha vā kāmaṃ jīvitecchā yadasti te || 37 ||

athavā kuru saṃgrāmaṃ balavatyasmi sāṃprataṃ |
preṣitāhaṃ suraiḥ sarvaistava nāśāya dānava || 38 ||

haniṣyāmi mahābāho tvāmahaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ |

ṛṣiruvāca ||

ityuktaḥ satayā devyā dhanurādāya dānavaḥ || 39 ||

mumoca tarasā bāṇānkarṇākṛṣṭāñchilāśitān |
devī ciccheda tānbāṇaiḥ krodhānmuktairayomukhaiḥ || 40 ||

tayoḥ parasparaṃ yuddhaṃ saṃbabhūva bhayapradaṃ |
sapadātiratha sainyaṃ devyā siṃhena nāśitaṃ || 41 ||

devyuvāca ||

mā garvaṃ kuru maṃdātmaṃstiṣṭha tiṣṭha raṇāṃgaṇe |
kariṣyāmi nirātaṃkānhatvā tvāṃ surasattamān || 58 ||

ṛṣiruvāca ||

ityuktvā caṣakaṃ haimaṃ gṛhītvā surayāyutaṃ |
papau punaḥ punaḥ krodhāddhaṃtukāmā mahāsuraṃ || 60 ||

evaṃ sa mahiṣo nāma sasainyaḥ sasuhṛdgaṇaḥ |
trailokyaṃ mohayitvā tu tayā devyā nipātitaḥ || 65 ||
